# PART 01 — TONE 1: SUNDAY AND WEEKDAY OFFICES (SATURDAY VESPERS THROUGH SATURDAY MATINS)

*[Engraved Headpiece: An ornamental border depicting the Prophet Moses, King David, the Prophet Isaiah, Christ the Good Shepherd, the Most Pure Virgin Theotokos with the Christ Child, and Saint John Chrysostom]*[^15]

**THE BEGINNING, WITH GOD MOST HOLY, OF THE OCTOECHOS, THAT IS, THE BOOK OF EIGHT TONES.**

*On Saturday evening, at Little Vespers:*[^16]

*On "Lord, I have cried," we set 4 verses, and we sing 3 Resurrectional stichera, repeating the sticheron of the first verse.*[^17]

*Tone 1:*

*(The work of our venerable father John Damascene)*[^18]

*Resurrectional Sticheron 1:* Accept our evening prayers, O holy Lord, and grant us remission of sins; for Thou alone art He Who hath manifested the Resurrection unto the world.

*Resurrectional Sticheron 2:* Encompass Sion, ye peoples, and embrace her, and give glory within her unto Him Who is risen from the dead; for He is our God, Who hath delivered us from our iniquities.[^19]

*Resurrectional Sticheron 3:* Come, ye peoples, let us sing and worship Christ, glorifying His Resurrection from the dead; for He is our God, Who hath delivered the world from the deceit of the enemy.

*Glory... Both now...*

*Theotokion Dogmatikon, in the same Tone:*

*Theotokion Dogmatikon (continued):* Today is a festival of virginity, brethren! Let creation leap for joy, let mankind dance; for the holy Theotokos hath summoned us, the undefiled treasury of virginity, the spiritual paradise of the Second Adam, the storehouse of the union of the two natures, the festival of the saving reconciliation, the bridal chamber wherein the Word truly espoused the flesh, the light cloud that bore in the body Him Who is above the Cherubim. By her prayers, O Christ God, save our souls.

*Then: "O Joyful Light." Prokimenon: "The Lord is King," with its verses [LXX Ps. 92:1];*[^20] *and after "Vouchsafe, O Lord," the priest says no litany, but we sing the Resurrectional sticheron at the Aposticha, in Tone 1:*

*Resurrectional Aposticha Sticheron:* By Thy Passion, O Christ, we have been set free from passions, and by Thy Resurrection we have been delivered from corruption; O Lord, glory to Thee.

*Other Stichera to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Tone 1, Podoben: "Joy of the heavenly ranks" (Небе́сныхъ чинѡ́въ):*

*Verse: I will remember thy name in every generation and generation [LXX Ps. 44:18].*[^21]

*Sticheron 1:* Preeminently glorified art thou throughout generations of generations, O Virgin Maiden Mother, Theotokos Mary, protectress of the world, having in the flesh given birth to the Son of the unoriginate Father, truly co-eternal with the Spirit; entreat Him that we may be saved.

*Verse: Give ear, O daughter, and behold, and incline thine ear [LXX Ps. 44:11].*[^22]

*Sticheron 2:* Held fast by unexpected afflictions, O pure one, having thee as our sole intercessor, O Virgin, we cry aloud in thanksgiving: Save us, O all-holy Bride of God; for thou art the refuge of the world, and the protection of our race.

*Verse: The rich among the people shall pray before thy face [LXX Ps. 44:13].*[^23]

*Sticheron 3:* The world was renewed at thy childbirth, O maiden Theotokos, salvation of the faithful and unslumbering intercessor for those who piously pray to thee, O all-pure one! Cease not fervently praying for all who sing thy praise.

*Glory... Both now...*

*Dogmatikon, in the same Tone:*

*Theotokion Dogmatikon:* A cloud of the co-eternal Light did the Prophet call thee, O Virgin; for descending from thee like rain upon the fleece [LXX Ps. 71:6],[^24] the Father's Word, and having shone forth from thee, enlightened the world and destroyed deceit, Christ our God. Praying fervently unto Him, O most holy one, we pray: Cease not to intercede for us who confess thee to be the true Theotokos.

*Then: "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart." Trisagion Prayers. After "Our Father:" The Resurrectional troparion (see fol. 5);*[^25] *Glory... Both now... its Theotokion. The litany: "Have mercy on us, O God," and the Dismissal.*

*On Saturday at Great Vespers:*[^26]

*After the Initial Psalm, the usual kathisma of the Psalter.*[^27]

*On "Lord, I have cried," we set 10 verses, and we sing 3 Resurrectional stichera, 4 Eastern stichera, and 3 or 6 from the Menaion. If a celebrated saint occurs, Glory... from the Menaion; Both now... Theotokion 1 of the Tone.*[^28]

*Resurrectional Stichera of the Octoechos, Tone 1:*

*Resurrectional Sticheron 1:* Accept our evening prayers, O holy Lord, and grant us remission of sins, for Thou alone art He Who hath manifested the Resurrection unto the world.

*Resurrectional Sticheron 2:* Encompass Sion, ye peoples, and embrace her, and give glory within her unto Him Who is risen from the dead; for He is our God, Who hath delivered us from our iniquities.

*Resurrectional Sticheron 3:* Come, ye peoples, let us sing and worship Christ, glorifying His Resurrection from the dead; for He is our God, Who hath delivered the world from the deceit of the enemy.

*Other Eastern Stichera, Tone 1:*[^29]

*Eastern Sticheron 1:* Rejoice, ye heavens; trumpet forth, ye foundations of the earth; cry out in rejoicing, ye mountains [LXX Isa. 44:23, 49:13]![^30] For behold, Emmanuel hath nailed our sins to the Cross, and giving life, He hath slain death, having raised up Adam, as the Lover of mankind.

*Eastern Sticheron 2:* Let us sing unto Him Who was willingly crucified in the flesh for our sake, Who suffered, and was buried, and rose from the dead, saying: Establish Thy Church in Orthodoxy, O Christ, and bring peace unto our life, as Thou art good and the Lover of mankind.

*Eastern Sticheron 3:* Standing before Thy life-receiving tomb, we unworthy ones offer praise unto Thine ineffable lovingkindness, O Christ our God; for Thou, the Sinless One, didst accept the Cross and death, that Thou mightest grant resurrection unto the world, as the Lover of mankind.

*Eastern Sticheron 4:* Let us praise the Word, co-unoriginate and co-eternal with the Father, Who came forth from the virginal *Eastern Sticheron 4 (continued):* womb ineffably, and willingly accepted the Cross and death for our sake, and rose in glory, saying: O Giver of life, Lord, glory to Thee, Savior of our souls.

*Glory... from the Menaion; Both now... Theotokion:*

*Theotokion Dogmatikon:* Let us praise the Virgin Mary, the glory of all the world, who blossomed forth from men and gave birth to the Master, the heavenly gate, the hymn of the bodiless powers, and the beauty of the faithful; for she was revealed as heaven and the temple of the Godhead; she destroyed the barrier of enmity, brought in peace, and opened the kingdom. Having her, therefore, as the anchor of faith, we have as our champion the Lord Who was born of her. Be of good courage therefore, be of good courage, O people of God; for He shall defeat the enemies, as He is all-powerful.

*The Entrance. "O Joyful Light." After the Entrance, the daily Prokimenon:*

*Tone 6: The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty [LXX Ps. 92:1].*[^31]

*Verse 1: The Lord is robed in strength, and He hath girded Himself [LXX Ps. 92:1].*[^32]

*Verse 2: For He hath established the world, so that it shall not be moved [LXX Ps. 92:1].*[^33]

*Verse 3: Holiness becomes Thy house, O Lord, unto length of days [LXX Ps. 92:5].*[^34]

*Then the usual litany. "Vouchsafe, O Lord." "Let us complete our evening prayer," and the rest.*

*At the Aposticha:*

*Resurrectional Sticheron, Tone 1:*

*Resurrectional Sticheron:* By Thy Passion, O Christ, we have been set free from passions, and by Thy Resurrection we have been delivered from corruption. O Lord, glory to Thee.

*Other Stichera in Alphabetical Order.*[^35] *Verse: The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty [LXX Ps. 92:1].*

*Alphabetical Sticheron 1:* Let creation rejoice, let the heavens be glad, let the nations clap their hands with gladness; for Christ our Savior hath nailed our sins to the Cross, and having slain death, hath bestowed life upon us, having raised up all-father Adam, as the Lover of mankind.

*Verse: For He hath established the world, so that it shall not be moved [LXX Ps. 92:1].*

*Alphabetical Sticheron 2:* Being King of heaven and earth, O Incomprehensible One, Thou wast willingly crucified in Thy love for mankind. When Hades met Thee below, it was embittered, and receiving the souls of the righteous, they rejoiced; and Adam, seeing Thee, the Creator, in the nethermost regions, arose. O marvel! How did the Life of all taste death? But as He willed, He enlightened the world that cries aloud and saith: Risen from the dead, O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Verse: Holiness becomes Thy house, O Lord, unto length of days [LXX Ps. 92:5].*

*Alphabetical Sticheron 3:* Bearing myrrh, the myrrh-bearing women with haste and lamentation reached Thy tomb; and not finding Thy most pure body, yet learning from the Angel of the new and glorious miracle, they said unto the Apostles: The Lord is risen, granting great mercy unto the world.

*Glory... from the Menaion, if there is one; but if not, Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

*Theotokion:* Behold, the prophecy of Isaiah is fulfilled [Isa. 7:14];[^36] for as a Virgin thou didst give birth, and after childbirth thou didst remain even as before childbirth; for He Who was born was God, and therefore He re-fashioned the nature of things. But O Mother of God, despise not the supplications of thy servants, which are offered unto thee in thy temple; but as thou bearest the Compassionate One in thine arms, have pity upon thy servants, and pray that our souls may be saved.

*Then: "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart." Trisagion Prayers. After "Our Father:"*

*Resurrectional Troparion, Tone 1:*

*Resurrectional Troparion:* While the stone was sealed by the Jews and the soldiers were guarding Thy most pure body, Thou didst arise on the third day, O Savior, granting life unto the world; therefore the heavenly powers cried aloud unto Thee, O Giver of life: Glory to Thy Resurrection, O Christ; glory to Thy kingdom; glory to Thy dispensation, O Thou Who alone art the Lover of mankind!

*Theotokion:*

*Theotokion:* When Gabriel proclaimed unto thee, "Hail!" O Virgin, at his voice the Master of all became incarnate in thee, the holy Ark, even as righteous David said [LXX Ps. 131:8].[^37] Thou wast revealed as more spacious than the heavens, having borne Thy Creator. Glory to Him Who made His abode within thee; glory to Him Who came forth from thee; glory to Him Who hath set us free through thy childbirth!

*On Saturday at Compline:*[^38]

*The priest says:*

**Priest:** *Blessed is our God...*

*And we:* Amen. *Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee. O Heavenly King...* Trisagion Prayers. And after *Our Father:* *Lord, have mercy (12 times).* *Glory... Both now...* *Come, let us worship (3 times).* Psalm 50: *Have mercy on me, O God [LXX Ps. 50];*[^39] Psalm 69: *O God, be attentive unto helping me [LXX Ps. 69];*[^40] Psalm 142: *Lord, hear my prayer [LXX Ps. 142].*[^41] *After this: Glory to God in the highest. Lord, Thou hast been our refuge. Vouchsafe, O Lord, this night. Then: I believe in one God [the Nicene-Constantinopolitan Creed].*[^42] *After this, we sing:*

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos:*[^43]

*Ode 1, Tone 1. Heirmos:* Israel, delivered from bitter bondage, crossed the impassable sea as dry land. Beholding the enemy drowned, it sang a hymn unto God as Benefactor, Who performs wonders with an outstretched arm; for He hath been glorified.

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* The angelic ranks were struck with awe in fear, O Sovereign Lady, praising thee; and every intellect sings of thee as Mother of the Creator because of goodness; for having given birth to Christ, thou didst surpass every rank of praise.

*Troparion 2:* Tormented by grievous tribulations and harassed by foes, wretch that I am, I call out with tears: Stretch forth thy hand unto me from on high, O all-abundant one, delivering me, and vouchsafe that I may live without peril through thy prayers. Glory...

*Troparion 3:* Heal the secret transgressions of my soul with the medicine of mercy, and calm the surging of the flesh, O Theotokos! And turning back the spears and arrows of the enemies, pierce them firmly into their own hearts. Both now...

*Troparion 4:* The virginal womb consumed the ancient, murderous pasture by giving birth to Christ; therefore all creation now rejoices, O all-pure one, as having been brought to life, and in unison sings praises unto thy Son and God.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Let not the mortal boast in his own wisdom and riches [LXX 1 Reg. 2:10; Jer. 9:23],[^44] but in the faith of the Lord, orthodoxly crying unto Christ God and singing ever: Establish me upon the rock of Thy commandments, O Master.

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* Sleeping once on his journey, great Jacob marveled as he beheld the angels descending from on high unto the earth upon a ladder, O Virgin [Gen. 28:12];[^45] and awakening, he clearly depicted thee as the gate of heaven.

*Troparion 2:* Cast into tribulations by a temporary restraint, wretch that I am, and tormented by tempestuous perils, woe is me, I cry: O thou who didst give birth to God and hast exalted our horn, save me by thy prayers. Glory...

*Troparion 3:* Stretching forth a mighty hand from heaven, O Christ, King over all visible and intelligible things, my Jesus, put the heads of the enemies under the feet of those who faithfully proclaim the Theotokos to be Thy Mother. Both now...

*Troparion 4:* Having of old been purified by the coal of the Spirit, Isaiah clearly cried out that the Son was to be born of thy womb, O all-abundant Virgin, *Troparion 4 (continued):* to be born; Whom in the latter times, for my sake, without a husband thou didst bear.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Of old Habakkuk heard Thy marvelous report, O Christ, and cried aloud with fear: God shall come from Theman, and the Holy One from the dense overshadowed mountain, to save His anointed [LXX Hab. 3:2-3, 13].[^46] Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* Forefiguring thee as the one who among women is fair and all-honorable, coming up as from the wilderness [Cant. 3:6, 8:5][^47] and bearing the Branch Christ on thine arm [Isa. 11:1],[^48] O Mother of God, the prince of myrrh cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Troparion 2:* Incline Thine ear unto me, O good one, and behold my affliction and the multiplication of perils; for having stretched the soul's eye toward thee, O Lady, and bowing my knees with tears, I now entreat thee, crying aloud: Put an end unto the turmoil of my tribulations. Glory...

*Troparion 3:* Knowing thee to be an invincible wall, drawn by supplication, I thy servant now flee unto thee; and I account the arrows of the foes as infantile and ineffective, O all-abundant one! Therefore, rejoicing, I cry aloud: Glory to thy childbirth, O Mother of God!

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* The power of the Most High overshadowed thee, O Virgin, by the descent of the Divine Spirit [Luke 1:35];[^49] and then beyond nature, the Lord of all things, having given life to flesh and soul, united it unto Himself, remaining in the same nature.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Shine forth Thy never-setting light, O Christ, into the hearts of those who faithfully sing of Thee, granting us peace which surpasses all understanding [Phil. 4:7],[^50] that running from the night of ignorance unto the day by Thy light, we may give glory unto Thee, O Lover of mankind.

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* Having of old foreseen thee as the unhewn mountain, O all-lauded one, Daniel cried aloud manifestly of the precious stone, Christ the Savior of the world, Who was cut from thee [Dan. 2:34, 45];[^51] and honoring Him now, we the faithful praise thee, O Virgin Bride of God.

*Troparion 2:* Fallen, wretch that I am, into many tribulations, praying with anguish and weeping of heart, shamelessly I, thy servant, cry aloud: Deliver me, O Theotokos, from the troubles that encompass my lowliness, and fill me with gladness. Glory...

*Troparion 3:* Calm the fierce abyss of my passions with thy mighty prayer, O good one; for thou who didst give birth to Christ apart from passions, *Troparion 3 (continued):* that living the remainder of my life in tranquility of soul, I may sing thy praise in hymns.

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* How bearest thou God upon thine arm? Tell us; and how givest thou suck unto Him Who holds all things in His hand? O all-blessed Virgin! "I remain pure" (she said) "even after childbirth, having given birth to Christ God, taking away the debt of Adam and of the ancestral mother."

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Wholly held fast by measureless passions, I have fallen down into the whale of evils; but lead me up from corruption, O God, even as Jonah [Jon. 2:1-7],[^52] and grant me passionlessness through faith, that with the voice of praise and the spirit of salvation I may sacrifice unto Thee.

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* Not departing from the bosom of the Father, the pre-eternal Son was seated in the motherly bosom; and in the last times, He Who from before the ages was with the Father, came forth from the womb of the Virgin: leading all up unto immortal life by ineffable goodness.

*Troparion 2:* Bound with enemy chains because of affliction, woe is me, I have been cast down into the bars of Hades. But appear from heaven, O pure Maiden of God, raising me, thy servant, by thy prayers; and give a helping hand unto him who sings of thy divine childbirth. Glory...

*Troparion 3:* Wretch that I am, I have fallen into the pit of destruction, and many beasts surround me. But O Lady, by thy prayers turn away the stones and preserve thy servant unharmed; for thou didst bear in thy womb Christ, the chief Cornerstone [LXX Ps. 117:22; Eph. 2:20].[^53] Both now...

*Troparion 4:* Of old the choir of the divine prophets proclaimed prefigurations of thy childbirth, O Virgin: calling thee a radiant cloud, and a lampstand, and an urn [Exod. 25:31, 16:33; Heb. 9:4],[^54] and a table, and heavenly dew, the bread, the manna, and the gate, a throne, and a palace, a rod [Num. 17:8],[^55] and paradise, as having given birth to Christ.

*Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory... Both now...*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:*[^56]

*Sessional Hymn:* We all know thee to be the Mother of God, revealed truly as Virgin even after childbirth, fleeing with love unto thy goodness; for we sinners have thee as protectress, and in tribulations we have acquired thee as salvation, the only all-immaculate one.

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The holy youths, having of old shown forth for piety, passed through the unbearable flame of the furnace as through a bridal chamber...[^57]

*Ode 7. Heirmos (continued):* ...manifestly, and chanting in harmony, they sang the hymn: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^58]

*Troparion 1:* Passing through Thine impassable gates, O Queen of all, the Pre-eternal One preserved Thy tokens pure and intact, pure also after childbirth [LXX Ezek. 44:2].[^59] Therefore we cry aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion 2:* Cast into a furnace, I am burned by sevenfold flames of soul-destroying misfortunes; but do thou thyself, O good Lady, shower dew upon me by thy supplications, that I may cry aloud: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^60]

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* Grown old in passions and unslackening assaults and afflictions, and having arrived at the setting of my life, having no share in virtues, devoured by sloth, I cry aloud unto thee, O Lady: O consolation of earthly dwellers, have mercy on me!

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* Serving the Trinity in unity in Orthodox manner, we earthly dwellers, proclaiming thee as the pure Virgin Mother who didst bear God in the flesh, divinely sing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The furnace dewy with fire showed forth of old an image of a transcendent marvel; for the fire burned not the young children, showing forth the seedless, divine childbirth of Christ from the Virgin [LXX Dan. 3:24–27].[^61] Therefore let us sing aloud in praise: Let all creation bless the Lord, and exalt Him supremely unto all ages [LXX Dan. 3:57]![^62]

*Troparion 1:* The all-true word of the priest prefigured thy childbirth, O Virgin! For in truth thou didst give birth to the Word of God, and the Virgin womb through which God passed was not opened; therefore, rejoicing, as is due, we chant in harmony unto thee as the Mother of God, and exalt the pure one supremely unto all ages.

*Troparion 2:* Burn up with divine fire the thorns that have grown up unfruitfully in my soul, O all-immaculate one, and by thy prayers raise me up unto virtues to bear fruit unto Christ [Matt. 13:7, 22];[^63] for the ever-living Flower, having budded forth from thee, hath adorned all creation. Therefore we honor thee, the pure God-parent, unto all ages.

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* O Mother of God, grant me quickly, without hesitation, healing in fierce afflictions; for having fallen into tribulations and misfortunes, wretch that I am, I summon with weeping thy swift protection. Therefore, O all-pure one, hasten to deliver me, and save me from every torment; that *Troparion 3 (continued):* blessing I may chant of thy childbirth.

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* Of old, Aaron's rod that budded prefigured thee, O Virgin [Num. 17:8];[^64] for thou alone didst bring forth an offshoot without a husband, having now received into thy womb the heavenly rain [LXX Ps. 71:6].[^65] Therefore, rejoicing, we all chant unto thee as the Mother of God, as is due, and exalt thee supremely unto all ages.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Ineffable is the mystery of the Virgin: for she was shown forth as heaven, and the Cherubic throne, and the light-bearing bridal chamber of Christ God, the Almighty [LXX Ezek. 1:26, 10:1].[^66] Her do we piously magnify as the Mother of God.

*Troparion 1:* Most glorious is the mystery of the Virgin: for Him Whom the supreme heavenly majesties could not contain, she contained in her womb [3 Kings 8:27].[^67] Therefore, having come together, we call her blessed, and faithfully rejoicing we magnify her.

*Troparion 2:* Seeing thee alone as higher than the heavens, the radiant dawn of God, O immaculate one, the Cherubic throne, and bridal chamber, and holy bed, we earthly dwellers praising thee magnify Christ our God, Whom thou didst bear from pure loins.

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* Many afflictions surround me, and evil tribulations now assault me, and diseases and grievous sins have cast me into the pit [LXX Ps. 87:5–7].[^68] Therefore I beseech thee in the bitterness of my soul, O most holy Mother of God: grant that I may find deliverance!

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* Give peace unto the world, O Christ, through the supplications of the pure Maiden of God, casting down the enemy's power under the feet of our faithful Emperor (name),[^69] and having established ineffable tranquility for him, preserve him unto ages.

*Then, "It is truly meet." Trisagion. And after "Our Father," the Hypakoe, Tone 1. "Lord, have mercy" (40 times). Prayer: "O Thou Who at all times." "Lord, have mercy" (3 times). Glory... Both now... "More honorable than the Cherubim." "In the name of the Lord, bless, father." Then the prayer: "O undefiled, unblemished,"[^70] and the rest as usual, and the dismissal, and mutual forgiveness.*

*The Order of the Sunday Midnight Office.*[^71]

*Be it known that if there be no All-Night Vigil on Sunday, the Midnight Office takes place before Matins in this manner:*

**Priest:** Blessed is our God...

**Reader:** Amen. Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee. O Heavenly King... Trisagion. Most Holy Trinity... Our Father...

**Priest:** For Thine is the kingdom...

*Then, "Lord, have mercy" (12 times). Glory... Both now... "O come, let us worship" (3 times). Psalm 50: "Have mercy on me, O God," and straightway:*

*Canon to the Holy and Life-Originating Trinity.*[^72]
*(Whose acrostic is: I sing to Thee, O single three-sun nature. The work of Metrophanes).*[^73]

*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Thy victorious right hand hath been glorified in strength in a manner befitting God [LXX Exod. 15:6];[^74] for, O Immortal One, as almighty it shattered the adversaries, forging anew for the Israelites a path through the deep [LXX Exod. 14:21–22, 15:1–19].[^75]

*Refrain: Most Holy Trinity, our God, glory to Thee.*

*Troparion 1:* The Seraphim unceasingly glorify the one tri-hypostatic Principle: unoriginate, ever-existing, Maker of all, incomprehensible [LXX Isa. 6:2–3],[^76] Whom also every tongue faithfully honors with hymns.

*Troparion 2:* That Thou mightest show forth unto men Thy single, three-radiant Godhead, having created man of old, Thou didst fashion him according to Thine image: having given him mind, and word, and spirit, as the Lover of mankind [Gen. 1:26–27].[^77]

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* Showing forth from on high the single dominion in the three divine-originating Hypostases, O Father, Thou didst say unto Thy co-working Son and Spirit: Come, let Us go down, and confuse their tongues [Gen. 11:7]![^78]

*Both now... Theotokion:*

*Troparion 4:* Mind indeed is the unbegotten Father (as was figuratively foretold by the all-wise), and the Word is the co-unoriginate consubstantial Son, and the Holy Spirit, Who in the Virgin effected the incarnation of the Word [Luke 1:35].[^79]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou Who alone knowest the weakness of human nature, and didst compassionately take form therein: gird me with power from on high [LXX Ps. 17:33, Luke 24:49],[^80] that I may cry unto Thee: Holy is the living temple of Thine ineffable glory, O Lover of mankind!

*Troparion 1:* Thou Who of old didst manifestly appear unto Abraham as tri-hypostatic, yet single in the nature of Godhead [Gen. 18:1–3],[^81] didst prefigure the most true theology; and faithfully *Troparion 1 (continued):* we sing unto Thee, the God of single origin and three suns.

*Troparion 2:* Begotten of Thee in manner befitting God, incorruptibly, O Father, Light from Light shone forth, the immutable Son; and the divine Spirit, Light, proceeded [Nicene Creed];[^82] and we faithfully worship and glorify the tri-hypostatic radiance of the one Godhead.

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* The Trinity in Unity is praised supernaturally, ineffably beyond understanding, by the noetic beings, who cry out with thrice-holy voices unceasing praise [LXX Isa. 6:3];[^83] by whom also the tri-hypostatic Lord is sung in harmony with us.

*Both now... Theotokion:*

*Troparion 4:* In time without seed, O Mother of God, He Who is beyond all years came forth from thee, the Invisible One having become like unto us, and having taught us the one nature and lordship of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Spirit; therefore do we glorify thee.

*Lord, have mercy (3 times).*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:*

*Tone 1, Podoben: "While the soldiers were guarding Thy tomb" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*

*Sessional Hymn:* Let us all worship the Father and the Son, and the upright and co-equal Spirit; glory to the uncreated Trinity and supreme divine Power, Whom the ranks of the bodiless praise: this day let us also, who are born of earth, faithfully praise Her with fear!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Guide us into the way of repentance, who ever turn aside unto the trackless ways of evils and anger the all-good Lord, O unwedded, blessed Mary, refuge of despairing men, dwelling-place of God!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Having with clairvoyant eyes perceived thee as the mountain overshadowed by the grace of God, Habakkuk proclaimed that from thee should come forth the Holy One of Israel, for our salvation and renewal [LXX Hab. 3:2–3].[^84]

*Troparion 1:* Shine forth upon me, O three-sun divine Principle, with the rays of Thy divinizing illuminations, that in the eyes of my heart there may be envisioned the beauty, which is beyond mind, of Thy divine-originating brightness, and the light-bestowing and sweet communion!

*Troparion 2:* In the beginning Thou didst establish the heavens, O Lord, and all their host by Thy all-effecting Word, and by the co-essential Spirit of Thy mouth [LXX Ps. 32:6];[^85]

*Troparion 2 (continued):* with Whom also Thou rulest over all things in the three-radiant monad of Godhead.

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* Since Thou didst create me according to Thine image and likeness [Gen. 1:26],[^86] O divine-originating and all-effecting Trinity, unconfused Unity, give me understanding and enlighten me, that I may do Thy holy, good, (in strength) and perfect will [Rom. 12:2]![^87]

*Both now... Theotokion:*

*Troparion 4:* Thou didst give birth unto One of the Trinity, O all-immaculate one, the supreme divine Son Who for our sake was incarnate of thee, illuminating earthly dwellers with the unwaning light and radiances of the three-sun Godhead.

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Thou Who didst enlighten the ends of the world with the radiance of Thy coming, O Christ, and didst make them bright by Thy Cross: enlighten with the light of Thy divine knowledge the hearts of those who praise Thee in Orthodox faith!

*Troparion 1:* O Trinity of single origin, Who didst deign that the first angelic creation should be directly illuminated by the unapproachable rays of Thy beauty [Pseudo-Dionysius]:[^88] enlighten with Thy splendors those who in Orthodox faith sing unto Thee!

*Troparion 2:* Now human nature, O single three-sun divine Principle, which Thou didst bring into being out of goodness, sings unto Thee, asking deliverance from transgressions, and assaults, and misfortunes, and afflictions.

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* By faith we glorify Father, and Son, and Holy Spirit, one nature, dividing yet indivisible, the one God of invisible and visible creation.

*Both now... Theotokion:*

*Troparion 4:* All the sayings of the prophets prefigured thy childbirth, O all-immaculate one, which is ineffable and hard to declare; which we have known as initiating into the mystery of the single and three-sun Godhead.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* The lowest abyss hath compassed us about [LXX Jonah 2:6];[^89] there is none to deliver; we were accounted as sheep for the slaughter [LXX Ps. 43:23, Rom. 8:36].[^90] Save Thy people, O our God; for Thou art the strength of the ailing and their restoration!

*Troparion 1:* As possessing equal-standing power, O super-essential Trinity, in the sameness of will Thou art a simple and indivisible Unity; do Thou therefore preserve us by Thy power!

*Glory...*

*Troparion 2:* By Thy will, as good, Thou didst bring into being all ages out of non-being, O incomprehensible Trinity, and afterwards Thou didst create man; but even now deliver me from every distress!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

*Troparion 3:* Thou wast the house of the unsetting Sun [LXX Ps. 103:19, Mal. 4:2],[^91] Who almighty created and set in order the great luminaries [LXX Ps. 135:7–9],[^92] O all-pure Virgin Bride of God; but even now deliver me from the darkening of passions!

*Lord, have mercy (3 times).*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:*

*Tone 1, Podoben: "While the soldiers were guarding Thy tomb" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*

*Sessional Hymn:* Unto the Holy Trinity and indivisible Nature, divided without division in three Persons, and remaining undivided in the essence of Godhead, let us who are born of earth bow down with fear, and glorify Her as Creator and Master, the all-good God.

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Direct my wretched soul, O pure one, and have compassion on her because of the multitude of transgressions, who hath slipped into the abyss of destruction, O all-blameless one! And in my fearful hour of death, do thou snatch me away from the accusing demons and every torment!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* We the faithful perceive thee as a noetic furnace, O Mother of God; for as the supremely exalted One saved the three youths [Dan. 3:23–27],[^93] so He, the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers, being whole within thy womb, renewed the world!

*Troparion 1:* O Word of God, co-essential radiance of God the Almighty, even as Thou didst promise [John 14:23],[^94] make Thine abode in me by divine operation, as Thou art tenderhearted, together with Thy Father and Spirit, and show me to be fearful unto demons and passions. (Twice)

*Glory...*

*Troparion 2:* That Thou mightest show unto us the abyss of Thy tenderheartedness, O Master, Thou didst send Thy Son unto our lowliness, and didst re-fashion us unto the first brightness; but even now give me understanding by the divine Spirit!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

*Troparion 3:* He Who is borne upon the Cherubic throne, and the King of all, dwelt in thy virginal womb, O all-immaculate one, delivering all from corruption, as the Lover of mankind; but even now preserve me by thy prayers!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The furnace dewy with fire showed forth of old an image of a transcendent marvel; for the fire burned not the young children, showing forth the seedless, divine childbirth of Christ from the Virgin [LXX Dan. 3:24–27].[^95] Therefore let us sing aloud in praise: Let all creation bless the Lord, and exalt Him supremely unto all ages [LXX Dan. 3:57]![^96]

*Troparion 1:* By Thy divine nod, O Lord of all, tri-hypostatic and Almighty, Thou didst stretch out the heavens as a curtain [LXX Ps. 103:2];[^97] and Thou didst suspend the depth of the earth by Thine almighty hand [Job 26:7];[^98] therefore strengthen Thy servants also by Thy love and faith, O Lover of mankind, that we may glorify Thee with longing unto ages!

*Troparion 2:* With divine-originating light enlighten those who sing unto Thee, O Light with three suns in Persons, yet single again in essence, ever to look unto Thy light-bestowing rays, whereby I may be filled with Thy sweet, and light-giving, and most rich glory, and faithfully exalt Thee supremely unto ages!

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* Receive praise and honor from our incomprehending mind, O all-perfect Trinity, Who illuminest all things and enlightenest the mind by authority and power. The heavenly powers praise Thee; and we earthly dwellers unceasingly cry aloud, and exalt Thee supremely unto ages!

*Both now... Theotokion:*

*Troparion 4:* Having inalterably assumed human nature, Thy Son, O all-pure Mother of God, through the exceeding abundance of goodness, having delivered it from ancient corruption, ascended into the heavens [Mark 16:19, Luke 24:51, Acts 1:9].[^99] Unto Him we chant in thanksgiving: Let all creation bless the Lord, and exalt Him supremely unto all ages!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* The burning bush unburnt showed forth an image of thy pure childbearing [LXX Exod. 3:2];[^100] and now we pray thee to quench the furnace of temptations raging against us, that we may unceasingly magnify thee, O Mother of God!

*Troparion 1:* O Savior of visible and noetic creation, most holy consubstantial Trinity, save Thy servants from the enemy's assault and malice, and ever preserve Thy flock unharmed by snares!

*Troparion 2:* That Thou mightest show forth the unsearchable depth of Thine essential goodness, O three-sun and single-originating all-powerful God, Thou hast given saving promises unto Thy servants, which vouchsafe us to fulfill!

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* Look down upon our supplications, O Thou Who in three divine-originating Hypostases art the one God truly believed in; and grant consolation unto Thy servants, through the prayers of the all-pure and all-praised Mother of God.

*Both now... Theotokion:*

*Troparion 4:* He could not endure to behold earthly nature fallen into corruption through the envy of the enemy, by the disobedience of the first-created [Wis. 2:24];[^101] but bowing the heavens He came down [LXX Ps. 17:10], not departing from the Godhead, and was incarnate in the Virgin without a father, that He might deify earthly dwellers [2 Pet. 1:4].[^102]

*After this, the refrains of Gregory of Sinai, which are said after the Trinity Canons on all Sundays: "It is truly meet to glorify Thee in a manner befitting God," which are written at the end of this book.[^103] And the rest of the Midnight Office, and the dismissal.*

*At Matins.*

*After the Six Psalms we sing:*

*"God is the Lord," in Tone 1; after which, the Resurrectional Troparion (twice). Glory... To the Saint, if there be a troparion. Both now... Theotokion of the same tone. Then the usual chanting of the Psalter.*

*After the First Chanting of the Psalter:*

*Resurrectional Sessional Hymns, Tone 1:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* While the soldiers were guarding Thy tomb, O Savior, they became as dead by the lightning-flash of the angel who appeared, proclaiming the resurrection unto the women [Matt. 28:2–4].[^104] Thee do we glorify, the destroyer of corruption; unto Thee do we fall down, Who didst rise from the tomb, and unto our only God!

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end [LXX Ps. 9:33].*[^105]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Nailed willingly to the Cross, O compassionate One, and placed in a tomb as dead, O Giver of life, Thou didst crush the dominion of death by Thy death, O Mighty One! For the gatekeepers of Hades trembled at Thee [Job 38:17];[^106] and Thou didst raise up with Thyself the dead from all ages, as the only Lover of mankind!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* We all know thee to be the Mother of God, revealed truly as Virgin even after childbirth, who with love flee unto thy goodness: for we sinners have thee as protectress; we have acquired thee as salvation in tribulations, the only all-immaculate one.

*After the Second Chanting of the Psalter:*

*Sessional Hymns, Tone 1:*

*Tone 1, Podoben: "When the stone had been sealed" (Ка́мени запеча́танꙋ):*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* The women came early in the morning unto the tomb, and seeing the angelic vision they trembled [Mark 16:1–5, Luke 24:1–5].[^107] The tomb shone with *Sessional Hymn 1 (continued):* life; the marvel astonished them: therefore going, they proclaimed the resurrection unto the disciples [Matt. 28:7–8].[^108] Christ hath despoiled Hades, as the only strong and mighty One, and He hath raised up all who were corrupted, having destroyed the fear of condemnation by the Cross.

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart, I will tell of all Thy marvelous works [LXX Ps. 9:2].*[^109]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Thou wast nailed to the Cross, O Life of all, and wast numbered among the dead, O immortal Lord [LXX Ps. 87:5, Isa. 53:12];[^110] Thou didst rise on the third day, O Savior, raising Adam up from corruption. Therefore the heavenly powers cried aloud unto Thee, O Christ Giver of life: Glory to Thy resurrection! Glory to Thy condescension, O only Lover of mankind!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*

*Tone 1, Podoben: "While the soldiers were guarding Thy tomb" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*

*Theotokion:* O Mary, venerable receptacle of the Master, raise us up who have fallen into the chasm of grievous despair, and offenses, and afflictions; for thou art the salvation and help, and mighty protection of sinners, and thou savest thy servants!

*Then: "Blessed are the undefiled in the way" [LXX Ps. 118:1].[^111] After this we sing: "Blessed art Thou, O Lord," and the Resurrectional Troparia: "The assembly of angels" (which are written at the end of this book).[^112] Then the little litany, and after it the Hypakoe.*

*Hypakoe, Tone 1:*[^113]

The repentance of the thief opened Paradise [Luke 23:42–43],[^114] and the weeping of the myrrh-bearing women announced joy [Matt. 28:8–9];[^115] for Thou art risen, O Christ God, granting great mercy unto the world.

*Hymns of Ascent.*[^116]

*Antiphon 1, Tone 1:*

When I was afflicted, hear my pain, O Lord, unto Thee do I call [LXX Ps. 119:1].[^117]

Unto those who dwell in the wilderness there is unceasing divine longing, as they are outside the vain world.

*Glory...*

Unto the Holy Spirit belongs honor and glory, even as it is due unto the Father, and likewise unto the Son; therefore let us chant of the single dominion of the Trinity.

*Both now... the same.*

*Antiphon 2:*

Unto the mountains of Thy laws hast Thou lifted me up [LXX Ps. 120:1];[^118] enlighten me with virtues, O God, that I may sing unto Thee.

Taking hold of me with Thy right hand, O Word, keep me, preserve me, lest the fire of sin burn me [LXX Ps. 138:10].[^119]

*Glory...*

By the Holy Spirit all creation is renewed, running again unto its first state; for He is of equal power with the Father and the Word.

*Both now... the same.*

*Antiphon 3:*

Because of those who said unto me: Let us go into the courts of the Lord, my spirit was glad, my heart rejoices together [LXX Ps. 121:1].[^120]

In the house of David there is great fear; for there thrones being set, all the tribes of the earth and nations shall be judged [LXX Ps. 121:5].[^121]

*Glory...*

Unto the Holy Spirit it is meet to bring honor, worship, glory, and dominion, even as it is worthy of the Father, and fitts the Son; for the Trinity is a Unity in nature, though not in Persons.

*Both now... the same.*

*Prokimenon, Tone 1:*

*Now will I arise, saith the Lord; I will set Myself in salvation, I will not hesitate concerning him [LXX Ps. 11:6].*[^122]

*Verse: The words of the Lord are pure words [LXX Ps. 11:7].*[^123]

*Let every breath praise the Lord [LXX Ps. 150:6].*[^124]

*The Morning Gospel of the day. After which:*

Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ, let us worship the holy Lord Jesus, the only Sinless One. We venerate Thy Cross, O Christ, and we praise and glorify Thy holy Resurrection; for Thou art our God, and we know no other beside Thee; we call upon Thy name. Come, all ye faithful, let us worship Christ's holy Resurrection; for behold, through the Cross joy hath come into all the world. Ever blessing the Lord, we sing of His Resurrection; for having endured crucifixion, He hath destroyed death by death.

*Psalm 50: "Have mercy on me, O God."*

*Glory... Through the prayers of the Apostles, O Merciful One, blot out the multitude of our transgressions.*

*Both now... Through the prayers of the Mother of God, O Merciful One, blot out the multitude of our transgressions.*

*Then, Tone 6:*

*Have mercy on me, O God, according to Thy great mercy, and according to the multitude of Thy compassions blot out my transgression [LXX Ps. 50:3].*[^125]

*Then the Sticheron:*

Jesus having risen from the tomb, as He foretold, hath given unto us eternal life and great mercy [Matt. 28:6].[^126]

*Save, O God, Thy people... And the Exclamation: Through the mercy and compassions and love for mankind...*

*Canons.*[^127]

*The Resurrectional Canon to 4; the Cross-Resurrectional to 3; and the Theotokion to 3; and the Menaion to 4. But if a Saint be celebrated, the Resurrectional to 6, the Cross-Resurrectional to 2, and the Theotokion to 2.*

*Canon of the Resurrection, Tone 1.*[^128]

*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Thy victorious right hand hath been glorified in strength in a manner befitting God [LXX Exod. 15:6]; for, O Immortal One, as almighty it shattered the adversaries, forging anew for the Israelites a path through the deep [LXX Exod. 14:21–22, 15:1–19].

*Refrain: Glory to Thy holy resurrection, O Lord.*

*Troparia:*

*Troparion 1:* He Who in the beginning with all-pure hands divinely fashioned man from dust...[^129]

*Troparion 1 (continued):* ...shaping me, Thou didst stretch out Thy hands upon the Cross, recalling from the earth my corruptible body, which Thou didst take from the Virgin.[^130]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst submit to slaying for my sake, and didst deliver Thy soul unto death, O Thou Who by divine inbreathing didst place a soul within me; and having loosed me from eternal bonds and raised me with Thyself, Thou didst glorify me with incorruption.[^131]

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, fount of grace; rejoice, ladder and heavenly door! Rejoice, lampstand and golden urn, and unquarried mountain, who didst bear Christ the Life-giver unto the world![^132]

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Ode 1. Tone the same.*
*Heirmos:* Christ is born...[^133]
*Refrain: Glory to Thy Cross and Resurrection, O Lord.*

*Troparion 1:* Christ deifies me by being incarnate; Christ exalts me by humbling Himself; Christ makes me impassible, as the Life-giver suffers in the nature of flesh. Therefore I sing the hymn of thanksgiving: For He hath been glorified![^134]

*Troparion 2:* Christ exalts me by being crucified; Christ raises me with Himself by being slain; Christ bestows life upon me. Therefore with gladness, clapping my hands, I sing unto the Savior the victory song: For He hath been glorified![^135]

*Theotokion:* Thou didst conceive God, O Virgin, and didst bear Christ in virginity, incarnate from thee, O all-pure one: the only-begotten Son, single in hypostasis, yet recognized in two natures: For He hath been glorified![^136]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Mother of God.*
*Ode 1. Tone the same.*
*Heirmos:* Thy victorious right hand...[^137]
*Refrain: Most holy Lady, Mother of God, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* What worthy song can our weakness offer unto thee, save the joyful greeting wherewith Gabriel mystically instructed us: Rejoice, O Virgin Mother of God, unwedded Mother![^138]

*Troparion 2:* Unto the Ever-Virgin and Mother of the King of the celestial powers, let us the faithful spiritually cry aloud from a most pure heart: Rejoice, O Virgin Mother of God, unwedded Mother![^139]

*Troparion 3:* The abyss of thy birthgiving is immeasurable and incomprehensible, O all-hymned one! With undoubting faith, therefore, we purely offer unto thee, saying:[^140]

*Troparion 3 (continued):* ...Rejoice, O Virgin Mother of God, unwedded Mother!

*Then the Canon from the Menaion.*
*Katavasia:* I shall open my mouth...[^141]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou Who alone knowest the weakness of human nature, and didst compassionately take form therein: gird me with power from on high, that I may cry unto Thee: Holy is the living temple of Thine ineffable glory, O Lover of mankind![^142]

*Troparion 1:* Being my God, O Good One, Thou didst have compassion on me who was fallen, and deigning to come down unto me, Thou didst lift me up by Thy crucifixion, that I may cry unto Thee: Holy is the living temple of Thine ineffable glory, O Lover of mankind![^143]

*Troparion 2:* Being the hypostatic Life, O Christ, and having clothed Thyself with me who was corrupted, as the merciful God, descending into the dust of death, O Master, Thou didst destroy the dominion of death, and having risen from the dead on the third day, Thou didst clothe me with incorruption.[^144]

*Theotokion:* Having conceived God in thy womb through the Holy Spirit, O Virgin, thou didst remain unconsumed; since the bush burning yet unconsumed clearly prefigured thee unto the Lawgiver Moses, as one receiving the unendurable fire.[^145]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Heirmos:* Begotten of the Father before the ages...[^146]

*Troparion 1:* Unto Christ God Who took upon His shoulder the strayed sheep, and by the Tree cast down its sin, let us cry aloud: O Thou Who hast exalted our horn, holy art Thou, O Lord![^147]

*Troparion 2:* Unto Him Who brought up from hades the great Shepherd Christ, and by His sacred hierarchy wisely shepherded the nations through the Apostles, let us the faithful minister in truth and by the divine Spirit.[^148]

*Theotokion:* Unto the Son Who of His own will became incarnate without seed from the Virgin, and by divine power preserved pure after childbirth her who bare Him, unto God Who is over all, let us cry aloud: Holy art Thou, O Lord![^149]

*Canon to the Mother of God.*
*Heirmos:* Thou Who alone knowest...

*Troparion 1:* A swift cloud do we truly name thee, O Virgin, following the prophetic utterances; for upon thee the Lord came, to cast down the handmade idols of Egyptian deception, and to enlighten those who served them.[^150]

*Troparion 2:* The prophetic choir truly named thee the sealed fountain and closed door, clearly describing unto us the tokens of thy virginity, O all-hymned one,[^151] *Troparion 2 (continued):* ...which thou didst preserve even after childbirth.

*Troparion 3:* Gabriel, accounted worthy to behold the super-essential Mind as far as was possible, O all-immaculate Virgin, brought unto thee the joyful voice, clearly announcing the conception of the Word, and preaching His ineffable birth.[^152]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Perceiving thee with prophetic eyes as the mountain overshadowed by the grace of God, Habakkuk proclaimed that out of thee should go forth the Holy One of Israel, for our salvation and renewal.[^153]

*Troparion 1:* Who is this Savior Who comes from Edom, wearing a crown of thorns, having His garment stained red, hanging upon the Tree? This is the Holy One of Israel, for our salvation and renewal![^154]

*Troparion 2:* Behold, ye disobedient people, and be ashamed! For He Whom ye, with demented mind, begged Pilate to lift up on the Cross as an evildoer, having destroyed the power of death, hath risen from the tomb in a manner befitting God.[^155]

*Theotokion:* We know thee, O Virgin, as the Tree of Life; for not a death-bearing fruit of eating did bud forth from thee for men, but the enjoyment of ever-existing life, unto the salvation of us who sing of thee.[^156]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos:* Rod from the root of Jesse...[^157]

*Troparion 1:* Who is this comely One from Edom, and this redness of His garments from the vineyard of Bosor? Comely as God, yet as man wearing a garment reddened with the blood of flesh? Unto Him we the faithful sing: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^158]

*Troparion 2:* Christ, having appeared as High Priest of good things to come, hath abolished our sin; and having shown a marvelous path by His own blood, entered into the greater and more perfect tabernacle, as our Forerunner into the holy places.[^159]

*Theotokion:* Thou didst remit Eve's ancient debt, O all-hymned one, through the new Adam Who appeared for our sake; for having united unto Himself through a pure conception flesh endowed with mind and soul, Christ came forth from thee, one Lord in both.[^160]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos:* Perceiving thee as the mountain...

*Troparion 1:* Hear marvels, O heaven, and give ear, O earth! For the daughter of dust-born Adam was called of God, and became the parent of her own Creator, unto our salvation and renewal.[^161]

*Troparion 2:* We sing of thy great and awesome mystery; for hidden from the supramundane ranks, He Who Is came down upon thee like rain upon the fleece, O all-hymned one, unto the salvation of us who sing of thee.[^162]

*Troparion 3:* O Holy of holies, all-hymned Mother of God, the Expectation of the nations and salvation of the faithful! From thee shone forth the Redeemer, the Life-giver, and Lord: pray Thou Him that Thy servants may be saved.[^163]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Christ, Who didst enlighten the ends of the world with the radiance of Thy coming, and didst illumine them by Thy Cross: enlighten with the light of Thy divine knowledge the hearts of those who sing of Thee in Orthodox manner.[^164]

*Troparion 1:* The Judeans put to death by the wood of the Cross the great Shepherd of the sheep and Lord; but He, like dead sheep buried in hades, delivered them from the dominion of death.[^165]

*Troparion 2:* Having preached peace through Thy Cross, and having proclaimed remission unto captives, O my Savior, Thou didst put to shame him that had the power of death, O Christ, showing him naked and impoverished by Thy divine resurrection.[^166]

*Theotokion:* Despise not the petitions of those who faithfully ask, O all-hymned one, but receive them, and convey them unto Thy Son, O all-pure one, the only Benefactor God; for we have acquired thee as mediatress.[^167]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Heirmos:* Being the God of peace...[^168]

*Troparion 1:* O depth of the riches and wisdom of God! The Lord, catching the wise, hath delivered us from their craftiness; for having suffered willingly through the weakness of the flesh, yet quickening through His own strength, He raised the dead.[^169]

*Troparion 2:* Being God, Christ is united to flesh for our sake, and is crucified, and dies; He is buried, and rises again, and ascends radiantly with His flesh unto the Father; wherewith He shall come again, and save those who piously serve Him.[^170]

*Theotokion:* O Holy of holies, pure Virgin, thou didst give birth to the Holy of holies Who sanctifies all, Christ the Redeemer. Therefore we proclaim thee Queen and Lady of all, as Mother of the Creator of creation.[^171]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos:* O Christ, Who didst enlighten...

*Troparion 1:* The heavenly Hosts make merry, beholding thee; the gatherings of men rejoice with them; for through thy childbirth they were joined together, O Virgin Mother of God, which we worthily glorify.[^172]

*Troparion 2:* Let all human tongues and minds be moved unto praise of the true adornment of mankind! The Virgin manifestly stands forth, glorifying those who sing in faith of her marvels.[^173]

*Troparion 3:* Glorified is the song and praise of the all-wise, offered unto the Virgin and Mother of God; for she was the temple of super-divine glory, whom we worthily glorify.[^174]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* The lowest abyss hath encompassed us, and there is none to deliver; we are accounted as sheep for the slaughter; save Thy people, O our God, for Thou art the strength of the ailing, and their restoration![^175]

*Troparion 1:* By the transgression of the first-created, O Lord, we were grievously wounded; but by Thy wound, wherewith Thou wast wounded for our sake, O Christ, we were healed; for Thou art the strength of the ailing, and their restoration![^176]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast brought us up out of hades, O Lord, having slain the all-devouring sea monster, O Almighty One, casting down his power by Thy dominion; for Thou art Life, and Light, and the Resurrection.[^177]

*Theotokion:* The forefathers of our race rejoice in thee, O all-pure Virgin, having received again through thee the Eden which they lost by transgression; for thou art pure both before childbirth and after childbirth.[^178]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Heirmos:* From the belly Jonah...[^179]

*Troparion 1:* Being an impassible and immaterial Mind, Christ God was joined to the human mind, the divine nature mediating with the grossness of the flesh; and without change He was wholly united unto the whole of me, that being crucified He might grant salvation unto the whole of me who had fallen.[^180]

*Troparion 2:* Adam, having been deceived, falls, and being tripped up is crushed, having been seduced of old by the hope of deification; but he ariss, being deified by union with the Word, and through suffering receives impassibility, being glorified upon the throne as a son, sitting with the Father and the Spirit.[^181]

*Theotokion:* Not leaving the bosom of the unoriginate Begetter, He takes up His abode in the bosom of the pure Maiden; and He[^182] *Theotokion (continued):* ...Who was formerly without mother becomes incarnate without father, God Who reigns in righteousness; His fearsome lineage is without genealogy and ineffable.

*Canon to the Mother of God.*
*Heirmos:* The lowest abyss hath encompassed us...

*Troparion 1:* The celestial ranks stand in servitude before thy childbirth, marveling worthily at thy seedless birthgiving, O Ever-Virgin! For thou art pure both before childbirth and after childbirth.[^183]

*Troparion 2:* The Word, Who previously was bodiless, became incarnate from thee, O all-pure one, creating all things by His will: He Who brought forth from non-being the hosts of incorporeal powers, as almighty.[^184]

*Troparion 3:* The enemy was slain by thy life-bearing fruit, O Virgin full of God's grace! And hades was trampled underfoot manifestly, and we who were in bonds were set free. Therefore I cry aloud: Destroy the passions of my heart![^185]

*Kontakion, Tone 1, Podoben: "When Thou shalt come" (Є҆гда̀ прїи́деши):*
Thou didst rise as God from the tomb in glory, and didst raise the world with Thyself; and human nature sings of Thee as God, and death hath vanished; and Adam keeps festival, O Master, and Eve now delivered from bonds rejoices, crying aloud: Thou art He Who grants resurrection unto all, O Christ![^186]

*Ikos:* Let us sing of Him Who rose on the third day as the almighty God, Who shattered the gates of hades, and raised from the tomb those who were there from the ages, Who appeared unto the Myrrh-bearing women as He was well pleased, saying first unto them: "Rejoice!"; and proclaims joy unto the Apostles, as the only Life-giver. Therefore in faith the women announce the tokens of victory unto the disciples, and hades groans, and death wails; the world makes merry, and all rejoice with it; for Thou, O Christ, hast granted resurrection unto all![^187]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* We the faithful perceive thee, O Mother of God, as a spiritual furnace; for as the Supremely Exalted One saved the three children, so the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers wholly renewed the world in thy womb.[^188]

*Troparion 1:* The earth was seized with fear, the sun was hidden and the light was darkened, the divine veil of the temple was rent, and the rocks were split; for the Righteous One hangs upon the Cross,[^189] *Troparion 1 (continued):* ...the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers!

*Troparion 2:* Thou becamest as one helpless, and wounded among the dead willingly for our sake, O Supremely Exalted One; Thou didst liberate all, and by Thy mighty hand didst raise them with Thyself: the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^190]

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, fount of ever-living water! Rejoice, delightful paradise! Rejoice, unwedded one! Rejoice, universal joy, through whom shone forth unto us the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers![^191]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos:* The children brought up in piety...[^192]

*Troparion 1:* Of old indeed was the earth cursed, having been stained red with Abel's blood by a fratricidal hand; but sprinkled with Thy God-flowing blood it was blessed, and leaping for joy it cries: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou![^193]

*Troparion 2:* Let the Judean God-opposing people wail for the audacity of slaying Christ; but let the nations make merry, and clap their hands, and cry aloud: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou![^194]

*Theotokion:* Behold, shining upon the Myrrh-bearers, the Angel cried aloud: Come and see the tokens of Christ's resurrection, the Plashchanytsia and the tomb, and cry aloud: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou![^195]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos:* We the faithful perceive thee...

*Troparion 1:* Jacob prophetically perceived thee as the ladder, O Mother of God; for through thee the Supremely Exalted One appeared upon earth, and dwelt with men as He was well pleased: the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^196]

*Troparion 2:* Rejoice, pure one! From thee came forth the Shepherd, Who truly clothed Himself with Adam's skin, (the Supremely Exalted One) putting on the whole of me as man on account of His incomprehensible compassion: the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^197]

*Troparion 3:* The pre-eternal God truly became the New Adam from thy pure blood. Him do thou now entreat, to renew him that calls aloud: O praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers![^198]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* In the furnace, as in a crucible, the children of Israel shone purer than gold with the beauty of piety, saying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing praise and exalt Him supremely unto all ages![^199]

*Troparion 1:* O Word of God, Who by Thy will dost make and transform all things, turning the shadow of death into eternal life by Thy Passion: Thee, the Lord, do all the works of the Lord unceasingly praise, and exalt supremely unto all ages.[^200]

*Troparion 2:* Having risen from the tomb on the third day, O Christ, Thou didst break down the ruin and misery in the gates and strongholds of hades. Thee do all works unceasingly sing as Lord, and exalt supremely unto all ages.[^201]

*Theotokion:* Let us sing of her who seedlessly and supernaturally, from the divine radiance, gave birth to Christ the pearl of great price, saying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing praise and exalt Him supremely unto all ages![^202]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos:* Showed forth of old an image of a transcendent marvel...[^203]

*Troparion 1:* Come, ye people, let us worship the place where the all-pure feet stood, and where upon the Tree the divine life-creating hands of Christ were outstretched for the salvation of all men; and standing about the life-bearing tomb, let us sing: Let all creation bless the Lord, and exalt Him supremely unto all ages![^204]

*Troparion 2:* The most iniquitous slander of the God-slaying Judeans was refuted; for He Whom they called a deceiver arose as the Mighty One, mocking the foolish seals. Therefore rejoicing let us sing: Let all creation bless the Lord, and exalt Him supremely unto all ages![^205]

*Triadikon:* The all-pure Seraphim, theologizing the glory in three radiances and one Lordship, with fear in servitude glorify the tri-hypostatic Godhead. With them let us also piously sing: Let all creation bless the Lord, and exalt Him supremely unto all ages![^206]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos:* In the furnace the children of Israel...

*Troparion 1:* Let us all sing of the light-bearing bridal chamber, from which Christ the Master of all came forth as a Bridegroom, crying aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing praise and exalt Him supremely unto all ages![^207]

*Troparion 2:* Rejoice, glorious throne of God! Rejoice, wall of the faithful, through whom Christ the Light shone forth unto those in darkness, who call thee blessed,[^208] *Troparion 2 (continued):* ...and cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing praise and exalt Him supremely unto all ages!

*Troparion 3:* Having given birth unto the Lord Who is the Author of salvation for us, entreat Him earnestly for all who cry aloud, O all-hymned Virgin: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing praise and exalt Him supremely unto all ages![^209]

*Then the Canticle of the Mother of God:*
*My soul doth magnify the Lord.*
*With the refrain:*
*More honorable than the Cherubim...*[^210]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* The burning bush showed forth unconsumed an image of thy pure childbirth; and now we beseech thee to quench the furnace of temptations raging against us, that we may unceasingly magnify thee, O Mother of God![^211]

*Troparion 1:* O how the lawless and disobedient people, taking evil counsel, justified the proud and impious one, but condemned upon the Tree the Righteous One, the Lord of glory! Whom we worthily magnify.[^212]

*Troparion 2:* O Savior, blameless Lamb, Who takest away the sins of the world, Thee do we glorify, Who didst rise on the third day with the Father and Thy divine Spirit, even the Lord of glory; Whom theologizing we magnify.[^213]

*Theotokion:* Save Thy people, O Lord, whom Thou didst purchase with Thy precious blood, granting unto our Emperor (name) strength against enemies, and bestowing peace upon Thy churches, O Lover of mankind, through the prayers of the Mother of God.[^214]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos:* A strange mystery...[^215]

*Troparion 1:* Thy Cross hath been glorified by Thine ineffable power, O Lord! For Thy weakness was shown forth unto all as stronger than strength; whereby the mighty indeed were cast down to the earth, and the poor are led up to heaven.[^216]

*Troparion 2:* Our loathsome death was put to death by Thy resurrection from the dead; for appearing unto those in hades, O Christ, Thou didst bestow life. Therefore singing of Thee as Life and Resurrection, and hypostatic Light, we magnify Thee.[^217]

*Triadikon:* The unoriginate and boundless nature is known in three unities, the divine-originating hypostases: one Godhead in Father, and Son, and Spirit; trusting in Whom our divinely-wise Emperor (name) is saved.[^218]

*Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos:* The burning bush showed forth unconsumed...[^219]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst sprout forth from the root of David, the prophet and forefather of God, O Virgin, but thou didst also truly glorify David, having given birth unto the prophesied Lord of glory; Whom we worthily magnify.[^220]

*Troparion 2:* Every law of praise is overcome by the majesty of thy glory, O all-pure one. But, O Lady, from thine unworthy servants receive with fervor, from love offered unto thee, O Mother of God, this song of praise![^221]

*Troparion 3:* O thy marvels beyond understanding! For thou alone brighter than the sun, O Virgin, didst grant unto all to understand the newest wonder of thine incomprehensible childbirth, O all-immaculate one; therefore do we all magnify thee.[^222]

*After the Katavasia, the Little Litany. Then:*
*Holy is the Lord our God (3 times).*[^223]
*The Morning Exapostilarion.*[^224]
*AT THE PRAISES*[^225]
*Resurrectional Stichera, Tone 1.*

We sing of Thy saving Passion, O Christ, and we glorify Thy Resurrection.[^226]

O Thou Who didst endure the Cross, and didst abolish death, and didst rise from the dead: bring peace unto our life, O Lord, as the only Almighty One.[^227]

O Thou Who didst despoil hades, and didst raise man by Thy resurrection, O Christ: vouchsafe us with a pure heart to sing of Thee and to glorify Thee.[^228]

Glorifying Thy divine condescension, we sing of Thee, O Christ: Thou wast born of the Virgin, yet wast not separated from the Father; Thou didst suffer as man, and willingly didst endure the Cross; Thou didst rise from the tomb, coming forth as from a bridal chamber, that Thou mightest save the world: Lord, glory to Thee![^229]

*Other Stichera, Anatolian.*[^230]
*Tone the same.*

When Thou wast nailed upon the wood of the Cross, then was the enemy's dominion slain; creation was shaken with fear of Thee, and hades was despoiled by Thy dominion; the dead Thou didst raise from the tombs, and unto the thief Thou didst open paradise: Christ our God, glory to Thee![^231]

The venerable women, having reached Thy tomb with weeping and haste, and finding the tomb opened, and having learned from the Angel the new and most glorious marvel, announced unto the Apostles: That the Lord is risen, granting great mercy unto the world.[^232]

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end [LXX Ps. 9:33].*[^233]

*Anatolian Sticheron 3:* We venerate the divine wounds of Thy passions, O Christ God, and the sovereign hierurgy accomplished upon Sion, which was manifested by God at the end of the ages; for the Sun of righteousness hath enlightened those sleeping in darkness, guiding them unto the unwaning radiance: O Lord, glory to Thee![^234]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will speak of all Thy marvelous works [LXX Ps. 9:2].*[^235]

*Anatolian Sticheron 4:* Give ear, O riotous Jewish race! Where are they who came unto Pilate? Let the guarding soldiers say: where are the seals of the tomb? Where was He laid Who was buried? Where was He sold Who was not sold? How was the treasure stolen? Why do ye slander the Savior's rising, O lawless Judeans? He is risen Who is free among the dead, and He grants great mercy unto the world.[^236]

*Glory... Morning Evangelical Sticheron. Both now... Theotokion: Most blessed art thou [O Virgin Theotokos]: The Great Doxology. Resurrectional Troparion: Today is salvation come into the world: Litanies, and the Dismissal.*[^237]

*AT THE LITURGY*[^238]
*The Typika, The Beatitudes.*
*Tone 1.*

*Troparion 1:* Through eating of food, the enemy led Adam out of paradise; but through the Cross, Christ led the thief back into it, who cried: Remember me when Thou comest in Thy kingdom![^239]

*Troparion 2:* I venerate Thy passions, I glorify Thy Resurrection; with Adam and the thief, with a radiant voice I cry unto Thee: Remember me, O Lord, when Thou comest in Thy kingdom![^240]

*Troparion 3:* Thou wast crucified, O Sinless One, and wast laid in the tomb of Thine own will; but Thou didst rise as God, raising Adam up with Thyself: Remember me, who cry, when Thou comest in Thy kingdom![^241]

*Troparion 4:* Having raised the temple of Thy body by Thy three-day burial, Thou didst raise up Adam and those descended from Adam, O Christ God: Remember us who cry, when Thou comest in Thy kingdom![^242]

*Troparion 5:* The Myrrh-bearers came weeping unto Thy tomb, O Christ God, very early in the morning; and they found an Angel sitting in white robes,[^243] *Troparion 5 (continued):* ...who cried: Why seek ye? Christ is risen; weep no more![^244]

*Troparion 6:* Thine Apostles, O Lord, having come unto the mountain where Thou hadst commanded them, O Savior, and seeing Thee, worshipped; and Thou didst send them unto the nations, to teach and to baptize them.[^245]

*Glory... Triadikon:* Let us worship the Father, let us glorify the Son, and together let us praise the All-holy Spirit, crying and saying: O All-holy Trinity, save us all![^246]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Thy people bring Thy Mother unto Thee in prayer, O Christ! By her supplications grant us Thy compassions, O Good One, that we may glorify Thee Who didst shine forth unto us from the tomb.[^247]

## Monday: The Holy Bodiless Powers[^248]

*On Sunday Evening, at Vespers:*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera of the Master:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "O all-lauded martyrs" (Прехва́льніи мꙋ́ченици):*

*Sticheron 1:* O All-good Father! Thou didst send Thy Son, the All-good Lord, to take the prodigal son upon His shoulders, to bring back the straying sheep, and to lead him back whence he had fallen: into the fold of Thine Angels, the undeluded powers.[^249]

*Sticheron 2:* O All-good Father! Moved by the abundant goodness of Thy tender-hearted compassions, as God Thou didst bring all things out of non-existence, and afterward didst create man with Thy hand according to Thine image; and when he had become corrupt through the transgression of the commandments, Thou didst rebuild him, as the Lover of mankind.[^250]

*Sticheron 3:* O Lord, Lover of mankind, Who art the all-true Son of the Heavenly Begetter! Thou wast manifested for our sake, being the Son of Man, that taking me upon Thy shoulders as a sheep—man deceived by the deceit of the serpent—Thou mightest lead me up into the heavenly fold.[^251]

*Other Stichera, to the Bodiless Powers.*
*Tone and Podoben the same.*

*Sticheron 1:* Ye bodiless Angels, who stand before the throne of God, illuminated with the radiances thence proceeding, and shedding streams of light,[^252] *Sticheron 1 to the Bodiless Powers (continued):* ...ever shining, and becoming secondary lights: pray ye unto Christ to grant peace and great mercy unto our souls![^253]

*Sticheron 2:* Ye immortal Angels, all-rich ones, having truly received imperishable life from the First Life! Ye have become partakers of ever-existing glory and holy beholders of eternal wisdom, filled with light, and transformed into lamps, fitly manifested.[^254]

*Sticheron 3:* Archangels, Angels, Principalities, Thrones, Dominions, six-winged Seraphim, and divine many-eyed Cherubim, instruments of wisdom, most divine Powers and Authorities: pray ye unto Christ to grant peace and great mercy unto our souls![^255]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Having become the pure dwelling-place of God, O pure Virgin! Pray with the bodiless hosts that I may purely accomplish the journey of this life, that the evening of death may not overtake me unprepared and dispatch me into the flame of fire to be tormented; but snatch me away from this![^256]

*Then: "O Joyful Light." Then the Prokeimenon, Tone 1:*
*Behold now, bless ye the Lord, all ye servants of the Lord.*
*Verse: Ye that stand in the house of the Lord, in the courts of the house of our God [LXX Ps. 133:1–2].*[^257]
*Then: "Vouchsafe, O Lord, to keep us this evening without sin," and the Litany.*

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera of Compunction, Tone 1:*

*Sticheron 1:* As the abyss of my transgressions is vast, O Savior, and I am sorely overwhelmed by my sins: give me Thy hand, as unto Peter; save me, O God, and have mercy on me![^258]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven [LXX Ps. 122:1].*[^259]

*Sticheron 2:* Seeing that in evil thoughts and deeds I have condemned myself, O Savior: grant me the thought of repentance, O God, that I may cry unto Thee: Save me, O good Benefactor, and have mercy on me![^260]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt [LXX Ps. 122:3].*[^261]

*Sticheron 3:* Through the prayers of all the Saints and of the Theotokos, O Lord, grant us Thy peace, and have mercy on us, as Thou alone art compassionate.[^262]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion Automelon:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Joy of the heavenly ranks" (Небе́сныхъ чинѡ́въ):*

Joy of the heavenly ranks, mighty protection of men upon earth, O most pure Virgin! Save us who flee unto thee; for after God, O Mother of God, we have placed our hope in thee.[^263]

*Then: "Lord, now lettest Thou Thy servant depart." Trisagion through "Our Father." Troparion of the Saint. Glory... Both now... Theotokion. Then the Litany, and the Dismissal.*[^264]

*On Sunday, at Compline:*[^265]
*Supplicatory Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 1. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Thy victorious right hand hath been glorified in strength in manner becoming God; for that hand, O Immortal One, being all-powerful, shattered the adversaries, fashioning anew for the Israelites a path through the deep.[^266]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^267]

*Troparion 1:* Ineffably thou didst conceive God, and beyond nature didst give birth unto Him, O all-pure Sovereign Lady, Who redeemed earthly beings from transgressions; therefore I entreat thee: Deliver me from my many transgressions![^268]

*Troparion 2:* I am overwhelmed by an abyss of sins, and countless tribulations and sorrows; but by thy mighty pilotage and by thy prayer, O All-immaculate One, direct me unto the divine stillness of repentance.[^269]

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* O protectress of my whole life, deliver me from many perils, that with a voice of thanksgiving I may hymn thee, O pure Mother of God; and snatch me away from future torments![^270]

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* Quell the assaults of tribulations and the storm of temptations, O all-immaculate Mother of the Redeemer, Creator, and our God, granting me the grace of salvation and purification.[^271]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Thou Who alone knowest the weakness of human nature, and hast mercifully taken form therein: gird me with power from on high, that I may cry aloud unto Thee: Holy is the living temple of Thine ineffable glory, O Lover of mankind![^272]

*Troparion 1:* The vision of thy pure countenance is indeed sweet unto Angels, saving unto men, and dreadful unto the spirits of wickedness; honoring and kissing it with faith, O Mother of God, we enlighten our souls.[^273]

*Troparion 2:* With virginal blood from thee, the Creator and Lord clothed His flesh; entreat Him, O All-immaculate One, to show compassion for Thine ineffable mercy's sake unto me, corrupted by unfitting deeds.[^274]

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* Having gained thee as the guardian of my life and an impregnable rampart: vouchsafe me also after my departure to find thee as a shelter and an invincible champion, leading me unto God, and bestowing eternal life and glory, O All-immaculate One.[^275]

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* Seedless was thy conception, O Pure One, and incorrupt thy childbirth; for God clearly was He[^276] *Troparion 4 (continued):* ...Who was born, calling human nature back from its fall. Therefore we praise thee as the true and real Mother of God.[^277]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Beholding thee with prophetic eyes as the mountain overshadowed by the grace of God, Habbakuk proclaimed that the Holy One of Israel should go forth from thee, unto our salvation and renewal.[^278]

*Troparion 1:* An unrestrained rush toward evil draws me on through the working of the enemy and evil habit; help me, O Sovereign Lady, lest the all-destroying one fall upon me utterly, hindering me by death from repentance.[^279]

*Troparion 2:* I have spent the whole time of my life in wicked fornications and grievous wounds; and now the workers of these things drag my humble soul away: O Virgin, Mother of God, help me![^280]

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* Give ear unto my sorrowful voice, O All-immaculate One, which with groanings I offer from the depth of my soul, and grant me remission of the debts which I, wretched one, have incurred in thought and foolish disposition.[^281]

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* O Sovereign Lady of the world, Mother of God, help me! The most wicked enemy swallows me up greedily with grievous remembrances and imaginations that separate me from God; therefore leave me not, nor despise me.[^282]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Christ, Who hast enlightened the ends of the world with the radiance of Thy coming, and hast illumined them with Thy Cross: enlighten the hearts of those who praise Thee in orthodox fashion with the light of Thy divine knowledge.[^283]

*Troparion 1:* Behold, I have gained a ready help unto salvation; therefore I fall down before thee, and cry with tears: O Mother of God, deliver me from the arrows of the stranger, and from the future reckoning.[^284]

*Troparion 2:* Thou art my strength, thou art my praise and rejoicing, and thou art my guardian, protection, and refuge, and an invincible champion, O most holy Virgin Theotokos; therefore save thy servant.[^285]

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* Cleanse my wretched soul, defiled by passions, with the sprinkling of thy divine prayer, O all-pure Mother of God, granting the radiant garment of thy salvation.[^286]

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4 (continued):* In my life thou art my steadfast pilot, delivering me from the tempest of many tribulations; and at my departure (I pray thee) stand by me, saving me, O Mother of Christ God![^287]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* The lowest abyss hath surrounded us, there is none that delivers; we are accounted as sheep for the slaughter; save Thy people, O our God! For Thou art the strength of the infirm and their restoration.[^288]

*Troparion 1:* O unwedded Mother of God, all-pure one! Stretch forth thy hand unto me, and snatch me out of the depth of evils which I, most wretched one, have brought upon my passionate soul by my folly.[^289]

*Troparion 2:* Arise, O pure one, unto the aid of thy servant, who placs his hope, after God, in thine invincible protection, lest the enemy prevail, and seize and destroy me.[^290]

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* Healer of the sick, resurrection of the fallen, cleansing of sinners, O Mother of God: I entreat thee, falling down with tears, and I cry unto thee: As thou art powerful, save me who am perishing![^291]

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* Have mercy, O pure one, upon my humble soul; prevent and snatch it away, rescuing it from the evil one; for he strivs to cast it into the pit on account of the evils which great sloth hath wrought.[^292]

*Then: "Lord, have mercy" (3 times). Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "While the soldiers were guarding Thy tomb" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*
