All of us have acquired thee as an intercessor before the Lord, O Pure One, and we run unto thy holy temple, asking help from thee, O Ever-virgin! Deliver us therefore from demonic malice and torment, and from the fearful condemnation, who call thee blessed.[^293]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* As a spiritual furnace, O Mother of God, do we the faithful perceive thee; for even as He Who is supremely exalted saved the three Children, so hath the praiseworthy and supremely glorified God of our fathers renewed the whole world within thy womb.[^294]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast the divine tabernacle of the Master, having conceived Him in thy womb and given birth unto Him in the flesh, O only Immaculate One. Therefore deliver us from passions and diseases, and sorrows and debts; for thou hast strength and great power.[^295]

*Troparion 2:* Quell the intolerable sickness, O Pure One, of my most wretched[^296] *Troparion 2 (continued):* ...soul, granting me true joy, O Sovereign Lady, dispelling the turbid wave of my many transgressions; for thou art my refuge and deliverance.[^297]

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* All the hope of my salvation have I placed upon thee; for I have acquired thee as a help, an unconquerable pillar, and a confirmation, and an assurance of steadfastness; and through thee I hope to obtain the Kingdom, O Sovereign Lady.[^298]

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* Thou wast the Orient of the Sun of glory, O pure Sovereign Lady; for through thee He was manifested, and freed all from darkness, ignorance, and foul transgressions. Therefore I cry unto thee: Free me from outer darkness![^299]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* In the furnace the Children of Israel, as in a crucible, shone more purely than gold with the beauty of piety, saying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing praise and exalt Him supremely unto all ages![^300]

*Troparion 1:* The turmoil of passions sorely grievs my soul, O Mother of God! But having given birth unto the Author and Giver of stillness and peace, O Pure One, preserving me in a peaceful frame, fill me with gentle joy and gladness.[^301]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast been shown forth as the Mediatress of salvation unto us, O Mother of God, having given birth unto the Savior and Master of all. Therefore I entreat thee: Vouchsafe salvation unto my humble soul, that I may faithfully hymn thee unto all ages.[^302]

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* O thou who didst ineffably conceive the Creator of all and God: save me from corruption and every temptation, and grant me ever to cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing praise and exalt Him supremely unto all ages![^303]

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* Thou didst give birth unto the Creator of all and God, O Virgin! Entreat Him to grant cleansing of transgressions, and deliverance from afflictions and trials, and release from eternal fire, unto those who unceasingly glorify thy glory.[^304]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* The bush burning with fire yet unconsumed showed forth an image of thy pure childbirth; and now we beseech thee to quench the furnace of temptations raging against us, that we may unceasingly magnify thee, O Mother of God![^305]

*Troparion 1:* O all-immaculate Mother of God, open unto me thy merciful compassions, and deliver me from the mouth of the spiritual wolf that seeks to destroy and corrupt[^306] *Troparion 1 (continued):* ...me. Have mercy, I pray thee; turn not away thy servant put to shame![^307]

*Troparion 2:* Be thou the protectress of my whole life, and my firm defense; and deliver me, O All-pure One, from many trials and sorrows, and from the talkative slander of enemies; yea, and deliver me from eternal fire.[^308]

*Glory...*

*Troparion 3:* O sole visitation of the sick, sole restoration of the fallen, O Sovereign Lady! Sole guide unto God and entrance, sole Mediatress of eternal good things: have mercy upon me who alone have sinned more than all.[^309]

*Both now...*

*Troparion 4:* Receive now my tearful prayer, and grant me remission of transgressions and of the many evils which I have wrought, O all-hymned Sovereign Lady, Mother of God! For I am perishing utterly through boundless despair.[^310]

*Then: "It is truly meet to bless thee," and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*[^311]

*On Monday at Matins:*[^312]
*After the 1st Kathisma of the Psalter, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 1:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* In iniquities was I conceived, prodigal that I am; I dare not look up unto the height of heaven; yet daring upon Thy love for mankind, I cry: O God, cleanse me and save me![^313]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath [LXX Ps. 6:2].*[^314]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* If the righteous scarcely be saved, where shall I appear, a sinner, who have not borne the burden and heat of the day? But number me, O God, with the laborers of the eleventh hour, and save me![^315]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Beholding the wonder of wonders in thee, O full of grace, creation rejoices; for having conceived without seed, thou didst ineffably give birth unto Him Whom the ranks of Angels cannot gaze upon; entreat Him, O Mother of God, that our souls may be saved.[^316]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "While the soldiers were guarding Thy tomb" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Make haste to open Fatherly embraces unto me; prodigally have I spent my life; looking upon the inexhaustible riches of Thy compassions, O Savior, despise not now my impoverished heart; for unto Thee, O Lord, in compunction do I cry: I have sinned against Thee, save me![^317]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath [LXX Ps. 6:2].*[^318]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Having foolishly withdrawn myself from Thee, O All-compassionate One! Prodigally have I spent my life, ever serving irrational passions. But receive me, through the prayers of Thine Angels, O tender-hearted Father, as the prodigal son, and save me, I pray Thee![^319]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel [LXX Ps. 67:36].*[^320]

*Martyrikon:* Clothed by Thee, O Lord, in the praise of suffering and the dignity of crowns, the glorious Passion-bearers conquered through the endurance of wounds inflicted by the lawless, and by divine power received victory from heaven. Through their prayers deliver me also from the invisible enemy, O Savior, and save me.[^321]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* O steadfast protectress and helper of all! Unshakeable hope and defender of the world! Have mercy upon us as thou art good, and grant cleansing of transgressions and salvation of souls, and guide us onto the path of repentance; for unto thee do we flee in faith, and thee do we entreat warmly, and ask in love: Withdraw not thy mercy from us.[^322]

*Other Sessional Hymns, when the 3rd Kathisma is chanted:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "While the soldiers were guarding Thy tomb" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Thy tribunal is fearful and Thy judgment is just, but my deeds are grievous; yet do Thou, O Merciful One, anticipate me and save me, and deliver me from torment; deliver me, O Master, from the portion of the goats, and vouchsafe me to stand on Thy right hand, O most righteous Judge![^323]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Surpassing the immaterial hosts, O Maiden, and alone transcending the heavenly Ranks, thou receivest worthy praise from them, O All-pure One! But entreat Thy Son with the Angels, that I, who alone am condemned, may be delivered from the tyranny of passions.[^324]

*Penitential Canon,*[^325]
*whose acrostic is: "Wash away the filth of my transgressions, O Word!"*
*(The work of Lord Joseph).*
*Tone 1. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Thy victorious right hand hath been glorified in strength in manner becoming God; for that hand, O Immortal One, being all-powerful, shattered the adversaries, fashioning anew for the Israelites a path in the deep.[^326]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*[^327]

*Troparion 1:* The multitude of my sins blot out, I pray Thee, O Christ, according to the multitude of Thy mercy, and grant me the thought of conversion, that I may glorify Thy goodness which surpasses understanding.[^328]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast manifested on earth through the loving-kindness of Thy compassions, taking flesh[^329] *Troparion 2 (continued):* ...as man; therefore receive me, who have sinned more than any man, O Word of God, who now fall down in repentance before Thy compassions.[^330]

*Refrain: Holy Martyrs, pray to God for us.*[^331]

*Martyrikon 1:* Imitating the slaying and death of Christ Who suffered willingly, ye endured manifold sufferings and death, O ever-memorable ones; therefore, O Martyrs, ye attained immortal life.[^332]

*Martyrikon 2:* Bearing the fire of divine love most gloriously within, O Martyrs, ye feared not the material fire; but by the fervent endeavors of your sufferings ye consumed every substance of polytheism.[^333]

*Theotokion:* The ranks of the bodiless hosts sing of thee with us, O all-hymned Virgin! For thou didst give birth in the flesh unto the Uncontainable One within thy womb, remaining a virgin, even as before childbirth, O Bride of God.[^334]

*Second Canon, to the Bodiless Powers,*[^335]
*whose acrostic is: "Theophan's first song to the Angels."*
*(The work of Lord Theophanes).*
*Tone 1. Ode 1. Heirmos the same.*

*Refrain: Holy Archangels and Angels, pray to God for us.*[^336]

*Troparion 1 (twice):* Standing radiantly before the Master's throne, all-holy Angels: entreat the Son co-unoriginate with the Father, even the Angel of Great Counsel, to inspire speech in me who hymn you.[^337]

*Troparion 2:* Being mirrors of the God-originating light, receiving within themselves the radiance of the thrice-sunny lamp so far as is fathomable: the Divine Intellect, having first conceived the Angelic Ranks, brought them into being.[^338]

*Theotokion:* He Who as God adorned the commanders of the hosts on high, took up His dwelling within thy womb that knew not wedlock, which is higher than the Seraphim, O Mother of God, and unchangeably became flesh.[^339]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Thou Who alone knowest the weakness of human nature, and hast mercifully taken form therein: gird me with power from on high, that I may cry aloud unto Thee: Holy is the living temple of Thine ineffable glory, O Lover of mankind![^340]

*Troparion 1:* Arise, come, O my soul, and cry aloud unto thy Creator Who knows all thy secret things, and bring forth fruits of repentance, that the compassionate Lord may have mercy upon thee and deliver thee from eternal fire.[^341]

*Troparion 2:* Cleanse me, O only Good One, cleanse and save me, as the publican who[^342] *Troparion 2 (continued):* ...with fear I cry unto Thee: dragged down by a multitude of sins, and bowed down by the weight of transgressions, and filled with boundless shame.[^343]

*For the Martyrs:* Filled with true wisdom and understanding, the Martyrs made foolish the wisdom of the Greeks, and destroyed their crafty malice; and having suffered valiantly, they worthily received the crowns of victory, rejoicing.[^344]

*For the Martyrs:* Confessing a Monad indeed in essence, but a Trinity in Persons, O passion-bearers, ye destroyed the error of polytheism through divine faith, and were shown forth as luminaries, enlightening the hearts of all with beams of grace.[^345]

*Theotokion:* O holy and pure Bride of God, thou didst give birth unto the Holy One Who rests among the saints, the Son and Word co-unoriginate with the Father, Who sanctifies in the Holy Spirit those who piously worship Him.[^346]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Heirmos the same:*
*Troparion 1:* Directly drawing near unto the God-working Light, the Seraphim, being abundantly filled by Him with primary effulgences, shine forth in primary fashion, and become secondary lights, deified by the Godhead. *(Twice)*[^347]

*Troparion 2:* Desiring with fervor to praise the angelic splendors, we faithful ask the help granted by God through them, with purity of mind and most pure lips, that we may receive their illumination.[^348]

*Theotokion:* Deemed worthy, as was meet, to behold the Supersubstantial Mind, Gabriel brought unto thee the joyful salutation, O all-immaculate Virgin, manifestly announcing unto thee the conception of the Word and proclaiming thine ineffable childbearing.[^349]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Habakkuk, beholding with foresighted eyes the mountain overshadowed by the grace of God, foretold that the Holy One of Israel should come forth from thee, unto our salvation and renewal.[^350]

*Troparion:* Unto whom shall I liken thee, O my wretched soul, who workest evil and doest not good? Turn back, cry aloud unto Him Who willingly became poor for thy sake: O Knower of hearts, have compassion and save me![^351]

*Troparion:* Thou hast ordained repentance, O Savior, for those who turn back; grant this unto me, O Good One, before the end of life, giving me compunction and sighing, as formerly unto the harlot...[^352]

*Troparion 2 (continued):* ...who kissed Thy feet, O Master.[^353]

*For the Martyrs:* Having been filled with spiritual waters, O Martyrs, ye were shown forth as rivers of living water by the divine beckoning, and through the Spirit ye dried up the turbid streams of deceit, O Christ, and gave drink to the minds of the faithful.[^354]

*For the Martyrs:* Having mightily struggled, the divine Martyrs endured the torment of fire and sword and all cruel things. By their entreaties, O Word of God, deliver from the greatest eternal torments those who praise Thee in faith.[^355]

*Theotokion:* Born of the Father without generation, the Son took upon Himself a temporal birth, being incarnate of thee, desiring as the Compassionate One to take away the ancient warfare of earthborn men, being Himself above days and years.[^356]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos the same:*
*Troparion 1:* The Thrones, first filling the beauty, and the Cherubim and Seraphim, shine directly with divine rays, receiving the God-working sacred hierarchies, singing in hymnody: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.[^357]

*Troparion 2:* The Seraphim praise the threefold Unity of the Godhead with Triadic radiances, revealing with unceasing voices the most pure mystery of theology, and instructing in the orthodox faith.[^358]

*Troparion 3:* Reverently receiving the outpouring of wisdom and the richness of understanding of the solitary and simple self-subsistent Wisdom, the Cherubim grant them in a God-imitating manner unto the rest, emitting illumination unto them.[^359]

*Theotokion:* He Who is understood to be higher than every creature, working miracles in truth upon earth through His life-originating creation beyond understanding, by His efficacious brightnesses dwelt within thy pure womb, O All-pure One.[^360]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Christ, Who didst enlighten with the radiance of Thy coming, and didst illumine the ends of the world by Thy Cross: enlighten with the light of Thy divine knowledge the hearts of those who praise Thee in orthodox fashion.[^361]

*Troparion:* I have fallen into the corruption of passions, and I fear Thy righteous judgment seat, O righteous Lord. Therefore I pray unto Thee: strengthen me to do good works which justify me.[^362]

*Troparion:* The unknown and secret things of my heart Thou knowest, O my God, and Creator, and Lord! Condemn not...[^363]

*Troparion 2 (continued):* ...me then in the hour of judgment, when Thou comest to judge all things.[^364]

*For the Martyrs:* Having partaken of fire, the holy ones showed the kindling of their divine love. Therefore, bedewed with the expectation of future good things, the God-bearing ones rejoiced.[^365]

*For the Martyrs:* Strengthened by the hope of good things, the Martyrs endured the tearing of their limbs most patiently, and with the sinews of their unslackened endurance they strangled the multi-wiled one.[^366]

*Theotokion:* Eloquent lips cannot tell the ineffable mystery of thy childbearing, O Bride of God! For thou givest birth unto the Incomprehensible One, and carriest in thine arms Him Who holds all things in His hand.[^367]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos the same:*
*Troparion 1:* Kindled with divine love, the Dominions, Authorities, and Powers, the secondary ranks, with unceasing lips sing praise unto the God-originating one Essence and Power. *(Twice)*[^368]

*Troparion 2:* The Archangelic ranks, and those of the Angels and Principalities, with countless hosts, are governed by the Spirit; and they are brightly taught to revere the one Tri-hypostatic illuminating Essence.[^369]

*Theotokion:* Thou wast adorned above all angelic beauty, having conceived the Creator and Lord of Angels, Who became incarnate, O most pure Mother of God, all-immaculate.[^370]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* The lowest abyss hath surrounded us, and there is none to deliver; we are accounted as sheep for the slaughter; save Thy people, O our God! For Thou art the strength of the ailing, and their setting-aright.[^371]

*Troparion:* Being the Physician, O Christ, heal the passions of my heart, and wash me from every defilement with the streams of my compunction, O my Jesus, that I may sing and magnify Thy tender compassion.[^372]

*Troparion:* As I stray upon the paths of perdition, and fall into the pit of sins, turn me back, O Christ, and lead me unto the unerring paths of Thy precious ordinances, that I may glorify Thee.[^373]

*For the Martyrs:* Being pelted with stones, the truly precious stones did not deny the Living Stone, nor unto graven stones did they...[^374]

*For the Martyrs (continued):* ...sacrifice, the glorious and crown-bearing martyrs of the Lord.[^375]

*For the Martyrs:* Having renewed their souls with the plow of faith, the Martyrs, through the divine Spirit, brought forth a hundredfold ear of suffering, and were deemed worthy of blessed sustenance.[^376]

*Theotokion:* Thou wast shown forth, O All-immaculate One, having given birth unto the Fire of the fiery ministers; and in primary fashion excelling all creation, O most pure Virgin, blessed among women, rejoicing in God.[^377]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos the same:*
*Troparion 1:* The ranks of the bodiless ones, O Lord, standing before the throne of Thy glory, O Lover of mankind, honor Thee with unceasing angelic voices; for Thou art their strength, O Christ, and their song. *(Twice)*[^378]

*Troparion 2:* Beholding Thy face, O Angels—the unsearchable beauty, the super-divine loveliness of Thy divine brightness—they are enlightened; for Thou art both their Light and their rejoicing.[^379]

*Theotokion:* He Who before was the bodiless Word became incarnate of thee, O Most Pure One! He Who makes all things by His will, and Who brought the hosts of the bodiless from non-existence, as the Almighty.[^380]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* We the faithful discern thee, O Theotokos, to be a spiritual furnace; for even as the Supremely Exalted One saved the three children, so wholly within thy womb He renewed the world: the praised and supremely glorified God of the fathers.[^381]

*Troparion:* In ancient times Daniel tamed lions, having virtue as his companion. Emulate him, O soul; and by ever looking unto God, make inactive the one who ever roars as a lion and desires to seize thee.[^382]

*Troparion:* Through the great excess of prodigality, O Lord, I have grievously defiled my soul. Possessing surpassing goodness, O Christ, receive me as the prodigal, and have compassion on me who sing: O praised and supremely glorified God of the fathers![^383]

*For the Martyrs:* Powerfully strengthened by the law of Christ, the invincible Martyrs destroyed the counsels of the lawless, and having lawfully finished their course, they sang: Blessed art Thou, O praised God of our fathers![^384]

*For the Martyrs:* O God-illumined Martyrs of the Lord, kindled with the light of the Trinity, ye manifestly destroyed the darkness of torments and the mist of error, singing: O praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers![^385]

*Theotokion:* The laws of nature are renewed in thee; for beyond the laws of the flesh thou givest birth unto Christ the Lawgiver, O All-immaculate One, Who statuts deliverance for all who sing: O praised and supremely glorified God of the fathers![^386]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos the same:*
*Troparion 1:* Thou art the Unoriginate Light, O Master, Who didst shine forth from the Father of lights; Thou didst establish the hosts of Angels as lights, mirrors receiving Thine unsetting radiance: O praised and supremely glorified God of the fathers![^387]

*Troparion 2:* As the Lord of all, Thou manifestly savest the human race through angelic appointments; for these Thou hast appointed over all who believe and who in orthodox fashion sing unto Thee, the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^388]

*Troparion 3:* Tongue and mind cannot utter Thy wonders, O Master, and the splendor of Thy works; for Thou hast enlightened every embellishment of the heavenly powers: O praised and supremely glorified God of the fathers![^389]

*Theotokion:* Of thee, O Pure One, the Son became incarnate, Who before was without mother from the Father, and without father in that which pertains unto us, having become man for our sake; Whom the ranks of the bodiless now serve as the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^390]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* In the furnace, the children of Israel, as in a crucible, shone more brightly than gold with the beauty of piety, saying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^391]

*Troparion:* O Christ, my tender-hearted Redeemer! Deliver me from the sinful darkness and from all temptations that now hold me fast, as I cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^392]

*Troparion:* When Thou shalt come in glory, O Christ, to judge the world, number me also among the standing of Thine elect, as I cry aloud and say: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^393]

*For the Martyrs:* Ye passed over unto the land of Christ, O holy Martyrs; for having mightily struggled upon earth, ye received the heavenly life, singing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^394]

*For the Martyrs:* Having stripped off the corruptible body, into the garment of immortality as...[^395]

*For the Martyrs (continued):* ...victorious Martyrs, ye were clothed in Christ, crying from on high: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^396]

*Theotokion:* Sacred voices from afar foretold, O Most Pure One, that thou shouldest be the Mother of God Who made all things; unto Whom we sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^397]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos the same:*
*Troparion 1:* Emulating the angelic hosts enlightened and brought near to light by the beams of three-sun beauty, let us faithful sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^398]

*Troparion 2:* As the fountain of all good things, the supremely God-originating divine Power brings forth the secondary lights, who receive the First Light and cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^399]

*Troparion 3:* The Primary Mind and Creator supersubstantially established for Himself the transcendent angelic minds, who manifestly draw near and cry: Sing unto the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^400]

*Theotokion:* Him Who was begotten of the Father before the ages beyond speech, thou didst ineffably bear incarnate for us, O all-immaculate Virgin! Unto Whom we cry: Sing unto the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^401]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* The burning bush showed forth unburnt an image of thy pure childbearing; and we pray thee now to quench the raging furnace of temptations that assaults us, that we may unceasingly magnify thee, O Theotokos.[^402]

*Troparion:* Even as the Canaanite woman I cry unto Thee: Have mercy on me, O Word! For I have a soul suffering distress through the assaults of demons, and senselessly working lawless deeds, and having no feeling of Thy divine fear, O Long-suffering One.[^403]

*Troparion:* Set the feet of my soul, O Lord, upon the rock of Thy commandments; and trip up the serpent that shamelessly desires to trip me up, and deliver me from his evil-doing, for Thou art good and of great mercy.[^404]

*For the Martyrs:* Having already crossed over the surging water of temptations and the storm of cruel wounds, O Martyrs, ye have manifestly reached the haven of the heavenly kingdom, enjoying divine tranquillity.[^405]

*For the Martyrs:* Having become luminous, O Martyrs, ye were deemed worthy of the unwaning Light; and ye dwell in the church of the firstborn, and rejoice with the angelic choirs; with them pray unto the Giver of life on our behalf.[^406]

*Theotokion:* Thou carriest Him Who bears all things, and thou sucklest Him Who gives food unto all. Great and dreadful beyond understanding is thy mystery, O ark of precious holiness, Virgin Theotokos! Therefore in faith we call thee blessed.[^407]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos the same:*
*Troparion 1:* Thou didst establish the bodiless minds, O Savior, as partakers of Thine ineffable glory; and now through them preserve Thy people who flee unto Thee in faith and love, that we may unceasingly magnify Thee, O Master.[^408]

*Troparion 2:* O Almighty Ruler, Thou sendest an angel of peace to preserve Thy flock; for Thou art the Author of peace and love; one who guards the God-wise faith, and through Thy power destroys all heresies.[^409]

*Troparion 3:* O Thou Who art praised as altogether sweetness, plant in Thy churches, O Master, heavenly sweet radiance, and grant well-ordered stability, that we may unceasingly magnify Thee, the Savior.[^410]

*Theotokion:* The angelic ranks, O Most Pure One, now unceasingly honor thy childbearing; for standing in their order and beholding it, they are filled with His gladness, and unceasingly magnify thee as the Theotokos.[^411]

*Then: It is truly meet:*
*The little litany, and the Exapostilarion.*
*Then the usual psalms.*

*At the Aposticha:*
*Penitential Stichera, Tone 1:*

*Sticheron 1:* Another world awaits thee, O soul, and the Judge is about to expose thy secret and grievous deeds. Abide not therefore in the things here below, but anticipate the end, crying unto the Judge: O God, cleanse me and save me![^412]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad. In all our days let us be glad for the days wherein Thou didst humble us, for the years wherein we saw evils. And look upon Thy servants and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^413]

*Sticheron 2:* Despise me not, O my Savior, who am held fast by sinful slothfulness; raise up my thoughts unto repentance, and show me forth an approved laborer of Thy vineyard...[^414]

*Sticheron 2 (continued):* ...grant unto me the reward of the eleventh hour, and great mercy.[^415]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct Thou the works of our hands.*[^416]

*For the Martyrs:* Come, all ye peoples, let us honor with spiritual hymns and songs the passion-bearers of Christ, the luminaries of the world and preachers of the faith, the ever-flowing fountain from which healings pour forth unto the faithful. By their supplications, O Christ our God, grant peace unto Thy world, and unto our souls great mercy.[^417]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Joy of the celestial ranks" (Нб҃ныхъ чинѡ́въ):*
Holier than all the holy powers, more honorable than all creation, O Theotokos, Lady of the world: save us who have given birth unto the Savior, from myriad transgressions and afflictions, as thou art good, by thy prayers.[^418]

*Then: It is good to give praise: Trisagion. After the Our Father: troparion to the Saint, and litany and dismissal. First Hour, and the final dismissal.*[^419]

*On Monday.*
*The Beatitudes, Tone 1:*
*Refrain:* By food the enemy led Adam out of Paradise, but by the Cross Christ led the thief into it: Remember me, O Lord, when Thou comest into Thy kingdom.[^420]

*Troparion 1:* Grant me a fountain of compunction, O Christ God, through Thy tender-hearted compassion, cleansing me from every defilement of boundless evils, and make me a partaker of Thy kingdom, O Benefactor.[^421]

*Troparion 2:* We bring before Thee the ranks of Thine Angels in supplication, O Christ! Save and have compassion on us through them, as Thou art supremely good, overlooking all our transgressions both in knowledge and in ignorance.[^422]

*For the Martyrs:* By the streams of your blood, O holy ones, ye manifestly drowned the spiritual Pharaoh; and now ye pour forth streams of wonders, drying up the deep of infirmities; therefore ye are called blessed.[^423]

*Glory... Triadikon:* Let us worship the Father, let us glorify the Son, and together let all we faithful sing praise unto the All-holy Spirit, crying out and saying: O All-holy Trinity, save us all![^424]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Having given birth unto the timeless Light, guide unto the light my soul, ever darkened by the assaults of demons, O All-immaculate One, and deliver me from the coming fire by thine intercessions.[^425]

## Tuesday: The Holy Forerunner[^426]

*On Monday Evening:*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera. Tone 1:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "O all-lauded martyrs" (Прехва́льнїи мꙋ́ченици):*

*Sticheron 1:* All my life have I shamefully squandered, O Lord, wretched among harlots; like the prodigal I cry in compunction: O Heavenly Father, I have sinned, cleanse and save me, and cast me not away, who have estranged myself from Thee and am now become destitute on account of unfruitful works.[^427]

*Sticheron 2:* Having become poor, though Thou art rich, O Christ, Thou hast enriched men with immortality and brightness; therefore enrich me with virtues, who have become impoverished through worldly pleasures, and appoint me with poor Lazarus, delivering me from the torment of the rich man and from the Gehenna that lies before me.[^428]

*Sticheron 3:* With wickedness was I grievously enriched, and I loved delicacies, and received good things in life, O Lord, and was condemned unto Gehenna; my hungry mind, like Lazarus cast down before the gates of Thy divine deeds, have I neglected; have compassion on me, O Lord![^429]

*Other Stichera, to the Forerunner.*
*Tone and Podoben the same:*

*Sticheron 1:* O marvelous Prophet, Baptist and Forerunner of Christ! By thy prayers, I beseech thee, cause my heart, which hath dried up through unseemliness, to pour forth rivers of ever-flowing tears, that being saved through thee, though full of grief, I may magnify Him Who hath magnified thee.[^430]

*Sticheron 2:* O blessed Prophet, who didst baptize Christ Jesus Who takes away the abyss of the world's transgressions: upon thee do I place all my hope and the life of my expectation. Entreat Him, O John, I pray thee, to cleanse my heart and save me.[^431]

*Sticheron 3:* O wise preacher of grace, who didst foretell repentance unto all nations: grant unto my all-wretched and blinded soul, O divine Forerunner, to abide in repentance and ever to do the will of the Lord, that in faith and love I may glorify thee.[^432]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Pray with the Lamb, O Virgin, Who takes away the sins of the whole world, Whom the most great Forerunner preached unto all, that in the hour of judgment He may show me a stranger to the portion of the goats, and unite me, the unworthy one, unto His right-hand sheep.[^433]

*Then: O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon:*
*The Lord will hear me. Verse: When I call upon Him.*[^434]

*And the litany. Thereafter: Vouchsafe, O Lord, in this evening:*[^435]

*At the Aposticha:*
*Penitential Stichera, Tone 1:*

*Sticheron 1:* As a great abyss are my transgressions, O Savior, and I am grievously sinking through my sins; stretch forth Thy hand unto me as unto Peter, and save me.[^436]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee Who dwellest in heaven. Behold, as the eyes of servants look unto the hands of their masters, as the eyes of the maiden look unto the hands of her mistress: so do our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have compassion on us.*[^437]

*Sticheron 2:* Because in wicked thoughts and in deeds I have been condemned, O Savior, grant me the thought of turning back, O God, that I may cry unto Thee: Save me, O good Benefactor, and have mercy on me.[^438]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are greatly filled with contempt. Our soul is filled exceedingly with the reproach of the proud.*[^439]

*For the Martyrs:* Your confession before the judgment seat, O holy ones, spat upon the power of the demons, and delivered men from delusion; therefore also, as your heads were being severed, ye cried: Let the sacrifice of our souls, O Lord, be acceptable before Thee, for having loved Thee, we took no account of temporal life, O Lover of mankind![^440]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O all-lauded Virgin! In thee Moses with prophetic eyes beheld the mystery—the bush burning and unconsumed; for the fire of the Godhead did not burn thy womb, O Pure One. Therefore we pray thee, as the Mother of our God, to ask peace for the world, and great mercy.[^441]

*Then: Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion. After the Our Father: troparion, and dismissal.*[^442]

*On Monday at Compline.*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 1. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Let us all sing a triumphant song unto God, Who hath wrought marvelous wonders with His high arm, and saved Israel, for He hath been glorified.[^443]

*Refrain: Most holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^444]

*Troparion 1:* Rejoice, O all-pure sanctuary, Virgin, embellishment of Angels, and receptacle of the Spirit, undefiled bride of the unoriginate Father, and Mother of the Word of God![^445]

*Troparion 2:* Thou art Queen, as the King's daughter having blossomed forth, O Theotokos and Lady, and having given birth in the flesh unto the King and God, and thou reignest with Him.[^446]

*Glory...*[^447]

*Troparion 3:* David the chanter sings of thy childbearing, and Isaiah extols thy most pure womb, O all-pure Mary! And Christian people glorify thy childbearing.[^448]

*Both now... Theotokion:* The Church proclaims thy childbearing, O Virgin Bride of God, with orthodox teachings and divine realities; for it venerates the image of the incarnation of thy Son.[^449]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* The stone which the builders rejected, the same is become the head of the corner. This is the stone upon which Christ established the Church, which He redeemed from the nations.[^450]

*Troparion 1:* Possessing the glory of virginity, thou wast clothed with the grace of the Holy Spirit, O most pure Mary, shown forth as the Theotokos; a new and hidden mystery: for how, having given birth unto God in the flesh, dost thou remain a virgin?[^451]

*Troparion 2:* Who shall recount the work which Thou hast wrought, O Lord? For of her who was cursed to bring forth children in sorrows, Thou hast shown Thyself to be the Son; and fallen womanly nature rejoices in Thee, O God, for the sake of the Theotokos.[^452]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* The tempest of transgressions is loosed for the world through thee, O most pure Mary, sole Theotokos. Therefore having thee as a haven of salvation, we the faithful praise thee, singing thy great deeds.[^453]

*Both now... Theotokion:* It was sung of old by the Prophets that thy virginity should bring forth in time the Timeless One, and the Word beyond word, One of the Trinity, Christ our God; Whose images the Church venerates, O pure Mother.[^454]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Foreseeing in the Spirit the incarnation of the Word, O Prophet Habakkuk, thou didst proclaim, crying aloud: When the years draw near, Thou shalt be known; when the time is come, Thou shalt be shown forth. Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^455]

*Troparion 1:* The seven-light mirror of the divine Prophets in manifold ways sings of thy foreshadowings; and we, having in deed received the signs of thine outcome, seeing by realities that thou art truly the Theotokos, believe.[^456]

*Troparion 2:* Worthily honoring thee in truth as the Mother of God, O pure Virgin, we glorify thee, bringing unto thee our proper voice, who was proclaimed in the Law and the Prophets: Rejoice, thou that art full of grace, the Lord is with thee![^457]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Behold the divine mountain which Habakkuk beheld, covered with the virtues...

*Troparion 3 (continued):* ...manifestly overshadowing, the Theotokos hath shone forth in the world; through whom we who of old had fallen away through transgression were immediately brought near unto God.[^458]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Blessed is the tongue, O all-lauded and Pure One, which hath been deemed worthy to honor thy childbearing faithfully with orthodoxy, teaching, and works, truly manifesting all the images of the incarnation of thy Son.[^459]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Rising early from the night, we hymn Thee, O Christ God, Who becamest poor for our sake, and didst endure the Cross and death in the flesh.[^460]

*Troparion 1:* Mystically in secret did Moses show thee forth as the unburned bush, O Lady Theotokos! For thy material womb was not consumed by the Godhead.[^461]

*Troparion 2:* Hymning thee as the Mother of the Word of God, O pure Theotokos, we offer unto thee a blossoming spiritual gift, wherein we believe that we rejoice.[^462]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Turn not away praise from defiled lips, O most pure Theotokos! For there is no one on earth who dares to sing of thee worthily.[^463]

*Both now... Theotokion:* He Who was born of the Virgin is One in two essences: as invisible God, and as man Who willingly for our sake became like unto men.[^464]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Imitating the Prophet Jonah, I cry aloud: Deliver my life from corruption, O Good One, and save me, O Savior of the world, who cry: Glory to Thee![^465]

*Troparion 1:* The Prophet beholds thee as the spiritual seven-branched lampstand, O Virgin, manifestly showing forth the operations of the Spirit shining within thee.[^466]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast been shown forth as a tree, O Virgin, truly life-bearing, which by thy Fruit put to death the deceitful serpent, and brought forth life unto men, Christ God.[^467]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Uncomely praise is offered unto thee from defiled lips, O pure Mother of our God! Yet look upon the desire of my soul, and accept this.[^468]

*Both now... Theotokion:* The lawless rage in the tumult of bitter unbelief, whenever they merely see the assemblies of the Orthodox kissing thine image, O Virgin.[^469]

*Lord, have mercy (40 times). Glory... Both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 1, Podoben: "Thy tomb, O Savior" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*
Pilot my wretched life, O Pure One, and have compassion, O All-immaculate One, upon my soul that hath slipped...[^470]

*Sessional Hymn (continued):* ...through many transgressions into the abyss of perdition; and in the hour of death deliver me from the slandering demons and from the dreadful reckoning.[^471]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The fire touched not nor troubled Thy youths in the furnace, O Savior; then the three, as with one mouth, sang and blessed, saying: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^472]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast shown forth exceedingly adorned with virtues, O Bride of God, with the glory and honor of virginity; most comely indeed is thy beauty in its majesty; truly the Lord, as Lover, took thee unto Himself as His divine Mother.[^473]

*Troparion 2:* Manifestly thou wast the rod of uprightness, O Pure One, the most honorable rod of the kingdom; therefore thou didst bud forth the Fruit of the Word of God, which marvel the rod of Aaron clearly prefigured from afar.[^474]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Having defiled body, word, and soul with many bodily passions, how shall I sing of the beauty of thy virtues, O Pure One? I am at a loss and am terrified; but do thou thyself become my help through thy prayers.[^475]

*Both now... Theotokion:* I honor the union of Thine incarnation, O Christ, and in a manner becoming God I venerate the image thereof; for being the eternal God, Thou becamest unchangeable man from the Virgin, Himself perfect in both.[^476]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him before Whom the Angels and all the hosts tremble as Creator and Lord: praise, ye priests; glorify, ye youths; bless, ye people; and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^477]

*Troparion 1:* Let the divine hierurgy of God be praised, whereby the world was delivered from corruption; arise, all ye who of old were fallen; for Mary hath given birth unto Christ the Salvation.[^478]

*Troparion 2:* We hymn thee, O Most Pure One and Mother of God! And who does not glorify thee who art truly good, being the hope of our souls? Therefore, O Most Holy One, accept my supplication.[^479]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Be radiant today with hymns, ye faithful; glorify, ye Angels and men together; for the devil falls, and despair; for Mary hath given birth unto our hope of salvation.[^480]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Circumscribe not the Godhead, lie not, ye blind! For It is simply invisible and unseeable; but depicting the image of the flesh, I venerate it, and in faith I glorify the Virgin who gave birth unto the Lord.[^481]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* The luminous cloud, into which the Master of all descended as rain from heaven upon the fleece, and was incarnate for our sake, the Unoriginate becoming man: her do we all magnify as the pure Mother of our God.[^482]

*Troparion 1:* Rejoice, O Mary, great wonder of all creation! Rejoice, daughter indeed of David, yet Mother of the Lord! Rejoice, song of Gabriel! Rejoice, refuge and confirmation, and help unto all sinners upon earth, O Most Holy One![^483]

*Troparion 2:* In the divine Spirit, marvelous Joachim and divine Anna sing and raise a thanksgiving hymn, as seeing upon earth the Mother of the Creator, the daughter Mary whom they brought forth; by whose prayers do Thou save us all as God.[^484]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* O most holy Virgin, Mother of God the Most High! Thy childbearing upon earth hath appeared as salvation unto sinners and the lowly; for through thee were we saved having believed. But do thou save me both now, and unto all the ages of my life.[^485]

*Both now... Theotokion:* I know the Word to be incarnate, yet unchangeable; therefore I hymn Him faithfully, in two essences indeed, but in one Hypostasis. Truly proclaiming Him as God and man, I complete my song.[^486]

*Then: It is truly meet: Trisagion, and the rest as usual, and the dismissal.*[^487]

*On Tuesday, at Matins.*
*After the first stichology, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 1:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Conceived in iniquities, I, the prodigal, dare not look up unto the height of heaven; but relying on Thy love for mankind, I cry aloud: O God, cleanse me, a sinner, and save me![^488]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath [LXX Ps. 6:2].*

*Sessional Hymn 2:* If the righteous scarcely be saved, where shall I appear, a sinner, who have not borne the burden and heat of the day? But number me with the laborers of the eleventh hour, O God, and save me![^489]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Having acquired the Theotokos Mary as an unbreakable wall, come, ye faithful, let us fall down before her; for she hath boldness toward Him Who was born of her, to pray and deliver our souls from wrath and death.[^490]

*After the second stichology, Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:*
Hasten to open Fatherly embraces unto me; prodigally have I squandered my life, looking upon the inexhaustible wealth of Thy compassions, O Savior. Now despise not my impoverished heart, for in compunction I cry unto Thee, O Lord: Father, I have sinned against heaven and before Thee; save me![^491]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger [LXX Ps. 6:2].*

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Beholding the wealth of Job's virtues, the enemy of the righteous plotted to dig through and steal, and he pulled down the bodily pillar, yet he stole not the spiritual treasure; for he found the soul of the blameless one armed; but me he stripped and took captive. Forestall him therefore before the end, deliver me from the deceitful one, O Savior, and save me![^492]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel [LXX Ps. 67:36].*

*For the Martyrs:* As good soldiers, believing with one mind, ye feared not the threats of the tyrants, O holy ones. Ye came zealously unto Christ, taking up the precious Cross, and having finished your course, ye received victory from heaven. Glory to Him Who strengthened you! Glory to Him Who crowned you! Glory to Him Who works healings for all through you![^493]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Thou didst forestall the despair of the Ninevites, averting the foretold rebuke, and Thy mercy overcame wrath, O Lord. Have compassion now upon Thy people and Thy flock; cast down our enemies with Thy mighty hand, through the prayers of the Theotokos granting us Thy mercy.[^494]

*After the third stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 1:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Thy tomb, O Savior" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* I have fallen among thieves through my thoughts, and have been robbed in mind; and they have wounded my soul with sins, and I lie unhealed, O most merciful Jesus. But pour out Thy mercy through the prayers of the Forerunner, and heal the grievous wounds of my soul.[^495]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* O John, who of old didst baptize with the streams of Jordan the Cleansing of the whole world: pull me back as I am submerged by many transgressions, and wash me from every defilement, ever entreating the Lover of mankind as an acceptable mediator.[^496]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Leaping in his mother's womb, O Pure One, John recognized by divine grace God carried within thy womb, and faithfully worshipped. I beseech thee therefore, O Virgin: with the Forerunner entreat the Word Whom thou didst incarnate, to save thy servants.[^497]

*Penitential Canon.*
*Whose acrostic is: Receive, O Christ, the supplication of my words. A composition of Joseph.*[^498]
*Tone 1. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Let us all sing a triumphant song unto God, Who hath wrought marvelous wonders with His high arm, and saved Israel; for He hath been glorified.[^499]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*

*Troparion 1:* Being enslaved by the passions of sin, I fall down before Thee, O Lord, that Thou mayest show me free from them, diligently glorifying Thy goodness.[^500]

*Troparion 2:* I, wretched, have been wounded with the spear of sin and have died, and the enemy rejoices seeing me lying fallen. O Thou Who raisest up the dead, give me life and save me![^501]

*For the Martyrs. Refrain: Holy Martyrs, pray to God for us.*

*Troparion 3:* The choirs of the sufferers were glorified, having glorified with their honorable members Christ Who bore the flesh, and Who through His passions destroyed corruption.[^502]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* The glorious Martyrs, being pillars and ramparts of piety, remained unshaken by the assaults of the enemy. By their prayers, O God, have compassion on us all.[^503]

*Theotokion:* As a fiery throne, O Virgin, thou bearest the Creator; as an animate bridal chamber and beautiful palace, thou containest the King, Who became that which we are, without change or confusion.[^504]

*Another Canon to the Holy Great Prophet, Forerunner, and Baptist John.*
*Whose acrostic is: Hear the voice of those who cry unto thee, O Blessed One.*[^505]
*Tone 1. The Heirmos the same.*
*Refrain: Holy Forerunner and Baptist of the Savior, John, pray to God for us.*

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast the voice of the Word, O Blessed One! Therefore accept our voices which we raise unto thee, O Forerunner, delivering us from evils by thy mediation.[^506]

*Troparion 2:* Having shone forth like the dawn, like the sun, thou enlightenest the ends of the earth, and dost darken the deceitful spirits; therefore drive away the darkness from our souls.[^507]

*Troparion 3:* Thou didst preach unto the dead that our Life was coming, O Forerunner! Therefore put to death the passions that slay me, and show me forth, O Blessed One, as a partaker of divine brightness.[^508]

*Theotokion:* Having alone given birth in time unto the Timeless Son being incarnate, O most pure Lady...[^509]

*Theotokion (continued):* ...heal the chronic passions of my all-wretched soul.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* The stone which the builders rejected, the same is become the head of the corner. This is the stone upon which Christ established the Church, which He redeemed from the nations.[^510]

*Troparion 1:* Behold my weakness, O Thou Who didst clothe Thyself therewith! Behold the grievous deformity of my soul, and give ear unto my voice, O most merciful Christ, and transform this unseemliness into goodly beauty, O Savior![^511]

*Troparion 2:* Save the prodigal, O Jesus! Save me who alone have transgressed Thy saving laws, and unreasonably committed every sin, and have been led away by thoughts that alienate me from Thee, O Good One![^512]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* The ranks of the bodiless, O holy ones, truly marveled at your courage: how suffering gloriously at the judgment seat, though falling in body, ye cast down all bodiless foes by divine power![^513]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Still crimson with the blood of wounds, still bathed in drops of gore, O passion-bearers, ye stood rejoicing before the Lord, the immortal King, being crowned as victors![^514]

*Theotokion:* Thou givest birth unto Him Whom the Father begat before all ages; thou nourishest the Nourisher, knowing not the trial of man. Most glorious wonder, new mystery, O Full of Grace! Therefore every soul of the faithful glorifies thee.[^515]

*Another [Canon], the Heirmos the same.*
*Troparion 1:* Sacredly budding forth from a barren and unbearing woman, O Forerunner, thou wast shown to be fruitful in divine deeds; therefore make my heart, which is utterly barren, fruitful in good things, that in faith I may ever glorify thee.[^516]

*Troparion 2:* Strengthen my heart with the heavenly Bread, O Blessed One, which is weakened by wicked thoughts, and grant me zealously to fulfill the desires of the all-compassionate God, so that with steadfast faith I may ever praise thee.[^517]

*Troparion 3:* Thou didst proclaim the Lamb Who takes away the sins of the world, O blessed Prophet! Lighten, I pray thee, the heavy burden of my sins, granting me compunction that cleanses the defilements of the passions.[^518]

*Theotokion:*[^519] The Word, coming forth from thee fully clothed in flesh, O Theotokos, clothed Himself with whole Adam. Him therefore entreat, O Theotokos, to deliver us from all manner of tribulations and from the everlasting fire.[^520]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Foreseeing in the Spirit the incarnation of the Word, O Prophet Habakkuk, thou didst proclaim, crying aloud: When the years draw near, Thou shalt be known; when the time is come, Thou shalt be shown forth. Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^521]

*Troparion 1:* The wealth which Thou gavest me, O Christ, I squandered evilly, committing unseemly deeds, and I became naked, clothed in works of dishonor; therefore I cry unto Thee: Adorn me with the first robe, making me tender with divine grace.[^522]

*Troparion 2:* I was brought low through folly, and fell grievously, and I lie upon the earth incurably ailing; raise me up, O Resurrection of the fallen, and establish my heart, O Christ, upon the rock of saving repentance.[^523]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* Emulating Christ Who was lifted up upon the Cross, receiving wounds, the passion-bearers rejoiced together, and with the showers of their blood they stilled the rain-clouds of polytheism, pouring forth streams of healings.[^524]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Having sailed dryshod through the vehemence of torments and the bitter surging, the divine sufferers, by the piloting of saving hope, hastened unto the supreme haven, filled with all joy.[^525]

*Theotokion:* The angelic hosts marveled beholding thee: how He Who is invisible of aspect became visible after our likeness from thee, O Virgin Parent of God! Him entreat to grant salvation unto all who in faith glorify thee.[^526]

*Another [Canon], the Heirmos the same.*
*Troparion 1:* Having woven for thyself a robe of salvation in bodily nakedness, O Baptist of the Lord: clothe me, I pray thee, who am stripped of every good deed, with the garment of righteousness and gladness.[^527]

*Troparion 2:* Rain down upon me a drop of salvation, O Forerunner, who am wasting away through the scorching heat of lusts, thou who didst immerse in the streams of the Jordan Jesus the Giver of life, the Torrent of delight, that I may glorify thee as is meet.[^528]

*Troparion 3:* Neither an Angel nor a mediator saved us, but the Lord Himself Who came upon earth, for Whom thou didst make straight the paths, O Blessed One! Entreat Him now to show me the pathway leading unto the kingdom.[^529]

*Theotokion:* Thou hast been shown forth as a sanctified temple of God, Who beyond understanding dwelt within thee, O Virgin; entreat Him to cleanse us from sinful defilements, that we may be shown forth as a temple and dwelling-place of the Divine Spirit.[^530]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Grant us Thy peace, O Son of God! For we know no other God beside Thee; we name Thy name, for Thou art the God of the living and of the dead.[^531]

*Troparion 1:* I am filled with many and grievous fallings into sin, O Master compassionate and longsuffering! Have compassion on me, the condemned, and cast me not away from Thy presence.[^532]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst justify the publican who sighed, O Christ; imitating him, therefore, I beat upon my breast and cry unto Thee: Cleanse me, O Thou Who alone art tender-hearted and compassionate![^533]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* Thy saints, O Lord, were shown forth as unerring luminaries, driving away the deep night of deception, and enlightening the whole inhabited world with the brightness of wonders.[^534]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* As most precious stones, loving Thee, the living Stone, O Master, the Martyrs, rolled about by torments, cast down every structure of deception.[^535]

*Theotokion:* In two wills, O All-immaculate One, thou didst bring forth One of the Most Holy Trinity, bearing one Hypostasis; Him diligently entreat, that we all may be saved.[^536]

*Another [Canon], the Heirmos the same.*
*Troparion 1:* Thou didst settle in the wilderness, O Forerunner of Christ, even as Elijah of old; therefore establish my heart, which hath been desolated by transgressions, through divine diligence, O All-blessed One.[^537]

*Troparion 2:* Having become an immovable pillar and rampart of the faithful, O great Forerunner of Christ: show my thought, which is shaken by the wiles of the deceiver, to be immovable.[^538]

*Troparion 3:* Unto the dead was the Light proclaimed, Which shone forth from Light upon earth, by thy voice, O Blessed One, and we who sit in darkness were enlightened...[^539]

*Troparion 3 (continued):* ...therefore enlighten me who am grievously darkened.

*Theotokion:* David proclaims thee as Queen, a Virgin pure, O Most Pure One; therefore I pray thee: Make me an heir of the heavenly kingdom, that I may call thee blessed.[^540]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Imitating the Prophet Jonah, I cry aloud: Deliver my life from corruption, O Good One, and save me, O Savior of the world, who cry: Glory to Thee![^541]

*Troparion 1:* The bones of my wretched soul have been crushed, and I have become weakened by the weight of many pleasures; but help me, O Christ, sole Help of all![^542]

*Troparion 2:* I have fallen into the deep of grievous things and the tempest of passions; I cry unto Thee, O all-powerful Christ: Lead me up with Thy mighty hand, and save me![^543]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* The multitude of chosen ones by myriads, of holy passion-bearers, trampled underfoot myriads of noetic foes, and were united unto the myriads of heavenly minds.[^544]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Having dried up the deep of godlessness, O sufferers, ye inherited the torrent of delight; therefore dry up the flood of my sin![^545]

*Theotokion:* Manifested as the tabernacle of consecration, O Mary: sanctify my wretched soul alone defiled by pleasures, and make me a partaker of divine glory.[^546]

*Another [Canon], the Heirmos the same.*
*Troparion 1:* O Baptist, who didst preach repentance upon earth: show me the paths of repentance guiding me unto the light, and deliver me from the abysses of deception.[^547]

*Troparion 2:* Immersing the Abyss in river streams, dry up the abyss of my passions, granting me a fountain of tears, O Forerunner, preacher of Christ![^548]

*Troparion 3:* From spiritual infirmities, and bodily passions, life's adversities, and all manner of temptations and sorrows: save me, O Forerunner of the Lord![^549]

*Theotokion:* As the Mother of God and goodly among women, I pray thee diligently: Despise me not, O Pure One, but have compassion, and preserve me whole from all harm.[^550]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The fire touched not nor troubled Thy youths in the furnace, O Savior; then the three, as with one mouth, sang and blessed, saying: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^551]

*Troparion 1:* Job was accustomed unto patience, and remained unmoved by all the assaults of the deceitful one, even as a valiant pillar. Emulate him always, O my soul, and despond not at all in calamities.[^552]

*Troparion 2:* I was vanquished by bodily pleasures, and became irrational, being endowed with reason. O Word of God, Who by a word didst save the harlot: save me, the wretched one, that I may sing, blessing Thy goodness.[^553]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* Redeemed by the Blood of Him Who suffered in the flesh for our sake, O wise Martyrs, for Whose sake ye willingly resolved to pour out your blood: therefore ye reign with Him unceasingly.[^554]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* By the building up of sacred sufferings, ye were shown forth as a palace, O wise ones, wherein Christ hath joyfully made His rest, the one King and Lord, Who hath brought you unto heavenly mansions.[^555]

*Theotokion:* Out of all generations Christ chose thee alone for Himself, O All-praised One, as an honorable and pure dwelling place; and having shone forth from thee like the sun, He enlightened all the earth.[^556]

*Another [Canon], the Heirmos the same.*
*Troparion 1:* Having become the most eminent and glorious mediator between the Old and the New, O Baptist: intercede to renew me who am wholly grown old through sins, entreating Jesus, the Renewer of all things.[^557]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast shown forth upon earth legislating repentance, O Forerunner of Christ and Baptist! Strengthen all to accomplish it by thy prayers, that we may be delivered from immeasurable evils whereby we continually sin.[^558]

*Troparion 3:* Thou hast trodden the narrow path, passing through all self-control, O wise servant, and by the breadth of radiant divine visions thou wast wholly illuminated; grant that we also may enjoy them, entreating Christ.[^559]

*Glory...*
*Triadikon:* As faithful, we glorify the Trinity in one nature: the unoriginate Father, and the Son, and the Spirit likewise; one reigning essence, one Lordship, one Principle, and the Life that quickens us.[^560]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Offer supplication unto God, O Most Pure One, that He may overlook our transgressions and countless sins, and deliver us from the tormenting eternal fire.[^561]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him before Whom the Angels and all the hosts tremble as Creator and Lord: praise, ye priests; glorify, ye children; bless, ye people; and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^562]

*Troparion 1:* The deceitful serpent by evil counsel hath made me naked of all virtues; clothe me now with the garment of virtues, O my Savior, Who didst lay bare the enemy's malice.[^563]

*Troparion 2:* Coming to judge the human race in the dreadful hour, O Judge: send not me, who am condemned, into the fire of Gehenna, but have compassion and save me.[^564]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* Neither tribulation, nor distress, nor sword, nor fire was able to separate you at all from the love of Christ, O all-lauded sufferers of the Lord, Who loved you on account of His tender-heartedness.[^565]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Wrestling in the flesh with the bodiless foe, ye cast him down, O valiant sufferers; and now ye join in choral dance with the bodiless hosts, healing the passions of souls and bodies.[^566]

*Theotokion:* Finding thee as a flower in the valleys of life, the Planter of all took up His dwelling within thee; with the fragrances of virtues and purity make us now sweet-smelling, O Mother of God.[^567]

*Another [Canon]. Heirmos:* Him Who preserved the hymn-singing children in the furnace, and transformed the thundering flame of the furnace into dew: praise Christ God, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^568]

*Troparion 1:* I alone have sinned more than all born on earth; I alone have become a transgressor of Thy laws, O Lord! Therefore for the sake of the Forerunner, have compassion and save me.[^569]

*Troparion 2:* In character thou wast shown forth as an angel, O Baptist John, preaching the Angel of Great Counsel unto the ends of the earth; therefore we glorify thee with hymns unto all ages.[^570]

*Troparion 3:* Thou wast unrighteously beheaded, O Blessed One, who didst immerse the head of Christ in the waters. Strengthen all of us by thy prayers to trample upon the truly ruinous head of the deceiver.[^571]

*Theotokion:* Deliver me from improper sins, and from burning torment, and from tormenting darkness, gnashing of teeth, and the worm, O Virgin, sole protectress of the human race.[^572]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* The luminous cloud, wherein the Master of all descended as rain from heaven upon the fleece, and was incarnate for our sake, the Unoriginate becoming man: her do we all magnify as the pure Mother of our God.[^573]

*Troparion 1:* Behold, the time of repentance and pure labor; behold the day: do bright works; flee the darkness of passions; drive away the sleep of evil despondency, O my soul, that thou mayest be a partaker of divine light.[^574]

*Troparion 2:* Like the publican I sigh, like the harlot I shed tears, and like the thief I cry unto Thee: Remember me, O Compassionate One! And like the prodigal son I call out: I have sinned! I fall down before Thee like the Canaanite woman: despise me not, O merciful Christ.[^575]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* Unto bodily passions and spiritual afflictions ye were shown forth as truly physicians, having patiently given over your bodies unto wounds and unto violent death, O all-lauded sufferers of the Lord! Therefore ye are ever called blessed.[^576]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* More than the radiance of the sun did the patience of the all-wise passion-bearers of Christ shine forth; therefore by grace it visibly destroyed the prince of darkness, and dispersed the night of godlessness, and enlightened the hearts of the faithful.[^577]

*Theotokion:* By the illuminating rays of the Word Who shone forth from thy holy womb, O Virgin Theotokos, and enlightened the ends of the earth: enlighten my soul, darkened by the blackness of pleasures and by the slothfulness of passions, that with faith I may sing of thee.[^578]

*Another [Canon], the Heirmos the same.*
*Troparion 1:* Behold the lamp shining upon those who are in the darkness of life! Behold the sweetly speaking swallow, announcing spring unto all, Christ's great Forerunner, mediator between the Old and the New; by whose prayers may we be preserved evermore.[^579]

*Troparion 2:* Having a multitude of sins, I present thee now as an intercessor, as the friend of the Bridegroom, and cry unto thee, O Baptist: Grant me forgiveness of debts, O All-blessed One, and rekindle the lamp of my soul, utterly extinguished by slothfulness.[^580]

*Troparion 3:* With the bodiless Angels, with the sacred passion-bearers, and with the Prophets, O Forerunner: ever entreat the all-good God that we may obtain eternal good things, having been continually enriched with thee as a good defender...[^581]

*Troparion 4:* O fair swallow, honorable nightingale, all-good dove, wilderness-loving turtledove, Baptist of the Lord, offshoot of the wilderness! Show my soul, desolated by unfruitfulness, to be fruitful in good things.[^582]

*Theotokion:* Thou bearest Him Who bears all things, even as the Cherubic Throne, and thou nourishest Him Who nourishes us; Him unceasingly entreat, O pure and joy-bestowed Lady, that thy flock may ever be delivered from earthquake, pestilence, captivity, and every distress.[^583]

*Then: It is truly meet: and a bow. Small litany. Exapostilarion, and the usual psalms.*[^584]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 1:*

*Sticheron 1:* Another age awaits thee, O my soul, and the Judge is about to expose thy secret and grievous deeds. Abide not therefore in them, but forestall Him, crying unto the Judge: O God, cleanse me and save me![^585]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad; in all our days we were glad. For the days wherein Thou hast humbled us, for the years wherein we have seen evils. And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons [LXX Ps. 89:14-16].*[^586]

*Sticheron 2:* Despise me not, O my Savior, who am held fast by sinful slothfulness, but raise up my thought unto repentance, and show me to be an experienced laborer in Thy vineyard. Grant me the reward of the eleventh hour, and great mercy.[^587]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and prosper Thou the works of our hands upon us, yea, prosper Thou the work of our hands [LXX Ps. 89:17].*[^588]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* These soldiers of the great King opposed the commands of the tyrants; valiantly they were unconcerned about torments, and having trampled upon all delusion, they were worthily crowned, and they ask of the Savior peace for our souls, and great mercy.[^589]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Thou alone, O Pure One, wast the dwelling place of the Light Who shone forth from the Father; therefore I cry unto thee: Enlighten with the light of virtues my soul, darkened by passions, and settle me in the mansions of thy brightness in the day of judgment, O Most Pure One.[^590]

*Then: It is good to give thanks unto the Lord: Troparion, Litany, and Dismissal. Then the First Hour, and the usual psalms and the rest, and the final Dismissal.*[^591]

*On Tuesday, the Beatitudes, Tone 1:*

*Stanza 1:* Through eating, the enemy led Adam out of Paradise; but through the Cross, Christ led the thief into it, who cried: Remember me, O Lord, when Thou comest into Thy kingdom![^592]

*Stanza 2:* Sinning every day and transgressing Thy commandments, convert me, O God, and snatch me from torment, that I may glorify Thine ineffable compassion, O Lover of mankind.[^593]

*Stanza 3:* Thou wast shown forth as a lamp, O Forerunner of God, going before the unwaning Light Who ineffably shone forth unto us from a radiant cloud; Him therefore entreat continually, to have compassion and save our souls.[^594]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Stanza 4:* Having endured intricate torments, O all-lauded Martyrs, ye were deemed worthy of the heavenly supernal good things; therefore ye are ever called blessed by all men.[^595]

*Glory...*
*Triadikon:* In three Persons we all know one Godhead, which is indivisible: the Son and the living Spirit together with the Father, unoriginate and unconfused, abiding for ever unto all ages.[^596]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
After giving birth, O Ever-Virgin, even as before childbirth, thou wast preserved pure, having given birth unto God in the flesh; Him entreat, O Most Pure One, to deliver us from carnal and spiritual passions, we pray thee.[^597]

## Wednesday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross[^598]

*On Tuesday Evening.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 1:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "O all-lauded martyrs" (Прехва́льнїи мꙋ́ченици):*

*Sticheron 1:* Nailed unto the Cross, O Christ God, as a man, Thou didst deify human nature, and didst put to death the author of evil, the serpent; Thou didst deliver us from the curse of the tree, as Thou art tender-hearted, having become a curse and granting unto the world blessing and great mercy.[^599]

*Sticheron 2:* Being higher than all honor, O Master, Thou wast pleased to be dishonored, enduring reproachful death upon the tree, O Compassionate One, by reason whereof the human race hath found immortality and received its former life, when Thou didst die in the flesh, O Almighty One.[^600]

*Sticheron 3:* O all-honorable Cross, cleansing of all the faithful, strength of kings: sanctify all who bow down before thee and glorify Christ, Who stretched forth His most pure hands upon thee by reason of His ineffable tender-heartedness, and gathered all the ends of the earth into one.[^601]

*Other Stichera, to the Theotokos, Tone 1:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Joy of the heavenly ranks" (Нбⷭ҇ныхъ чинѡ́въ):*

*Sticheron 1:* Standing by the Cross of thy Son and God, and beholding His longsuffering, the pure Mother wept and said: Woe is me, my sweetest Child! Why dost Thou suffer these things unjustly, O Word of God, that Thou mayest save mankind?[^602]

*Sticheron 2:* Standing by the Cross, O unwedded Bride, and receiving wounds from sorrows, she cried aloud: Show me not to be childless, O my Child, who gave birth unto Thee, O Word of God! Leave me not alone, O Only-begotten, co-eternal with the Father![^603]

*Sticheron 3:* When I gave birth unto Thee, I had no sorrows at all, and how do I now endure sorrows, when Thou art unjustly crucified, O Word? the weeping Virgin cried bitterly: Woe is me! I cannot bear to see Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Lover of mankind![^604]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
When of old thou didst behold thy Son and Master stretching forth His hands upon the Cross, and His ribs pierced with a spear, O pure Mother, sobbing thou didst cry aloud: Woe is me! How dost Thou suffer, Who takest away the sufferings of men, O Lover of mankind![^605]

*Then: O Gladsome Light: After this, the Prokeimenon of the day. Vouchsafe, O Lord, to keep us this evening: And the Litany.*[^606]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 1:*

*Sticheron 1:* The Cross was planted on Calvary, and immortality blossomed forth for us from the ever-flowing fountain, the side of the Savior.[^607]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven. Behold, as the eyes of servants look unto the hands of their masters, as the eyes of the maiden unto the hands of her mistress, so do our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us [LXX Ps. 122:1-2].*[^608]

*Sticheron 2:* An unbroken wall unto us is the precious Cross of the Savior; for placing our hope thereon, we all are saved.[^609]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are exceedingly filled with contempt; especially hath our soul been filled; let the reproach be to them that are at ease, and contempt to the proud [LXX Ps. 122:3-4].*[^610]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* O fair merchandise was yours, O holy ones! For ye gave your blood and inherited the heavens; and having been tried for a season, ye rejoice eternally. Truly fair was your merchandise! For having left corruptible things, ye received incorruptible things, and joining in choral dance with the Angels, ye unceasingly sing unto the consubstantial Trinity.[^611]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Joy of the heavenly ranks" (Нбⷭ҇ныхъ чинѡ́въ):*
When she beheld the Lamb lifted up upon the Cross, the blameless Virgin wept and cried aloud: My sweet Child! What is this new and most glorious vision? How art Thou, Who holdest all things in Thy fist, nailed in the flesh upon the tree?[^612]

*Then: Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion of the Saint, and the Dismissal.*[^613]

*On Tuesday at Compline.*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 1:*

*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Thy victorious right hand hath in a manner becoming God been glorified in strength; for it, O Immortal One, being all-powerful, shattered the adversaries, fashioning a new path through the deep for the Israelites.[^614]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^615]

*Troparion 1:* He Who is incomprehensible unto the heavenly hosts, O all-blameless Maiden, took flesh from thy flesh, and renewed that which was corrupted by the first transgression.[^616]

*Troparion 2:* With hymns let us, the faithful, call blessed the Mother of the Word of God beyond word; for she, the all-immaculate one, was shown forth as an adornment unto earthly beings, and hath become an advocate for all who sin.[^617]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Thou wast shown forth more spacious than the heavens, having contained the Creator of all things, O Mother Ever-Virgin; therefore I cry unto thee: Deliver me from every death-bearing straitness, O Lady full of divine joy.[^618]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Grant us help, O Pure One, who are tempest-tossed by the tumult of afflictions; cast down the deceitful exaltations of our enemies, and grant salvation unto all who piously glorify thee.[^619]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou Who alone knowest the weakness of human nature, and didst mercifully take form therein: gird me with power from on high, that I may cry unto Thee: Holy is the living temple of Thine ineffable glory, O Lover of mankind![^620]

*Troparion 1:* The Full empties Himself for our sake, and the Unoriginate receives a beginning from thee, O all-blameless Virgin, and the Invisible is seen; and He Who nourishes all things is nourished with milk, providing for the renewal of men.[^621]

*Troparion 2:* Heal our affliction, O unwedded Virgin, who gavest birth unto the Healer and Lord, Who by grace hath shown us the entrance unto life; Him unceasingly entreat to have compassion and save thy servants.[^622]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* O thou who didst deify human nature beyond nature, O All-immaculate One: pray unto Him Who was born of thee, to grant forgiveness of our sins and eternal joy, O most holy and all-praised Virgin.[^623]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Having spent my life in slothfulness, I fall down before thee, O Most Pure One: by thy continual prayers unto God, raise up my mind, and wholly enlighten me who am blackened, with the brightness of repentance, that with faith and love I may glorify thee.[^624]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Beholding thee with farsighted eyes as the mountain overshadowed by the grace of God, Habakkuk proclaimed that out of thee should come forth the Holy One of Israel, for our salvation and renewal.[^625]

*Troparion 1:* We know thee as the divine palace of the Word, wherein taking up His dwelling in the flesh, He renewed us who were corrupted by passions; therefore, O Pure One, we honor thee as the Mother of God, and we glorify Him Who was born of thee.[^626]

*Troparion 2:* The incarnate Word took up His dwelling within thy womb, O Virgin, and drove away the curse which had formerly entered into human nature, and by His divine union deified it in a manner becoming God; therefore do we sing of thee.[^627]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Adam brought forth mortality, O All-immaculate One, from the time he tasted of the corruptible fruit; but now through thy childbearing he is made alive, and dwells in the mansions of Paradise; therefore as is our duty do we sing of thee.[^628]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Thou didst stem the torrent of death, having given birth unto Christ God, the Fountain of immortality, O Pure One; Him therefore entreat, O woman full of divine grace, to heal the death-bearing passions of my soul, and to save me.[^629]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Thou Who by the radiance of Thy coming didst enlighten, O Christ, and by Thy Cross didst illumine the ends of the world: enlighten with the light of the knowledge of Thee the hearts of those who orthodoxly sing of Thee.[^630]

*Troparion 1:* From thy womb shone forth the unwaning Sun, O All-immaculate One, and utterly destroyed the darkness, and made the earth radiant with divine understandings; therefore with faith do we sing of thee.[^631]

*Troparion 2:* Rejoice! we cry unto thee with the angelic voice, O Most Pure One! For thou gavest birth unto the Angel of Great Counsel, co-unoriginate with the Father, Who became flesh, O Virgin, in order to save man.[^632]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* The Lord, taking up His dwelling within thy womb, O Pure One, hath delivered from the dominion of the enemy those who piously believe in Him; therefore with a loud voice do we all sing of thee, O Most Pure One.[^633]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Having clothed Himself in me, the Master came forth from thee, O All-immaculate One, and deified corrupted nature; Him entreat, O Mother of God, to make me stripped of every sin.[^634]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* The lowest abyss hath encompassed us; there is none to deliver; we were accounted as sheep for the slaughter; save Thy people, O our God! for Thou art the strength of the weak and the restoration.[^635]

*Troparion 1:* Without seed didst thou conceive God within thy womb, and after strange childbirth thou didst remain a Virgin, O All-immaculate One! Therefore with praises do we glorify thee, who through thee, O Most Pure One, were delivered from the curse.[^636]

*Troparion 2:* The ladder which Jacob saw; the overshadowed mountain; the all-radiant cloud of divine light; the gate through which God alone passed: O blessed among women, thou art she, O All-immaculate One.[^637]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Apart from the laws of nature didst thou bring forth, O unwedded Virgin, the hypostatic Word of God Who heals our affliction; Him diligently entreat to save all of us.[^638]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Bringing my soul which is sick with transgressions, I cry unto thee, O all-holy, most glorious, and pure one: heal and save me by thine intercession, that I may sing of and magnify thy protection.[^639]

*Lord, have mercy (40 times). Glory... Both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:*
Possessing thy protection, O Most Pure One, and by thy prayers being delivered from calamities, guarded everywhere by the Cross of thy Son...[^640]

*Sessional Hymn (continued):* ...as is our duty we all piously magnify thee.[^641]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* We the faithful perceive thee, O Theotokos, as a spiritual furnace; for even as the Supremely Exalted One saved the three children, He renewed the world wholly within thy womb, the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^642]

*Troparion 1:* The shadows of the Law and the foreshadowings of the divinely speaking Prophets clearly preannounced thy seedless childbearing, O Most Pure and All-immaculate One! Singing whereof, we praise the hymned and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^643]

*Troparion 2:* Thou gavest birth unto the Dayspring of the sun, O pure woman full of divine grace, Who enlightens the fulness of all the faithful and hath brought to naught the night of impiety; therefore we honor the hymned and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^644]

*Glory...*
