*Troparion 3:* The unburned bush of old, O All-immaculate One, prefigured thy womb; for the fire of the Godhead consumed thee not at all. Therefore I pray thee: Deliver me from the unquenchable fire, that unceasingly, O Pure One, I may sing of thy marvelous deeds.[^645]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
O Mother of God, help of the faithful, cleansing of sinners, dispenser of all good things: receive me in thy merciful tender-heartedness, as I fall down and come unto thee with weeping, asking forgiveness of my sins.[^646]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* In the furnace the children of Israel, as in a crucible, shone purer than gold with the beauty of piety, saying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; praise and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^647]

*Troparion 1:* The types of the Law and the foreshadowings of old clearly predicted thee, O Virgin, as the pure Mother of the Lord; and we, now seeing the fulfillment of realities, sing of thee in harmony, and glorify thee unto all ages.[^648]

*Troparion 2:* Adorned with divine beauties, having given birth without separation in beauty unto the Word of God, O All-immaculate One, Who with divine splendors illuminates the hearts of all who in faith sing of Him unto all ages.[^649]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* With sacred voices from afar the Prophets told that thou shouldest be the Mother, O All-immaculate One, unto the Master God; unto Whom we all cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; praise and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^650]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
How fair, how all-good art thou, O Virgin, having given birth unto Him Who is fair in beauty...[^651]

...beyond cause didst give birth unto Christ, unto Whom we all cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; praise and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^652]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* The burning bush showed forth unburned a figure of thy pure childbearing; and now we pray thee to quench the furnace of temptations raging against us, that unceasingly, O Theotokos, we may magnify thee.[^653]

*Troparion 1:* The nature of earthly beings, which formerly was condemned, O Virgin, through thy supernatural and divine childbirth hath obtained immortality, and received its former beauty, singing unto thee in harmony with joyous songs.[^654]

*Troparion 2:* Thou bearest Him Who bears all things, and with milk dost nourish Him Who gives food unto all. Great and dread is thy mystery which is beyond understanding, O ark of venerable consecration, O Virgin Theotokos! Therefore do we all call thee blessed.[^655]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Unto me who lie in the darkness of slothfulness, O Most Pure One, cause light now to dawn, untying the thoughts of passions that blind me, and continually working deep peace within my soul, O All-immaculate One, that I may glorify thee.[^656]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
I am afraid of the unbribable judgment-seat and the unforgetting Judge, O All-immaculate One; for I have an unnumbered multitude of transgressions, having lived in slothfulness, all-passionate as I am; therefore, O Theotokos, take pity on me and have mercy.[^657]

*Then: It is truly meet: and a prostration. Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparia, and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*[^658]

*On Wednesday at Matins.*
*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 1:*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* When Thou wast crucified, O Christ, the tyranny perished, and the power of the enemy was trampled underfoot; for neither an Angel nor a man, but Thou Thyself, O Lord, hast saved us: glory to Thee![^659]

*Verse: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship at His footstool; for He is holy [LXX Ps. 98:5].*[^660]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* We worship the wood of Thy Cross, O Lover of mankind, for upon it Thou wast nailed, O Life of all; and Thou didst open Paradise, O Savior, unto the thief who came unto Thee in faith, and didst make him worthy of delights who confessed Thee: Remember me, O Lord! Receive even as that one...[^661]

...also us who cry: We all have sinned; in Thy tender-heartedness despise us not![^662]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
Beholding the Lamb upon the Cross crucified between two thieves, O longsuffering Word, and pierced in the ribs with a spear, the Ewe-lamb exclaimed, crying aloud with maternal love: What is this strange and terrible mystery, O my Jesus, ineffably accomplished? How art Thou covered by a tomb, O uncircumscribable God? Leave me not who gave birth unto Thee, O my sweetest Son![^663]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 1:*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Save, O Lord, Thy people, and bless Thine inheritance, granting victories unto our right-believing Emperor over adversaries, and preserving Thy commonwealth by Thy Cross.[^664]

*Verse: But God is our King before the ages; He hath wrought salvation in the midst of the earth [LXX Ps. 73:12].*[^665]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* The weapon of the Cross in battles appeared of old unto the pious King Constantine as an invincible victory over enemies by reason of faith; before this the adversary powers tremble; this hath become salvation unto the faithful, and the boast of Paul.[^666]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel [LXX Ps. 67:36].*[^667]
*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* By the sufferings of the Saints, which they endured for Thy sake, be Thou implored, O Lord, and heal all our sicknesses, O Lover of mankind, we pray Thee.[^668]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
O marvelous new wonder! O dread mystery! exclaimed the Ewe-lamb and undefiled Maiden, when she beheld the Lord stretched out upon the tree: How is He Who holds all things in His fist condemned as one condemned?[^669]

*After the 3rd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 1:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Thy tomb, O Savior" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* When the sun beheld Thee, O Word, hung upon the tree of the Cross in the flesh voluntarily for the tender-heartedness of Thy mercy, not enduring the audacity, it was darkened. Therefore illumine my darkened soul with Thine unapproachable light, and save me, I pray.[^670]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Having stretched forth Thy palms upon the Cross, O Compassionate One, Thou didst gather the nations that were far from Thee to glorify Thy great goodness; but look down upon Thine inheritance, and cast down those who make war against us, by Thy precious Cross.[^671]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
Beholding the Lamb and Shepherd hung dead upon the tree, the undefiled Ewe-lamb weeping uttered with maternal love...[^672]

...exclaiming: How shall I endure Thine ineffable condescension, O my Son, and Thy voluntary sufferings, O all-good God?[^673]

*Canon to the Precious and Life-Giving Cross, whereof the Acrostic is: By the Cross I am saved, of the Master Who suffered. (A composition of Joseph).*[^674]

*Tone 1. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Delivered from bitter slavery, Israel traversed the impassable deep as dry land; and beholding the enemy drowned, it sang a song unto God as Benefactor, Who works wonders with an high arm, for He hath been glorified.[^675]

*Verse: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*[^676]
*Troparion 1:* Lifted up upon the Cross, O Christ, Thou didst raise up fallen man with Thyself, and didst cast down all the power of the enemy, O Word. Therefore do I hymn Thy sufferings, of Thee Who didst suffer and deliver me from passions.[^677]

*Troparion 2:* Being the Lord of glory, Who didst crown man with glory, Thou wast crowned with thorns, that Thou mightest make our thorn-bearing nature fruitful, O Planter of divine deeds.[^678]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* The all-holy assembly of the Saints, having suffered lawfully, hath sanctified all creation with the streams of holy blood, and in God the Father hath destroyed the foul sacrifices offered unto demons.[^679]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* The clouds of torments hid not your patient struggles, O all-holy Martyrs; therefore, more than the rays of the sun did ye appear glorious, clearly flashing forth the beams of salvation.[^680]

*Theotokion:*
The Most Pure One was consumed by the sword of the passions of her Son, when she beheld Him pierced with a spear, cutting off the weapon that barred the way into Paradise and permitted not the divine entrance unto the faithful.[^681]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 1. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Thy victorious right hand hath in a manner becoming God been glorified in strength; for it, O Immortal One, being all-powerful, shattered the adversaries, fashioning a new path through the deep for the Israelites.[^682]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^683]
*Troparion 1:* Grant me a stream of tears from the soul, O All-immaculate One, who didst give birth unto the Fountain of forgiveness, washing away the filth of sin, and conducting my life in humility.[^684]

*Troparion 2:* Higher than creation didst thou become, O Pure Virgin, having incarnated God; therefore I pray thee: From the corruption of passions raise up...[^685]

...my soul, and lead me up unto the divine height of dispassion.[^686]

*Troparion 3:* Show thyself merciful unto me, O Pure One, on the Day of Judgment, I pray thee, delivering me from all dread standing and cruel torment, who flee under thy protection, O most pure Lady.[^687]

*Troparion 4:* Held fast by the darkness of sin and fallen, O Most Pure One, raise me up, granting me a shower of tears wherewith I may wash away my foul deeds; for thee alone do we have as protectress, O Theotokos.[^688]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Let not the mortal man boast in his wisdom and riches, but in the faith of the Lord, orthodoxly crying unto Christ God, and singing continually: Upon the rock of Thy commandments establish me, O Master.[^689]

*Verse: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*[^690]
*Troparion 1:* The lawless dug into Thy hands and feet, O my Jesus, Who with Thine ineffable palm didst first fashion man, O Christ God, and by Thy Passion didst deliver all from passions and corruption.[^691]

*Troparion 2:* Let the moon and the sun stand still, cried Joshua, prefiguring the darkening of the luminaries when the Master suffered in the flesh upon the Cross, whereby the evil principalities of darkness were put to shame.[^692]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* The glorious Martyrs zealously endured wounds, and being wounded, they mightily wounded the serpent Belial. Therefore do they continually heal the wounds of our souls by divine command.[^693]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Ye tore down the demonic shrines and temples, O Martyrs; and by sufferings and courage ye most gloriously erected yourselves through grace as temples capable of containing the Father.[^694]

*Theotokion:*
At the hour of the passions, beholding Him Who is fair in beauty having neither form nor comeliness, O Virgin, bitterly didst thou weep, O Pure One, exclaiming: Woe is me! How dost Thou suffer, O Son, Who desirest to deliver all from passions?[^695]

*Other Heirmos:* Thou Who alone knowest the weakness of human nature, and didst mercifully take form therein: gird me with power from on high, that I may cry unto Thee: Holy is the living temple of Thine ineffable glory, O Lover of mankind![^696]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast a temple, O Most Pure One, of Him Who reigns over all: me who have become a den of soul-corrupting robbers, snatch from their...[^697]

...maltreatment, and show me forth as a pure dwelling of the Holy Spirit. (Twice)[^698]

*Troparion 2:* Send me a shower of tears, drying up the streams of my evil deeds, I pray thee, who didst give birth unto the most tranquil abyss of tender compassion, Christ our God, O all-hymned Virgin Theotokos.[^699]

*Troparion 3:* Be Thou our strength and an unshakable pillar, O woman full of divine grace, and a shield and invincible weapon, driving far from us the hosts of noetic enemies, O most pure Theotokos, who honor thee with faith and love.[^700]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Of old the Prophet Habakkuk heard Thy marvelous report, O Christ, and with fear he cried: God shall come from Theman, and the Holy One from the mountain overshadowed, to save Thine anointed. Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^701]

*Troparion 1:* Wounds and stripes didst Thou endure, O Christ, healing the wounds of my heart; tasting bitter gall, Thou didst take away the harm of the sweet, corrupting taste; and nailed upon the tree, Thou didst destroy the ancient curse.[^702]

*Troparion 2:* When Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross, Thou didst draw nigh the nations that were cast far from Thee; and Thou didst reconcile us unto the Father, O Longsuffering One, placing Thyself in the midst as Mediator; and in the midst of the earth Thou didst endure reproachful suffering.[^703]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* In the sea of your blood, O divinely blessed ones, the noetic Pharaoh falling with all his hosts was mystically drowned. Therefore with joy, having been saved, ye came unto the promised land, and became citizens of heaven.[^704]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Strengthened by the Passion of Christ, the blessed ones had power to receive diverse sufferings in their venerable flesh; therefore they appease the passions of both souls and bodies of those who praise them and ever call them blessed.[^705]

*Theotokion:*
Beholding Thine unjust slaying, she who gave birth unto Thee, O Christ, sobbing cried aloud unto Thee: O righteous Judge and Son! How art Thou unjustly condemned, desiring to justify those condemned of old by transgression, who slid into corruption?[^706]

*Other Heirmos:* Perceiving thee with farsighted eyes as the mountain overshadowed by the grace of God, Habakkuk proclaimed that out of thee should come forth the Holy One of Israel, for our salvation and renewal.[^707]

*Troparion 1:* Me who am grievously drawn away from the commandments of God by demonic deceptions...[^708]

...despise not, O Good One! But have compassion, I pray thee, and show me to be higher than his deception, who flee, O Most Pure One, unto thy mercy. (Twice)[^709]

*Troparion 2:* O Thou Who alone art merciful, Christ God: by the prayers of her who gave birth unto Thee, have mercy on those who hope in Thee, and take pity, and guide us unto the light of Thy commandments, and make us worthy of eternal life.[^710]

*Troparion 3:* Asleep unto death, and lying in the tomb of despair, O Most Pure One, bound with the irons of my transgressions: by thy vigilant prayer raise me up, and direct me unto repentance, I pray thee.[^711]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Make Thine unwaning light to dawn, O Christ, in the hearts of those who faithfully hymn Thee, granting us peace which surpasses understanding; that running from the night of ignorance unto the day by Thy light, we may give glory unto Thee, O Lover of mankind.[^712]

*Troparion 1:* The sun, O Savior, was stripped of light, beholding Thee naked hanging upon the tree, Who didst suspend the earth upon the waters; and when the rocks perceived Thee lifted up, rock split upon rock through fear, and the foundations were shaken.[^713]

*Troparion 2:* Lifted up upon the tree and fastened with nails, Thou didst stain Thy fingers with blood, O Longsuffering One! And pierced in the ribs with a spear, Thou didst heal the wound of Adam, which he received by listening to a rib and disobeying Him Who made him.[^714]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* The multitude of the Martyrs appeared as Paradise, having in its midst the Tree of Life, Christ; for Whose sake having endured reproachful death with a valiant mind, by divine power they put to death the serpent that by eating had slain the first-created parents.[^715]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* With drops of blood, O sufferers of Christ, ye dried up the abyss of godlessness; and by divine outpourings of sacred wonders ye dam up the streams of passions of both souls and bodies; therefore as is meet are ye called blessed.[^716]

*Theotokion:*
The Undefiled One marveled, beholding Christ voluntarily lifted up upon the tree, and weeping she cried aloud: O my Son and God! having escaped pains in Thy birthgiving, now do I suffer pains, when Thou art unjustly crucified by the lawless.[^717]

*Other Heirmos:* Thou Who didst enlighten with the radiance of Thy coming, O Christ, and didst illumine with Thy Cross...[^718]

...the ends of the world: enlighten with the light of the knowledge of Thee the hearts of those who orthodoxly sing of Thee.[^719]

*Troparion 1:* Torrents of many transgressions rushed upon me and cast down the temple of my soul, O Most Pure One! But as thou art the restoration of the forefathers, O Theotokos, raise me up, thy servant. (Twice)[^720]

*Troparion 2:* Sunk wretched into the mire of passions and drowned by the tempest of many transgressions: stretch forth thy hand, O Lady, and lead me up, and guide me unto the haven of repentance.[^721]

*Troparion 3:* Grant me cleansing of the filth of the soul, and heal me of bodily sicknesses, I pray thee, and change me from the cruel despondency that grips me, by thine intercessions.[^722]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Wholly held fast by measureless passions, I have fallen down into the whale of evils; but lead me up out of corruption, O God, even as of old Thou didst Jonah, and by faith grant me dispassion, that with the voice of praise and the spirit of salvation I may sacrifice unto Thee.[^723]

*Troparion 1:* Lifting up his hands on high, Moses prefigured Thy Passion, Who didst stretch forth Thy palms upon the tree and destroy the destructive dominion of the evil one; therefore knowing Thee as Deliverer and Savior, we sing unto Thee, O Lover of mankind.[^724]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst endure death when lifted up upon the tree, and didst put to death our slayer, and didst make alive the creation of Thy hands, O Christ; and pierced in the ribs with a spear, Thou didst pour forth two rivers of forgiveness, Who art praised in two wills.[^725]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* Precious in the sight of the Lord is your death now become, O Saints, who honored all by your honorable sufferings, and put to shame the dishonorable Belial, who plotted by all assaults of torments to cast you all down.[^726]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Having with all your soul turned away unto diverse torments, O all-wise ones, ye found all-availing help from Him Who clothed Himself in all humanity; therefore, when cut limb from limb and brought nigh unto the fire, ye rejoiced.[^727]

*Theotokion:*
Beholding Thy Crucifixion, the Most Pure One cried aloud: O Son, what is this strange vision that is seen? Thou Who healest the sufferings of the sick, how dost Thou endure new suffering, O Christ? What have Thine enemies rendered unto Thee...[^728]

...O Benefactor, in return for the benefits they received?[^729]

*Other Heirmos:* The lowest abyss hath encompassed us; there is none to deliver; we were accounted as sheep for the slaughter. Save Thy people, O our God! for Thou art the strength of the weak and the restoration.[^730]

*Troparion 1:* Tear up the handwriting of my transgressions, O Most Pure One, with the spear of Him Who was born from thy seedless womb, and deem me worthy to be written in the book of the elect, who flee unto thy divine protection. (Twice)[^731]

*Troparion 2:* Cleanse Thy servants, O Christ, by the prayers of her who gave birth unto Thee, and send down forgiveness of transgressions, as Thou alone art good; for Thou art the Savior and Deliverer of all who hope in Thee.[^732]

*Troparion 3:* Me who am slain by passions, O most pure Virgin, who didst give birth unto the Giver of life, make me alive now by thy prayers, and show me to be victor over the evil enemy; for thee alone do we have as our helper before God.[^733]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* They traversed as a bridal chamber the unbearable furnace-flame, the holy children who of old clearly appeared for piety's sake, and singing in harmony they sang the hymn: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^734]

*Troparion 1:* Being Master, Thou didst receive a blow from a servant, O my Jesus, desiring to deliver me who was enslaved to the enemy; and nailed upon the Cross, Thou savest me who sing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^735]

*Troparion 2:* All creation was shaken when Thou wast crucified, O tender-hearted Lord! When Thou wast pierced with a spear, the enemy received a mortal wound in all his body, and wounded Adam was healed, crying: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^736]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* Shielded by the Cross, the glorious army of passion-bearers destroyed the hostile regiments by grace, and received crowns of victory, crying: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^737]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Having acquired a resolve stronger than fire, O sufferers, ye were not burned when brought into the fire; but ye burned up the evil substance of impiety, crying: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^738]

*Theotokion:*
Thy childbearing, O Virgin, most gloriously brought forth the Creator of the ages; and lifted up upon the Cross, He raised up the fallen with Himself, and showed them forth as heavenly citizens, who...[^739]

...cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^740]

*Other Heirmos:* We the faithful perceive thee, O Theotokos, as a spiritual furnace; for even as the Supremely Exalted One saved the three children, He wholly renewed the world within thy womb, the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^741]

*Troparion 1:* Having given birth unto the Fountain of life, O Most Pure One, raise up my dead soul with thy life-bearing right hand, and vouchsafe me to cry in contrition of my heart: O praised God of our fathers, blessed art Thou! (Twice)[^742]

*Troparion 2:* He Who is God before the ages was pleased to become the New Adam from thy pure blood. Him now entreat to renew me who am grown old, as I cry: O praised God of our fathers, blessed art Thou![^743]

*Theotokion:*
Coming by way of wrath, the enemy mercilessly desires to snatch away my humble soul. Destroy his counsels, O Most Pure One, and take pity on me who sing: O praised God of our fathers, blessed art Thou![^744]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The dew-bearing furnace showed forth of old a figure of supernatural wonder; for the fire burned not the young children, manifesting Christ's seedless and divine childbirth from the Virgin. Therefore, hymning let us sing: Let all creation bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^745]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast crucified upon the Cross voluntarily, Who by Thy nod didst stretch out the heaven; and Thou wast fastened with nails, desiring to bring to naught the passionate lapse of Adam, the first-created. Therefore, hymning we sing: Let every creature bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^746]

*Troparion 2:* When the stony-hearted assembly lifted Thee up upon the rock, the mountains were shaken and the earth trembled, O Word of God, and wavering souls were established unto divine life, ever crying: Let every creature bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^747]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* The holy passion-bearers clearly wove for themselves a saving garment, patiently suffering in bodily nakedness and receiving the rubbing of salt into their wounds. Therefore, hymning they sing: Let every creature bless the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^748]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Scraped unmercifully with claws, the passion-bearers, and mercilessly...[^749]

...cut limb from limb, and partaking of all manner of wounds, sacrificed not unto carved idols; but they appeared as pillars of courage unto the faithful, crying: Bless Christ, all ye works, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^750]

*Theotokion:*
When the foolish council that embittered Thee thought to take Thee away from the earth, she who knew not man cried aloud of old, beholding Thee lifted up upon the Cross: I am become childless and am troubled, and in heart I suffer pain with maternal love! Together with her creation glorifies Thee, the Deliverer of all, O Jesus, unto all ages.[^751]

*Other Heirmos:* In the furnace the children of Israel, as in a crucible, shone purer than gold with the beauty of piety, saying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; praise and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^752]

*Troparion 1:* Enlighten the eyes of my heart, O Pure One, who art the door of light, dispersing the cloud of passions and the deep darkness, that I may sing: Praise the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages! (Twice)[^753]

*Troparion 2:* Cease not to pray for all, O pure Lady, who call upon thee as the Mother of God, O all-immaculate Virgin, and who cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; praise and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^754]

*Troparion 3:* Having ineffably given birth unto Christ, the Cause of salvation: entreat Him on behalf of all who earnestly cry, O all-hymned Virgin: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; praise and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^755]

*Then we sing the Canticle of the Theotokos: My soul doth magnify the Lord [Luke 1:46-55]: And prostrations.*[^756]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Ineffable is the Virgin's mystery: for she hath been shown forth as heaven, the Cherubic Throne, and the light-bearing bridal chamber of Christ God the Almighty. Her do we piously magnify as Theotokos.[^757]

*Troparion 1:* When of old the wise thief beheld Thee, O Savior, hung upon the tree, Who didst suspend the earth upon the measureless waters, he faithfully cried unto Thee: Remember me! With whom we piously glorify Thy sufferings.[^758]

*Troparion 2:* When crucified Thou didst shake the foundations of the earth; and when pierced with a spear Thou dost pour forth immortal drops, blood and water: wherewith Thou hast cleansed humanity from passions, O Jesus! Therefore hymning Thee do we magnify.[^759]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* The Saints rejoiced in their torments, inciting themselves...[^760]

...unto sweetness, the noble ones, exclaiming: Let us stand, lo, the race-course is opened, Christ now bestows crowns upon those whom He hath loved![^761]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Every singular triumph of the faithful is enlightened, honoring the sufferings of all the invincible Martyrs and the multitude of afflictions; for the sake whereof they were vouchsafed painless sweetness and life, and everlasting enjoyment.[^762]

*Theotokion:*
Beholding Him Who is beyond cause, the Lover of mankind, the Word, willingly suffering from men, the Maiden exclaimed: What is this? God Who is impassible accepts passion, that He may deliver from passions those who worship Him in faith.[^763]

*Other Heirmos:* The burning bush showed forth unburned a figure of thy pure childbearing; and now we pray thee to quench the furnace of temptations raging against us, that unceasingly, O Theotokos, we may magnify thee.[^764]

*Troparion 1:* Take away the heavy burden of my transgressions, O All-praised Theotokos, and vouchsafe me to bear the most light yoke of Thy Son and God, and to journey along the path leading up unto heavenly perfection. (Twice)[^765]

*Troparion 2:* I tremble when considering, O All-immaculate One, the dread Day of Christ's coming; for all my life hath been spent in sins, and my soul is filled with passions; but take pity on me, and deliver me then from all condemnation.[^766]

*Theotokion:*
Receive, O Most Pure Lady, the supplication of thine unprofitable servant, and turn the turmoil of my soul and body into deep tranquility, that saved I may magnify thee.[^767]

*Then: It is truly meet: and a prostration, the Small Litany, and the Exapostilarion. Then the customary Psalms.*[^768]`

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 1:*
*Sticheron 1:* Unto Thee Who wast nailed upon the tree in the flesh, and didst grant us life, as Savior and Master do we sing unceasingly.[^769]

*Sticheron 2:* By Thy Cross, O Christ, there became one flock of Angels and men, and in one assembly heaven and earth rejoice, crying: Lord, glory to Thee![^770]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* Neither affliction, nor distress, nor famine, nor persecution, nor wounds, nor the fury...[^771]

...of beasts, nor sword, nor fire threatening to sever you from God could prevail against you, O all-praised Martyrs; but rather through love toward Him, striving as in alien bodies, ye forgot nature, despising death; therefore worthily of your afflictions ye received the reward, and became heirs of the Heavenly Kingdom. Pray unceasingly for our souls.[^772]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "O all-lauded martyrs" (Прехва́льнїи мꙋ́ченицы):*
Standing beside Thy Cross, O Word of God, the Ewe-lamb and Thine undefiled Mother, lamenting cried aloud: How art Thou dying upon the Cross, O my Son? Woe is me, my sweet Light! Whither hath the vision of Thy beauty now set, Who art fairer than all the sons of men?[^773]

*Then: It is good to give praise: and the Trisagion, and after Our Father: the Troparion, the Litany, and the Dismissal. Then the First Hour, and the customary Psalms, and the final Dismissal.*[^774]

*On Wednesday, The Beatitudes, Tone 1:*
*Troparion 1:* Through food the enemy led Adam out of Paradise, but by the Cross Christ led the thief back into it, who cried: Remember me, when Thou comest into Thy Kingdom![^775]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst endure an ignominious Passion, O Christ, and didst take away all our reproach, O Good One, and didst make us partakers of the Kingdom on high, who worship Thy descent.[^776]

*Troparion 3:* Covering the nakedness of Adam, O Christ, Thou wast stripped in the flesh; and lifted up upon the Cross, Thou didst lift us up out of the depth of evils. Therefore, O Word, we glorify Thy holy descent.[^777]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Having wounded the multitude of demons by your holy wounds, O all-praised Martyrs, by grace ye always truly heal all human sores and wounds.[^778]

*Glory...*
*Triadikon:* The Trinity enlightens all who purely honor It and know It as possessing indestructible dominion; unto Whom let us cry: Save those who sing unto Thee with love, O Trinity![^779]

*Both now...*
*Stavrotheotokion:* Standing beside the Cross and beholding Christ nailed in the flesh, the All-immaculate One exclaimed, crying aloud: Whither hath Thy truly ineffable beauty now set, O Compassionate One?[^780]

## Thursday: The Holy Apostles and Saint Nicholas[^781]

*On Wednesday Evening.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Apostles, Tone 1:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "O all-lauded martyrs" (Прехва́льнїи мꙋ́ченицы):*
*Sticheron 1:* Glorious Apostles, God-called disciples of Christ, teachers of the universe! Having found the Lord, Who is the Mediator between God and men, unto Him ye were joined in a manner becoming God; and Him as God and as all-perfect Man ye plainly proclaimed in the world.[^782]

*Sticheron 2:* All-wise Apostles, God-called disciples of Christ, teachers of the universe! By your prayers strengthen me to attend unto divine teachings, and help me ever to walk along the narrow path, that I may attain unto the most spacious dwelling of Paradise.[^783]

*Sticheron 3:* I hymn Peter the first-enthroned, Paul, James, Andrew, and Philip, Simon, Bartholomew, and Thomas, Matthew and John, Mark and Luke, who wrote the Good News: the God-chosen choir, with the other seventy, as eyewitnesses of the Word and His preachers.[^784]

*Other Stichera, to the Hierarch Nicholas, Tone 1:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Joy of the heavenly ranks" (Нбⷭ҇ныхъ чинѡ́въ):*
*Sticheron 1:* Flitting over the flowers of the Church like a fledgling of the celestial angelic nest, O thrice-blessed Nicholas, thou ever criest unto God for all people who are in dire misfortunes and temptations, and deliverest them by thy prayers.[^785]

*Sticheron 2:* Passing through celestial beauties, thou didst perceive that dread glory, the Holy of Holies. Therefore, O most sacred Father, thou ever revealest unto us heavenly words, visions of those living beings.[^786]

*Sticheron 3:* By active virtues thou didst make the adornment of the sacred vestments most radiant, O God-bearing Father. Therefore, for Christ's sake thou also dost sacredly work for us marvelous and glorious wonders, delivering us from fierce evils.[^787]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Tempest-tossed by the deep of transgressions, having fled unto the calm haven of thy pure prayer, O Theotokos, I cry unto thee: Save me, stretching forth thy strong right hand unto thy servant, O All-immaculate One![^788]

*Then: O Gladsome Light: Prokeimenon of the day. Vouchsafe, O Lord:*[^789]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera to the Apostles, Tone 1:*
*Sticheron 1:* The all-harmonious apostolic lute, moved by the Holy Spirit, brought to naught the abominable sacrifices of the demons; and having proclaimed the one Lord, it delivered the nations from the deception of idols, and taught them to worship the consubstantial Trinity.[^790]

*Sticheron 2:* Let us praise Peter and Paul in unison, Luke, Matthew, Mark, John, Andrew, Simon the Canaanite, James, and Philip, and let us worthily praise the whole choir of disciples.[^791]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* The earth hid you not, O all-praised Martyrs, but heaven received you, and the gates of Paradise were opened unto you, and being within, ye enjoy the Tree of Life. Pray unto Christ to grant our souls peace and great mercy.[^792]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O Virgin Theotokos unwedded, who in the latter times didst give birth unto God, and after childbirth wast truly shown forth again as a pure Virgin: the throne, and gate, and mystical table, and radiant lampstand.[^793]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion and Dismissal.*[^794]

*On Wednesday at Compline.*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 1.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Let us all sing a triumphant song unto God, Who hath wrought marvelous wonders with an high arm, and saved Israel; for He hath been glorified.[^795]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* The burning bush prefigured thee, O Theotokos; for thou didst truly bear the unendurable Fire, remaining unconsumed. Therefore with faithful voices do we ever hymn thee.[^796]

*Troparion 2:* Having clothed Himself in man, God the Word was incarnate of thee beyond understanding, O Most Pure One. Therefore every breath glorifies thee, and worships thee, and duly honors thee.[^797]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Thou didst conceive, O Most Pure One, the ineffable Word Who contains all the ends of the earth, and didst give birth unto Him; Whom earnestly entreat to have mercy on us.[^798]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Having given birth in time unto the incarnate, timeless God, Who alone is...[^799]

...O Most Pure Lady, heal the lifelong passions of my passionate soul.[^800]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Let my heart be established in Thy will, O Christ God, Who didst establish the second heaven above the waters, and didst found the earth upon the waters, O All-powerful One.[^801]

*Troparion 1:* That God might deify humanity, He became man from thee, O Pure Virgin, beyond word and understanding. Therefore in unison do we, the faithful, call thee blessed.[^802]

*Troparion 2:* He Who by nature is uncircumscribed, having become incarnate of thee, was circumscribed, O Maiden full of the grace of God! Pray unceasingly unto Him to take pity on and enlighten the souls of those who call thee blessed.[^803]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Drive away all the barrenness of my barren thought, and show forth my soul fruitful in virtues, O Most Holy Theotokos, helper of the faithful.[^804]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Deliver me, O All-immaculate One, from all misfortune, and from the many snares of the serpent, and from eternal fire and darkness, O thou who didst give birth unto the never-setting Light for us.[^805]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Foreseeing in the Spirit the incarnation of the Word, O Prophet Habakkuk, thou didst proclaim, crying: When the years draw near, Thou shalt be known; when the time is come, Thou shalt be shown forth! Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^806]

*Troparion 1:* Christ took up His abode within thy most pure womb, O All-holy Lady, and deified us, having assumed an animate flesh. Therefore as pure Mother do we orthodoxly hymn thee, O Lady, helper of the world.[^807]

*Troparion 2:* O Holy Theotokos, sanctify us, who didst give birth in the flesh unto the Most Holy One, Who desired to be likened unto men; and by thy prayers, O Most Pure One, show all to be partakers of the Heavenly Kingdom.[^808]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* O Virgin Theotokos, undefiled tabernacle, cleanse me now who am defiled with transgressions, by the pure beams of thy mercies; and grant me a helping hand, that I may cry: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^809]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Thou wast shown forth as a consecrated Church unto God, Who took up His abode within thee beyond understanding, O Virgin; entreat Him to cleanse the filth of our sins, that we may be shown forth as temples and dwellings of the Spirit.[^810]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Grant us Thy peace, O Son of God! For we know no other God beside Thee; we call upon Thy name, for Thou art the God of the living and of the dead.[^811]

*Troparion 1:* The deceitful tasting in Eden made me dead of old; but having given birth unto Life, O Pure One, thou didst quicken him who of old was slain by the tree, that I may hymn thee with songs of praise.[^812]

*Troparion 2:* Save me, O All-pure One, from fierce misfortunes, raise me up from festering passions, and deliver me from the captivity and malice of deceitful demons, who honor thee with love.[^813]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* We know thee as cloud and paradise, and door of light, table and fleece, and jar carrying the manna within, the sweetness of the world, O pure Virgin Mother.[^814]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Having given birth, O All-immaculate One, unto God Emmanuel, Who plainly became man for tender mercy's sake: entreat Him, as Lover of mankind, O Pure One, to take pity on people who have sinned.[^815]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* As the Prophet Jonah, lead up my life from corruption, O Christ God, I cry unto Thee, O Lover of mankind; for with Thee is life, and incorruption, and power.[^816]

*Troparion 1:* Defiled as I am with many sins, I pray thee, who art the good and undefiled tabernacle: Wash away all defilement by thy mediation.[^817]

*Troparion 2:* Be thou pilot unto me, O Pure One, who am tossed on the fierce deep of worldly temptations, and guide me unto the saving haven, and save me.[^818]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Surges of thoughts, and assaults of passions, and the depth of sins tempest-toss my wretched soul. Help me, O holy Lady![^819]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* O Mary, who wast forenamed the tabernacle of sanctification, sanctify my wretched soul defiled by pleasures, and make me a partaker of divine glory.[^820]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (40).*
*Glory... Both now... Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Thy tomb, O Savior" (Гро́бъ тво́й Сп҃се):*
O Virgin and nourisher of One of the Trinity, beautiful paradise, salvation of earthly beings: by thy protection save those who piously hymn thee; for thou didst give birth unto Him Who spoke through the Prophets, thou didst bear Him Who contains all things, as Mother of Christ God.[^821]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The fire touched not nor troubled Thy children in the furnace, O Savior; then the three, as with one mouth...[^822]

...sang and blessed, saying: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^823]

*Troparion 1:* The Son of the Unoriginate Father took up His abode within thy womb, taking a beginning, that as God He might deliver us from the deceitful rulers of darkness, O pure Theotokos, who worship Him.[^824]

*Troparion 2:* Embroidered with divine virtues, O pure Virgin, thou didst give birth unto the Word co-unoriginate with the Father, Who truly covered the heavens with virtues; Whom unceasingly entreat to take pity on us.[^825]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Sanctify our thoughts, establish the souls of all, O Mother of God, that they may rightly perform the ordinances of the Word of the Unoriginate Father, Who was incarnate of thee for the sake of ineffable tender mercy, O Virgin.[^826]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Slain by many passions, O All-immaculate One, quicken my mind, and strengthen me to do what is well-pleasing unto God, that I may magnify thee as the constant protectress and hope of Christians.[^827]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him at Whom the Angels and all the hosts are terrified as Creator and Lord: praise, ye priests; glorify, ye children; bless, ye peoples; and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^828]

*Troparion 1:* We are delivered from all manner of temptations, O All-immaculate One, by thy watchful prayers unto God, who know thee as the Theotokos, blessed and full of joy.[^829]

*Troparion 2:* The Bodiless One is incarnate of thee in a manner becoming God; Whom entreat, O Most Pure One, to put to death the passions of my flesh, and to quicken my soul which hath been slain by sins.[^830]`

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Having given birth unto the Savior and God, Who healed the crushing of earthly Adam, O Most Pure One: entreat Him to heal the incurably aching wounds of my soul.[^831]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Raise me up who lie in the depth of evils, and conquer the enemies who now war against me, consuming my soul with unlawful pleasures; despise me not, O Pure One, but take pity, and save me.[^832]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* The light-bearing cloud, whereinto the Master of all descended like rain from heaven upon the fleece, and was incarnate for our sake, becoming man, the Unoriginate One: all we magnify as the pure Mother of our God.[^833]

*Troparion 1:* O All-immaculate One, who didst give birth unto the divine Light Which shone forth from the Father: take pity on my soul darkened by the deceits of life and become a laughing-stock...[^834]

...unto enemies, and vouchsafe me the light of saving repentance, O Pure One.[^835]

*Troparion 2:* Isaiah beheld thee as a radiant cloud, O All-immaculate One, from whom the Sun of Righteousness, having shone forth upon us, mystically enlightened creation; therefore in faith do we hymn thee, who art fair among women.[^836]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Being sin-loving, I live in slothfulness, and tremble at the unbribable judgment-seat, O Pure One; whereat preserve me uncondemned by thy holy prayers, O Virgin Bride of God, that as protectress I may call thee blessed unceasingly.[^837]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* I tremble at the judgment-seat, O Virgin, and the unforgetting eye of Thy Son, having committed many sins upon earth; and therefore do I cry unto thee: All-merciful Lady, help me, and deliver me then uncondemned from distress, O Pure One.[^838]

*Then: It is truly meet: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparia and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*[^839]

*On Thursday at Matins.*
*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Apostles, Tone 1:*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* All-wise fishers of the universe, who from God have received the gift of prayer! Pray even now for us who cry: O Lord, save Thy people and Thy city, and deliver our souls from the evils that beset us, for the sake of the Apostles.[^840]

*Verse: Their sound hath gone forth into all the earth, and their words unto the ends of the world [LXX Ps. 18:5].*[^841]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* In hymns let us the faithful honor the sweet-voiced trumpets of Christ, the all-wise Apostles, the horses who troubled the sea of godlessness, and drew men as from the depth unto the divine haven of salvation, by the grace of the Spirit.[^842]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Having conceived unburned the Fire of the Godhead, and without seed given birth unto the Lord, the Fountain of life, O joyful Theotokos: save those who magnify thee![^843]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 1:*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Having with the rod of the Cross cast down the rhetorical webs with a rational net, the fishermen enlightened the nations to glorify Thee piously as the true God. Therefore do we cry a hymn unto Thee Who didst strengthen them: Glory to the Father and to the Son, glory to the Con-...[^844]

...substantial Spirit; glory to Him Who through them hath enlightened the world![^845]

*Verse: The heavens shall confess Thy wonders, O Lord, and Thy truth in the church of the saints [LXX Ps. 88:6].*[^846]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* The Light Which shone timelessly from Light, in time appeared in the flesh upon earth, and through you enlightened the world, O all-blessed ones. Therefore, all we being enlightened by your divine teachings, honor your sacred memory, O Apostles.[^847]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel [LXX Ps. 67:36].*[^848]
*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 3:* All we pray unto the Martyrs of Christ, having come unto them with love and faith; for they ask for our salvation, they pour forth the grace of healings, they drive away the legions of demons, as guardians of the faith.[^849]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
The divine Disciples and Apostles, O Maiden, by divine teachings proclaimed unto all thy ineffable childbearing, O most pure Theotokos, worthily teaching us to worship thee. But we pray thee: With them entreat that those who honor thee in faith may be saved.[^850]

*After the 3rd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 1:*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Radiant luminaries, light-bearing Disciples of the Savior, like fire ye illuminated the fullness of all the earth by divine teachings. Therefore I pray: Enlighten my soul which is in darkness, with your light-bearing rays, O all-blessed ones.[^851]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Dwelling sensibly in Myra, O Hierarch, thou wast shown forth spiritually anointed as with myrrh by the Spirit, O Father Nicholas, and with the myrrh of thy wonders thou didst make all fragrant, pouring forth ever-flowing myrrh, by thy fragrant songs and by thy memory.[^852]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
The Prophets clearly foreannounced thee, O Maiden, as Mother of God, and the divine Apostles proclaimed it in the world, and we have believed. Therefore, all we honoring thee with pious mind do hymn thee, and truly name thee Theotokos unceasingly.[^853]

*Canon to the Holy Apostles, a composition of Theophanes, Tone 1.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Thy victorious right hand hath in a manner becoming God been glorified in strength; for it, O Immortal One, being all-powerful, shattered the adversaries, fashioning a new path through the deep for the Israelites.[^854]

*Refrain: Holy Apostles, pray to God for us.*
*Troparion 1:* Enlightened by the divine beams of the Tri-sun radiance, O glorious ones, by adoption ye were truly called gods, O light-bearing Apostles! Therefore we worthily honor you in faith. (Twice)[^855]

*Troparion 2:* Having through faith become acceptable servants and fulfillers of all the commandments of the Word Who appeared on earth for tender mercy's sake in the grossness of the flesh...[^856]

...ye are unceasingly honored.[^857]

*Troparion 3:* With radiant rays of the Most Holy Spirit, O ever-blessed ones, enlighten me entirely, who am covered with the darkness of sins, and plainly guide me unto the path of repentance.[^858]

*Theotokion:*
O joy of the Apostles, O Theotokos, All-immaculate Lady, who didst become the Mother of Him Who spoke in them in a manner becoming God: with them pray that I may be delivered from the fire of Gehenna.[^859]

*Another Canon, to our Father among the Saints Nicholas the Wonderworker, whereof the Acrostic is: Unto thee, Nicholas, I offer the first singing, I, Joseph. Tone 1.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:* Let us all sing a triumphant song unto God, Who hath wrought marvelous wonders with an high arm, and saved Israel; for He hath been glorified.[^860]

*Troparion 1:* Adorned with righteous crowns, and standing before the throne of grace, O Nicholas: ever save by thy prayers those who now crown thee in faith with songs.[^861]

*Troparion 2:* Having received the grace of healings, O all-blessed Nicholas, heal the wounds of my soul by thy prayers, and deliver me from the temptations that assail me, I pray.[^862]

*Troparion 3:* By the power of thy prayer, O Nicholas, set aright my soul utterly paralyzed by transgressions, and deliver me from the bitter evils of life, I pray.[^863]

*Theotokion:*
Dispel the darkening of my mind, O All-immaculate One, with thy light; and deliver me from eternal darkness, that I may ever sing of thy great deeds.[^864]

*Ode 3 [to the Apostles]. Heirmos:* Thou Who alone knowest the weakness of human nature, and in Thy mercy didst take form therein: gird me with power from on high, that I may cry unto Thee: Holy is the animate temple of Thine ineffable glory, O Lover of mankind![^865]

*Troparion 1:* He Who alone is the invisible God, becoming visible in incarnation, chose you as disciples throughout all the world, who proclaim His name and surpassing glory, O most blessed and divine Apostles! (Twice)[^866]`

*Troparion 2:* Against Thee alone have I sinned, O Christ, against Thee alone have I transgressed, and have defiled my soul with evils; cleanse and save me by Thy mercy, having praying unto Thee...[^867]

...O Jesus Who alone art easily reconciled, Thy most wise Apostles.[^868]

*Troparion 3:* Deliver me from the bitterness of passions and thoughts, and from every sin, as merciful Apostles! And sweeten my mind with repentance, as bearing divine sweetness in your hearts, O all-praised ones.[^869]

*Theotokion:*
With the bodiless ministers, O Virgin unwedded, with all the Hosts on high, with the Martyrs and Apostles, entreat Christ Whom thou didst incarnate of thy pure blood, that His servants may be saved.[^870]

*Other Heirmos:* Let my heart be established in Thy will, O Christ God, Who didst establish the second heaven above the waters, and didst found the earth upon the waters, O Almighty One.[^871]

*Troparion 1:* Adornment of High Priests, fragrance of the Divine Spirit, by thy myrrh-streaming prayers drive away the foul-smelling passions of my heart, O wise Nicholas, I pray thee with love.[^872]

*Troparion 2:* In slothfulness do I end my life, wretch that I am, and I fear Thy dread judgment-seat, O Christ; whereat put me not to shame, being entreated through the sacred intercessions of Nicholas.[^873]

*Troparion 3:* Pre-eminently adorned with divine grace, O Hierarch Father Nicholas, from manifold temptations and tribulations save those who ever flee unto thy honorable shelter, O all-blessed one.[^874]

*Theotokion:*
Deliver me from all assaults, and from the many snares of the serpent, and from everlasting fire and darkness, O All-immaculate One, who didst give birth for us unto the Unwaning Light.[^875]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Habakkuk, beholding with foresighted eyes thee as a mountain overshadowed by the grace of God, foreproclaimed that the Holy One of Israel should come forth from thee, unto our salvation and restoration.[^876]

*Troparion 1:* Having troubled the sea of godlessness and unbelief by your riding, O divinely chosen steeds, Apostles of Christ, ye drowned the spiritual enemy, and drew up drowned men unto salvation. (Twice)[^877]

*Troparion 2:* Receptacles of the divine radiances of the Spirit, O Apostles, enlighten with the light of repentance my darkened soul, which hath become receptive to all manner of passions, O divinely blessed and divine Apostles.[^878]

*Troparion 3:* Clouds that have rained down the living water, divinely water my soul withered by the drought of passions, and make it bring forth ears of virtues unto salvation, O all-praised Apostles.[^879]

*Theotokion:*
Prophets, all-lauded Apostles, Martyrs, with the Mother of the Redeemer pray earnestly that we may be delivered from sins, and eternal torment, and temptations, and tribulations, and sorrows.[^880]

*Other Heirmos:* Foreseeing in spirit the incarnation of the Word, O Prophet Habakkuk, thou didst proclaim, crying: When the years draw near, Thou shalt be known; when the time is come, Thou shalt be shown forth. Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^881]

*Troparion 1:* As a fulfiller of all the commandments of God, O Father Hierarch Nicholas, hasten by thy prayers to help us keep the ordinances that lead unto salvation, and deliver us from all assailing misfortunes.[^882]

*Troparion 2:* Having piously finished thy course in Christ, direct our paths which lead unto Him, O God-bearing Father Nicholas, that having escaped wayward wandering, we may attain unto salvific perfection.[^883]

*Troparion 3:* Having put to sleep all the wiles of the enemy by divine vigils, O most wise Nicholas: bestow grace, O father, upon all of us who keep vigil and sing unto God, offering thee as an intercessor unto Him.[^884]

*Theotokion:*
Having understood by the Spirit of God, the Prophet prefigured thee, O Pure One, as an overshadowed mountain; by thy acceptable intercessions, O Theotokos, now refresh with grace those wasted by the heat of many transgressions.[^885]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Thou Who didst enlighten with the radiance of Thy coming, O Christ, and didst illumine the ends of the world with Thy Cross: enlighten with the light of Thy divine knowledge the hearts of those who sing unto Thee in orthodox manner.[^886]

*Troparion 1:* Mountains dropping sweetness and fair gladness were ye revealed, O all-glorious Apostles, taking away all the bitterness of the enemy, and delighting the faithful. (Twice)[^887]

*Troparion 2:* Ye recognized Christ coming as a stranger unto His own, and sincerely cleaved unto Him; therefore deliver me from the harm of the alien one, O divine Apostles of the Word.[^888]

*Troparion 3:* Heal the secret wounds of my soul by the sacred prayers of those who proclaimed in the world Thy divine coming, and Thy passions, O Compassionate One, and Thy resurrection from the tomb.[^889]

*Theotokion:*
With all the bodiless ones, entreat God the Word Whom thou didst bear in the flesh beyond word, O Theotokos Virgin, that thy servants may be delivered from irrational deeds and deadly pleasures.[^890]

*Other Heirmos:* Make the bright, ever-existing light shine upon us who rise early unto the judgments of Thy commandments, O Master, Lover of mankind, Christ our God.[^891]

*Troparion 1:* Planted in the courts of the Lord, O Hierarch Father Nicholas, and like a fruitful olive tree, by grace thou dost now anoint the faces of all with the oil of thy labors.[^892]

*Troparion 2:* Make supplication now, O Father Nicholas, for thy servants, that we may receive remission of transgressions, and be delivered from the sorrow that encompasses us, and from every strait.[^893]

*Troparion 3:* Thee do we pray, Nicholas, as a good intercessor unto the Lord: leave us not without protection, O holy one, but save us by thy customary prayer.[^894]

*Theotokion:*
Light-like temple of Christ, Maiden divinely graced! By thy prayers make us also temples unto the Father, and the Son, and the Spirit, performing things well-pleasing.[^895]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* The lowest abyss hath surrounded us, and there is none to deliver; we are accounted as sheep for the slaughter; save Thy people, O our God! For Thou art the strength and restoration of the weak.[^896]

*Troparion 1:* With spiritual nets ye caught the nations unto knowledge of the building of men which was revealed, O divinely blessed Apostles! Whom earnestly entreat for the world. (Twice)[^897]

*Troparion 2:* O my humble soul, O wretched soul, O impenitent soul: repent, and cry aloud unto Christ: I have sinned! Cleanse me, O Master, Lover of mankind, by the prayers of Thine Apostles, for Thou art exceeding good.[^898]

*Troparion 3:* O Almighty One Who of old didst gush forth water from the rock for Israel: dispel my darkening, O Christ, and make me bring forth streams of tears, by the prayers of Thine Apostles, as Thou art plenteous in mercy, that I may sing and magnify Thy tender mercy.[^899]

*Theotokion:*
O Virgin, entreat Him Who for His goodness was well-pleased to be born of thee, as Creator and God, to save from temptations and tribulations those who ever have hope in thee, O Most Holy One.[^900]

*Other Heirmos:* Imitating the Prophet Jonah, I cry aloud: Deliver my life from corruption, O Good One, and save me, O Savior of the world, who cry: Glory to Thee![^901]

*Troparion 1:* Having a multitude of compassions, O Christ, drive away the multitude of my evils, by the prayers of Nicholas, guiding my life which is ever tempest-tossed by the waves of sin.[^902]

*Troparion 2:* Mightily didst thou trample down the enemy, O wise one; strengthen us also by thy prayers, O Nicholas, to crush him, having been enriched with thee as a divine protector.[^903]

*Troparion 3:* Having been a true chief hierarch unto the people of Myra, make fragrant our spiritual senses, O Father Nicholas, and ever drive away the foul-smelling passions that war against us.[^904]

*Theotokion:*
Christ hath wrought great things for thee, O pure Virgin; entreat Him unceasingly to magnify in us His rich mercies, O woman full of the grace of God.[^905]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* We the faithful perceive thee, O Theotokos, as a spiritual furnace; for even as the Supremely Exalted One saved the three Children, so He renewed the world entirely within thy womb: the praised and supremely glorified God of our fathers.[^906]

*Troparion 1:* Jesus the Master, the Fountain of life, left you His disciples as rivers watering with waters: Praised and supremely glorified is the God of our fathers! (Twice)[^907]

*Troparion 2:* Bearing the spiritual fire in your hearts, the divine grace of Christ, ye disciples burned up the matter of godlessness; therefore burn up my material passions, who cry: Praised and supremely glorified is the God of our fathers![^908]

*Troparion 3:* Deliver me from fiery torment, O God, by the prayers of Thy glorious disciples, and cast me not away from Thy presence, O Lord, who cry in repentance: Praised and supremely glorified is the God of our fathers![^909]

*Theotokion:*
Deliver me from corruptive sins and passions, O Lord, Who wast incorruptibly born of the Virgin Mother of God; and grant incorruption unto all who sing in hymnody: Praised and supremely glorified is the God of our fathers![^910]

*Other Heirmos:* The fire touched not nor troubled Thy Children in the furnace, O Savior...[^911]

...Then the three, as with one mouth, sang and blessed, saying: Blessed is the God of our fathers![^912]

*Troparion 1:* By thy fervent prayer, O wise one, firmly establish the steps of my heart upon the rock of God's most bright commandments, unceasingly saving me, O Nicholas, from the harmful wiles of the author of evil, the enemy.[^913]

*Troparion 2:* Ask for us remission of many sins, and deliverance from worldly and grievous snares, and all assailing temptations, O sacred Nicholas, protector of the faithful, and foundation of hierarchs.[^914]

*Troparion 3:* I am that slothful and wicked servant who hid the talent which I received for good doing; and I fear that future judgment-seat, whereon, O God, Judge of all, condemn me not, for the sake of the supplication of Saint Nicholas.[^915]

*Theotokion:*
Thee, O All-holy, All-pure One, do thy servants ever entreat, by day and by night, asking with contrite mind that through thy prayers, O Pure One, remission of our sins may be granted us.[^916]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* In the furnace, the Children of Israel, as in a crucible, shone more pure than gold with the beauty of piety, saying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^917]

*Troparion 1:* As rays the great Sun spread you forth into all the universe, enlightening those who sing in faith, O Apostles: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing and supremely exalt Him unto all ages! (Twice)[^918]

*Troparion 2:* Being spiritual shepherds, as lambs of the Shepherd, as sheep, O God-beholding Apostles, entreat the Lamb, Christ our Redeemer, unceasingly to deliver me from the spiritual wolf, and to vouchsafe me the portion of the saved.[^919]

*Troparion 3:* O most wretched soul, groan and cry aloud unto the Lord: I have sinned more than all, and have lawlessly transgressed grievously; cleanse and save me as the harlot, as the publican, and as the thief, O Compassionate One, by the acceptable prayers of Thine Apostles.[^920]

*Theotokion:*
With the Angels, O Mother of God, with the Apostles and Martyrs, and with the Prophets, entreat Christ to save those who cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; sing and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^921]

*Other Heirmos:* Him Whom the Angels and all the hosts dread as Creator and Lord: sing, ye priests; glorify, ye children; bless...[^922]

...ye peoples, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^923]

*Troparion 1:* Standing upon the mountain of divine virtues, known unto the ends of the earth, O Nicholas, thou wast manifested by the displays of lofty wonders; therefore every tongue honors thee unto all ages.[^924]

*Troparion 2:* Having tasted of divine sweetness, O Venerable One, thou didst abhor the bitterness of passions and pleasures; wherefrom deliver us, praying Christ to calm the assailing tribulations.[^925]

*Troparion 3:* As an unshakable pillar and the confirmation of the faithful, ever establish me who am shaken by worldly malice and demonic breathings, O all-blessed Nicholas, by thy prayers.[^926]

*Theotokion:*
Heal, I pray thee, O Most Pure One, the passions of my heart, who didst give birth unto the Physician of all; and having entreated Christ, O Virgin, reveal me as a partaker of the portion of the righteous.[^927]

*Then we sing the Song to the Theotokos: "My soul doth magnify the Lord," and prostrations.*[^928]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* The burning bush showed forth unburned a figure of thy pure childbearing; and now we pray thee to quench the furnace of temptations raging against us, that unceasingly, O Theotokos, we may magnify thee.[^929]

*Troparion 1:* As divine and radiant luminaries of the Holy Spirit were ye shown forth, O blessed ones; by the radiance of your honorable and all-wise preaching ye enlightened all the universe, driving away the darkness of idolatry. (Twice)[^930]

*Troparion 2:* Having become branches of the divine spiritual Vine, ye brought forth the divine cluster that gushes forth the salvific wine, O glorious Apostles; therefore deliver me from the drunkenness of pleasures.[^931]

*Troparion 3:* I tremble, wretch that I am, when considering that dread judgment-seat of Thine, O my Christ; for I am now compassed about with shameful and foul deeds, and condemned before the judgment. Therefore have compassion on me by the prayers of Thine Apostles.[^932]

*Theotokion:*
Thou alone didst deify men, having brought forth the incarnate Word; Whom entreat, O Most Pure and All-immaculate Virgin, with the Apostles and Martyrs, for us who in faith call thee blessed and honor thee.[^933]

*Other Heirmos:* The light-bearing cloud, wherein the Master of all descended as rain from heaven upon the fleece, and was incarnate for our sake, becoming man, the Unoriginate: all we magnify as the pure Mother of our God.[^934]

*Troparion 1:* As a hierarch of Christ, as a radiant star, as a wonder-...[^935]

...worker performing wonders by his own hand, as a fountain of healings, as a helper unto those in afflictions, as a most fervent deliverer unto those who call upon thee in tribulations, O father, do we praise thee with sacred songs.[^936]

*Troparion 2:* Thee, the great shepherd and imitator in all things of Christ the Chief Shepherd, do we earnestly entreat, O Nicholas: from the sacred heights shepherd thy servants, and deliver us ever from all worldly misfortunes.[^937]

*Troparion 3:* The end already draws near; why art thou slothful, O my soul! Why dost thou not hasten to live in a manner well-pleasing unto God? Bestir thyself and arise at last, and cry aloud: O Lover of mankind, have compassion on me by the prayers of Nicholas, guiding my life, for Thou art good.[^938]

*Theotokion:*
O thou who didst give birth unto the Divine Light, enlighten me who am darkened by all the things of the evil one, and living in despondency, and provoking God to anger, O All-immaculate One! And guide me unto good deeds, as being the cause of all good things.[^939]

*Then: "It is truly meet," and a prostration, and the Small Litany.*[^940]

*Exapostilarion to the Apostles:*
*Tone 3, Podoben: "Thou Who didst adorn heaven with stars" (Не́бо ѕвѣзда́ми оу҆краси́вый):*
Apostles of the Lord, who by the word drew up all the world under the sun from the depth of deception unto the light: cease not to entreat the Lord for us.[^941]

*Theotokion:*
Refuge of Christians, fail not to entreat thy Son, together with the glorious hierarch, for us thine unworthy servants, that we may obtain everlasting joy.[^942]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera to the Apostles, Tone 1:*
*Sticheron 1:* The all-harmonious apostolic harp, moved by the Holy Spirit, hath abolished the abominable sacrifices of the demons; and having proclaimed the one Lord, it delivered the nations from the deception of idols, and taught them to worship the Consubstantial Trinity.[^943]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord: - - - and look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons [LXX Ps. 89:14, 16].*[^944]

*Sticheron 2:* With one accord let us praise Peter and Paul, Luke, Matthew, Mark, John, Andrew, Thomas, Bartholomew, Simon the Cananite, James, and Philip; and the whole choir of Disciples let us worthily praise.[^945]

*Verse: And let the brightness - - - and direct the works of our hands upon us; yea, direct Thou the work of our hands [LXX Ps. 89:17].*[^946]

*For the Martyrs:*
Rejoice in the Lord, ye Martyrs, for ye fought the good fight; ye resisted kings and overcame tyrants; ye feared not fire nor sword, nor wild beasts devouring your bodies; sending up hymnody unto Christ with the Angels, ye received crowns from heaven. Ask that peace be granted unto the world, and great mercy unto our souls.[^947]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "Joy of the heavenly ranks" (Нбⷭ҇ныхъ чинѡ́въ):*
Prophetically let us praise the Virgin: the golden urn of manna, the unburnt bush, the table, the throne, the golden lampstand holding the lamp, the uncut mountain, the ark of sanctification, and the gate of God.[^948]

*On Thursday, The Beatitudes, Tone 1:*
*Troparion 1:* Through food the enemy led Adam out of Paradise, but by the Cross Christ led the thief into it, who cried: Remember me, O Lord, when Thou comest into Thy Kingdom![^949]

*Troparion 2:* Having enlightened the ends of the earth with the divine beams of your teachings, ye destroyed the darkening of grievous godlessness; and having passed over unto the unwaning Light, ye are ever called blessed.[^950]

*Troparion 3:* Having the Father's hypostatic Wisdom making you all wise, O disciples of Christ, by the foolishness of preaching ye made the world wise, and brought it unto divine understanding.[^951]

*For the Martyrs:*
*Troparion 4:* Having endured torments as though bodiless, O sufferers of Christ, ye mightily conquered all the bodiless enemies; therefore ye are worthily called blessed unto the ages, O all-praised ones.[^952]

*Glory...*
*Triadikon:* Let us worship the Trinity, the faithful: the Son and the upright Spirit together with the Father, an indivisible, inseparable, and co-enthroned Unity, crying: Holy, Holy, Holy art Thou, O all-honorable Trinity![^953]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* We call thee blessed, O Most Pure One, even as thou didst foretell; for thou didst bear God in the flesh, Whom the choir of the Apostles proclaimed; with whom ask for us remission of transgressions, O All-hymned One.[^954]

## Friday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross[^955]

*On Thursday Evening.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Honorable and Life-Giving Cross:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "O all-lauded martyrs" (Прехва́льнїи мꙋ́ченици):*

*Sticheron 1:* Upon the Cross Thou didst stretch out Thy most pure hands, O Christ, calling those who were far from Thee, and establishing them near Thee. Therefore I pray Thee: Gather me who have been taken captive by passions, and grant me repentance, cleansing every passion-wrought defilement.[^956]

*Sticheron 2:* Having lifted up Thy most pure palms upon the tree, O Christ, Thou didst stain Thy fingers with blood, desiring to deliver the work of Thy divine hands, O Lover of mankind, Adam held by transgression in the realms of death, whom Thou didst raise up by Thy power, O Almighty One.[^957]

*Sticheron 3:* Thou didst endure suffering for our sake, O Savior, Who art unchangeable in nature and impassible in Godhead; crucified with malefactors, O Unoriginate, sinless Christ! And the sun, not enduring the audacity, was darkened, and all the earth was shaken, recognizing Thee as the Creator of the world.[^958]

*Other Stichera, Stavrotheotokia:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "O all-lauded martyrs" (Гла́съ и҆ подо́бенъ то́йже):*

*Sticheron 1:* Beholding the undefiled Virgin once upon the tree Him Whom she seedlessly bare from her womb, unable to endure the wounding of her inward parts, tearing her hair she said: O Thou Who by a nod upholdest all creation, how art Thou lifted up upon the Cross as one condemned, desiring in every way to save mankind?[^959]

*Sticheron 2:* O ineffable Offspring of the pre-unoriginate Father! the Most Pure One exclaimed, looking upon the Cross: My Child, how do I not understand what things these ungrateful people have rendered unto Thee? But in that Thou art come to save Thy creation, Thou endurest all things with longsuffering, O Tender-hearted One.[^960]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
Having tasted the ancient bitter food of Adam, Thou didst taste gall and vinegar, and didst sweeten it, O sweetest Child, lifted up upon the tree. Therefore sweeten me also as a righteous Judge, by Thy healing Passion, O Master, and rise again as almighty, the Virgin said weeping.[^961]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 1:*
*Sticheron 1:* The Cross was planted upon Calvary, and immortality blossomed forth for us...[^962]

...from the ever-flowing fountain, the side of the Savior.[^963]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes: - - - Even so our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy upon us [LXX Ps. 122:1-2].*[^964]

*Sticheron 2:* An unbroken wall for us is the precious Cross of the Savior; for hoping thereon, we all are saved.[^965]

*Verse: Have mercy upon us, O Lord, have mercy upon us: - - - Our soul hath been exceedingly filled; let reproach be to them that are at ease, and contempt to the proud [LXX Ps. 122:3-4].*[^966]

*For the Martyrs:*
By the prayers of all the Saints, O Lord, and of the Theotokos, grant us Thy peace, and have mercy upon us, as Thou alone art compassionate.[^967]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "O all-lauded martyrs" (Прехва́льнїи мꙋ́ченицы):*
Beholding Thy wrongful slaughter, O Christ, the Virgin, weeping, cried aloud unto Thee: O sweetest Child! How dost Thou suffer without justice? And how dost Thou hang upon the tree, Who suspended all the earth upon the waters? Leave me not alone, O plenteously merciful Benefactor, Thy Mother and servant, I pray Thee.[^968]

*On Thursday, At Compline, Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 1. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Delivered from bitter bondage, Israel passed through the impassable as dry land; beholding the enemy drowned, it sang a hymn unto God as Benefactor, Who worked wonders with a high arm; for He hath been glorified.[^969]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* I am held fast in despair, considering the multitude of my transgressions and the Judge's answer, O Sovereign Lady Theotokos! But be thou unto me a divine advocate, appeasing Him by thy compassion.[^970]

*Troparion 2:* Refuge of Christians, restoration of the fallen, O Most Pure One, cleansing of those who sin! Deliver me from the dread judgment and unquenchable fire, granting me everlasting life.[^971]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Thee alone, O Virgin, have all we the faithful acquired as a firm protector; for thou didst bear God. Therefore, with praises, O Pure One, do all generations of the earth call thee blessed, according to thy word.[^972]

*Both now...*
*Stavrotheotokion:* When the All-immaculate, Pure One beheld upon the Cross her Creator and Son, filled with dread she said: What is this, Child, that the wicked have rendered unto Thee evil for the good things which Thou didst show them?[^973]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Unto the Son, Who was incorruptibly begotten of the Father before the ages, and in the latter times of...[^974]

...the Virgin, seedlessly incarnate, let us cry aloud unto Christ God: O Thou Who hast exalted our horn, holy art Thou, O Lord![^975]

*Troparion 1:* All the prophets proclaimed thee the Mother of God through most glorious figures; and we, clearly seeing the fulfillment, believe, and pray that through thee we may attain divine tranquility.[^976]

*Troparion 2:* O Sovereign Lady of the world, salvation of the faithful and protection! Having received the sighs which are poured forth from the depth of my heart, O Pure One, and the streams of my tears, deliver and save me who am bound by many transgressions.[^977]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Having boldness as a Mother unto Christ God, O Pure One, pray unceasingly that we may be delivered from the children of the Hagarenes and from all harm; and establish us, O All-immaculate One, to glorify Him thankfully.[^978]

*Both now...*
*Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding her Son lifted up upon the Cross, the Most Pure One cried out, and in heartache she exclaimed, weeping: O Son, what have the impious and lawless done unto Thee, who were filled with Thy gifts?[^979]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* A rod from the root of Jesse, and a blossom therefrom, O Christ, Thou hast budded forth from the Virgin; from the mountain praised and overshadowed with the thick woods, Thou art come, having taken flesh from her that knew not wedlock, O Immaterial One and God! Glory to Thy power, O Lord.[^980]

*Troparion 1:* Cast down the cruelty of the wicked foes and the snares brought against me, and clothe me with thy strength, O All-immaculate One, preserving me ever unharmed, whole and unshaken, who brightly sing praises unto thee.[^981]

*Troparion 2:* Overcome by the law of the flesh, I, all-passionate, commit unseemly iniquities, and in no way dare to lift up mine eyes unto thee, O pure Sovereign Lady; but do thou, by the law of thy tender-heartedness, save me, wretched one, save me.[^982]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Thee alone next to God do we the faithful have as an unashamed hope and protection, O Sovereign Lady! By thy prayers deliver us from all enemies, visible and invisible, from harm and temptations, that we may unceasingly glorify thee.[^983]

*Both now...*
*Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding her Son Whom she bare without seed nailed upon the Cross, the All-immaculate One cried out as a Mother, and weeping, said: What is this new and most glorious, incomprehensible wonder which I behold in Thee, O my Son?[^984]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Being the God of peace and Father of compassions, Thou didst send unto us the Angel of Thy great counsel, granting peace; therefore, guided to the light of the knowledge of God, watching early from the night, we glorify Thee, O Lover of mankind.[^985]

*Troparion 1:* Having watchful prayer and fervent intercession for thy servants, anticipating us in all the assaults and tribulations of life, O Virgin, rescue and save us, lest we be swallowed up by sorrow and perish.[^986]

*Troparion 2:* O Mary, dwelling-place of God! Make me, who have become a dwelling-place of evil spirits by foul deeds and who foolishly fulfill their will, a dwelling-place of God through repentance.[^987]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* We who are grievously sick both in soul and in body with sinful passions, heal, as wise Mother of God; for thou hast truly given birth to Christ, the great Physician of souls and bodies, the inexhaustible fountain of life.[^988]

*Both now...*
*Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding her Son upon the wood of the Cross, her heart was torn, and with tears the All-immaculate One cried aloud: I am filled with awe at Thy longsuffering, O my Son, beholding a new wonder, how, being sinless, Thou endurest an unjust death.[^989]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* The sea-monster vomited Jonah like an infant from its womb, just as it had received him; and the Word, having dwelt in the Virgin and taken flesh, came forth, preserving her incorrupt; for He suffered no corruption, and kept her that bare Him unharmed.[^990]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast a vessel of the noetic myrrh which made all the earth fragrant with the good scents of the Godhead, O all-holy Bride of God! Therefore, by the fragrance of thy prayer consume from my soul all the stench of my transgressions.[^991]

*Troparion 2:* The fire of lusts scorches me greatly, and afflicts my humble heart, and unites me unlawfully to unseemly deeds. Hasten to extinguish it, O thou who didst bear the divine fire, my salvation, O Bride of God.[^992]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Deliver us, O all-lauded Virgin, from the assaults of enemies visible and invisible, and preserve us, O Mother of God, who in the Orthodox faith confess thee truly as the Theotokos; for thou ever hast the power, as having given birth to the Creator of all things.[^993]

*Both now...*
*Stavrotheotokion:* Standing by the Cross and beholding her Son hanging in the flesh, the All-immaculate One was inflamed with sorrow in her inward parts, and bedewing herself with tears, cried aloud: Child, truly ineffable toward all mankind is Thy tender-heartedness![^994]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (40). Glory... Both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:*
*Tone 1, Podoben: "While the soldiers were guarding Thy tomb" (Гро́бъ тво́й Спа́се):*
Beholding the Lamb and Shepherd hanging dead upon the tree, the undefiled Ewe-lamb, weeping, uttered, crying out as a Mother: How shall I endure Thine ineffable descent, O my Son, and Thy voluntary passions, O all-good God?[^995]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The children brought up together in piety, disregarding the ungodly command, feared not the threat of fire, but standing in the midst of the flame, sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^996]

*Troparion 1:* I endure not the assault and tumult of the demons; for the flame of carnal pleasures darkens me; but despise me not, O holy Theotokos, who have placed my hope in thee.[^997]

*Troparion 2:* O unwedded Virgin, all-holy Bride of God, Sovereign Lady! Loosen the bonds of my transgressions by thy prayers, and unite me to the bond of the love of Christ, that I may bear virtuous fruits.[^998]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Unto all who are in the world art thou an intercessor and a wall unto Christians, and an unashamed refuge, pure Mary! Therefore, honoring thee with faith, we cry aloud unto Christ: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^999]

*Both now...*
*Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding her Son hanging upon the Cross, the Most Holy Virgin marvelled with dread, and said: How shall I endure seeing Thee slain, Who art the Author of life and the Giver of life?[^1000]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The dew-bearing furnace showed forth the image of a supernatural wonder of old; for the fire consumed not the young children, displaying the divine seedless birth of Christ from the Virgin. Therefore, chanting let us sing: Let all creation bless the Lord, and exalt Him supreme unto all ages.[^1001]
